### [1] [TabletCraft: Bridging a 4,000-Year Cultural Gap with Bidirectional Akkadian NMT and Cuneiform Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02609)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TabletCraft是首个支持美索不达米亚楔形文字双向交互的开源系统，允许用户阅读古代泥板（阿卡德语→英语）和创作楔形文字泥板（英语→阿卡德语→楔形文字→渲染泥板），集成了基于116K双向样本训练的ByT5翻译模型、包含14,240个映射（95.3%覆盖率）的楔形文字符号转换器以及可视化泥板渲染器。在Akkademia验证集上，阿卡德语到英语的BLEU为49.1，英语到阿卡德语为48.5，这在反向翻译方向上属于首次公开的量化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存约50万块楔形文字泥板，但现代用户既不能读也不能写这一世界上最古老的文字系统，形成了4000年的文化鸿沟。现有NLP工具仅支持从阿卡德语到英语的单向、面向学者的翻译，无法让非专业用户反向创作楔形文字内容，使其始终是被动的文化消费者而非主动参与者。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：1) 基于ByT5的翻译模型，在116K条双向平行样本上训练；2) 楔形文字符号转换器，包含14,240个音素到楔形符号的映射，覆盖率95.3%；3) 可视化泥板渲染器。整个系统打包为可通过pip安装的工具包，提供命令行接口和Web演示，支持从英语到阿卡德语再到楔形文字再到渲染泥板的完整创作流程。

Result: 在Akkademia保留验证集（2,812条样本）上，阿卡德语到英语的BLEU分数为49.1，英语到阿卡德语的BLEU分数为48.5。这是英语到阿卡德语反向翻译方向首次公布的量化评估结果，两个方向的性能表现相当。

Conclusion: TabletCraft填补了古代文字双向交互的空白，使非专业用户能够从被动阅读者转变为主动创作者。两个翻译方向的BLEU分数接近，表明该系统的双向能力均衡且有效，为文化遗产的数字互动提供了新的开源途径。

Abstract: Half a million cuneiform clay tablets survive in museums worldwide, yet modern users can neither read nor write in the world's oldest writing system, leaving a 4,000-year cultural barrier that existing NLP tools have only partially addressed.
  Prior work enables one-way, scholar-oriented translation from Akkadian to English, but offers no path in the reverse direction: non-specialist users cannot compose new content in cuneiform, and therefore remain passive consumers of ancient culture rather than active participants.
  We present TabletCraft, the first open-source system that enables bidirectional interaction with Mesopotamian writing. Users can read ancient tablets (Akkadian to English) and compose new messages as cuneiform clay tablets (English to Akkadian to cuneiform to rendered tablet).
  The system integrates a ByT5-based translation model trained on 116K bidirectional samples, a cuneiform sign converter with 14,240 mappings (95.3% coverage), and a visual tablet renderer, packaged as a pip-installable toolkit with CLI and web demo.
  On the held-out Akkademia validation split (2,812 samples), we report 49.1 BLEU for Akkadian-to-English and 48.5 BLEU for English-to-Akkadian, the first published quantitative result in the reverse direction.

</details>


### [2] [BBOWP-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Black-Box Optimization Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02612)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kei Hiroshima,Nozomu Yoshinari,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出黑盒优化文字问题（BBOWP）这一新问题设定，要求系统从自然语言描述中同时推断搜索空间和优化算法。作者构建了BBOWP-Bench基准套件（包含数据集和评估框架），首次评估了LLM在该任务上的表现，发现LLM能够根据预算选择合适算法，但在搜索空间设计（尤其是不充分描述或高度特定问题中识别关键变量和平衡范围）方面仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题的公式化对最终解质量有重大影响，但好的公式化通常需要大量专业知识。现有研究尝试从自然语言自动推导优化问题，但已有基准仅关注可显式写成数学表达式的场景。许多实际问题本质上是黑盒优化问题（只有目标值可观测，函数形式未知），搜索空间设计和算法选择至关重要，用LLM自动化这些过程是重大挑战。

Method: （1）定义BBOWP问题设定：系统需从自然语言描述黑盒优化任务中推断搜索空间和优化算法；（2）构建BBOWP-Bench基准套件，每个实例包含自然语言问题描述、可执行的评估环境和人工设计的基线公式化，支持对搜索空间设计和算法选择两方面进行评估；（3）使用该基准对LLM进行首次评估。

Result: 当前LLM能根据给定的评估预算选择合适算法，但在搜索空间设计上存在不足，特别是在问题描述信息量不足或搜索空间高度依赖特定问题时，LLM在识别重要变量和平衡变量范围方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: BBOWP是自动化优化问题公式化的新方向，LLM在算法选择上表现良好但搜索空间设计仍有提升空间，需要进一步研究。代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Formulating an optimization problem strongly affects the quality of the final solution, yet good formulations usually require substantial expertise. Recent studies have therefore examined how to automatically derive optimization problems from natural-language descriptions, but existing benchmarks focus on settings where objectives and constraints can be written explicitly as mathematical expressions. Many practically important problems are naturally treated as black-box optimization (BBO) problems, in which only objective values are observable, and the functional form is unavailable. In BBO, the search space design, a part of the problem formulation, and the selection of the optimization algorithm are crucial for problem-solving. Automating these processes with large language models (LLMs) is a significant challenge. This paper introduces Black-Box Optimization Word Problems (BBOWP), a novel problem setting in which a system must infer both a search space and an optimization algorithm from a natural-language description of a black-box optimization task. To support research on this setting, we establish the BBOWP Benchmark Suite (BBOWP-Bench), a dataset and evaluation framework for BBOWP. Each instance combines a natural-language problem description, an executable evaluation environment, and a human-designed baseline formulation, allowing evaluation of both search-space design and algorithm selection. Using this benchmark, we provide the first evaluation of LLMs and show that current LLMs are capable of selecting suitable algorithms based on the given evaluation budget. However, they sometimes struggle with search space design, particularly in identifying important variables and balancing their ranges when the problem description is less informative or the search space is highly problem-specific. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/shiralab/bbowp-bench.

</details>


### [3] [MemArena: An Ego-Centric Benchmark for On-Device Agentic Personal Memory Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02613)
*Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemArena是一个基于MASim模拟器构建的单世界会话基准，模拟50个代理在15天的活动密集的自我中心式多会话交互（1030万对话文本令牌），并协同生成六维度的真实标签（回忆、推理、可信度）。评估五种开放权重读取器与多种记忆后端，发现：记忆后端选择对内容准确性影响大于读取器规模；基于权限的访问在所有后端上普遍失败（Oracle过度泄露，其他后端过度保守）；搜索延迟在大多数组合中仅占TTFT的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署的个人记忆助手需要在设备端使用开放权重模型处理私密人际对话，但现有记忆基准未能充分测试活动密集交互、自我中心视角和连贯多会话世界这三者的组合，MemArena旨在填补这一空白。

Method: MemArena使用MASim代理模拟器构建单一世界的对话基准，模拟50个代理15天的交互（1030万对话文本令牌，每个代理每天24100个纯文本自我中心观察令牌）。基于交互历史协同生成六种评估维度（回忆、推理、可信度）的真实标签，并评估五种开放权重读取器配合Vanilla上下文、BM25-RAG、Oracle检索、Memobase和MemSearch记忆后端。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 记忆后端选择对内容准确性的影响大于读取器规模扩展（在Qwen3-0.6B上后端切换获得+32.5/+19.2pp增益，超过读取器规模扩展的+10.6/+6.8pp）；(2) 权限感知访问在所有后端普遍失败，Oracle严重泄露而其他后端过于保守不敢披露；(3) 搜索延迟仅在极小读取器上显著，在Spark GB10边缘节点上记忆搜索增加固定的87/7/48ms（BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch），在大多数读取器-后端组合中仅占TTFT的小部分。

Conclusion: MemArena的评估表明记忆后端的选择是边缘个人记忆助手中的关键性能因素，权限感知访问机制仍需根本性改进，而搜索延迟在边缘推理中通常不是主要瓶颈。代码、MASim模拟器和MemArena-L基准将在论文被接受后发布。

Abstract: Edge-deployed personal memory assistants must handle private interpersonal conversations on-device with open-weight models. Yet, existing memory benchmarks often under-test the combination of activity-dense interaction, ego-centric perspective, and coherent multi-session worlds. MemArena fills these gaps with a single-world conversational benchmark built with its MASim agent simulator, for 50 agents over 15 days (10.3M dialog-text tokens, 24.1K text-only ego-observed tokens/agent/day). With the interaction history, it co-generates ground truth over six recall, reasoning, and trustworthiness evaluation dimensions. We evaluate five open-weight readers with Vanilla context, BM25-RAG, Oracle retrieval, Memobase, and MemSearch as memory backends. Three results stand out: (1) Memory-backend choice matters more for content accuracy: At Qwen3-0.6B, Memobase-to-MemSearch gains +32.5/+19.2 pp, exceeding MemSearch reader scaling (+10.6/+6.8 pp). (2) Permission-aware access fails universally, with Oracle leaking heavily and other backends too timid to disclose. (3) Search latency bites only at very small reader: on a Spark GB10 edge node, memory-search adds a moderate and fixed 87/7/48 ms (BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch) that composes a small part of TTFT for most reader-backend combinations. Code, the MASim simulator, and the MemArena-L benchmark will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [4] [OncoTriad-QA: A Patient-Level Radiology-Pathology-Genomics Benchmark for Pan-Cancer Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02615)
*Ahnaf Munir,Dannong Wang,Michael W. McDonald,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OncoTriad-QA，一个患者级别的泛癌症问答基准，涵盖放射学、病理学、基因组学和临床元数据四种模态，包含8.6万+语义问题、9281例TCGA患者案例、32个癌症队列。同时提出OncoVLM多模态模型，通过投影器将各模态证据映射到LLM接口，微调后平均领先MedGemma-4B达10.7分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学LLM/VLM基准多聚焦单一模态或狭窄的图像-文本任务，缺乏患者级别的多证据流整合评估，无法检验模型在综合癌症诊疗（放射学+病理学+基因组学+临床元数据）中的能力。这限制了模型在真实临床场景中的应用评估。

Method: ①构建OncoTriad-QA基准：基于TCGA 9281例患者、32个癌症队列，对齐CT/MRI、全切片病理、体细胞突变、拷贝数变异、DNA甲基化、bulk RNA-seq及临床元数据，通过源grounded的LLM辅助管线生成86.1k语义问题，并包含自动化一致性检查与临床医生审核。②提出OncoVLM参考模型：将模态原生的放射学、病理学、DNA甲基化和RNA-seq证据通过学习的投影器映射到LLM接口。

Result: 实验表明通用及医学LLM在全面泛癌症QA上能力有限，尤其在需要整合影像、肿瘤形态和分子证据的问题上。OncoVLM在OncoTriad-QA上微调后，在MCQ准确率和BERTScore-F1上平均超过MedGemma-4B 10.7分，在放射学-only、病理学-only和全部可用设置下的选择题和开放题上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: OncoTriad-QA作为首个患者级别的放射-病理-基因组学综合癌症问答基准，对训练和评估集成癌症问答模型具有重要价值；OncoVLM证明了该基准的有效性，为进一步推动多模态医学AI的临床级评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis and characterization require integrating complementary evidence from radiology, pathology, genomics, and clinical metadata. However, most medical large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks focus on isolated modalities or narrow image-text tasks, leaving patient-level oncology assessment across multiple evidence streams largely untested. We introduce OncoTriad-QA, a patient-level radiology-pathology-genomics benchmark for pan-cancer question answering. OncoTriad-QA contains 86.1k semantic questions across 9,281 TCGA patient cases from 32 cancer cohorts, aligning CT/MRI radiology, whole-slide histopathology, somatic mutations, copy-number alterations, DNA methylation, bulk RNA-seq, and clinical metadata. Case-specific annotations are constructed through a source-grounded LLM-assisted pipeline using curated labels, diagnostic reports, molecular profiles, and modality-derived evidence as primary sources of truth, with automated consistency checks and clinician review. We also introduce OncoVLM, a reference multimodal model that maps modality-native radiology, pathology, DNA methylation, and RNA-seq evidence into an LLM interface through learned projectors. Experiments show that existing general-purpose and medical LLMs remain limited on comprehensive pan-cancer QA, especially when questions require integrating imaging findings, tumor morphology, and molecular evidence. After fine-tuning on OncoTriad-QA, OncoVLM exceeds MedGemma-4B by an average of 10.7 points when using MCQ accuracy and BERTScore-F1, with consistent gains across multiple-choice and open-ended questions under radiology-only, pathology-only, and all-available settings. These results demonstrate the benchmark's value for training and evaluating models for integrated cancer question answering.

</details>


### [5] [Evaluating OpenAI's Privacy Filter: Cross-Lingual, Cross-Domain PII Detection Across 42 Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02616)
*Rohith Uppala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对OpenAI隐私过滤器(OPF)进行了独立、系统的评估，在涵盖22种语言和5个领域的42个综合基准上进行测试。零样本下OPF在部分PII基准上优于Baseline，但对非拉丁字母文本和叙事散文中的PII检测性能严重退化，且在不同领域间存在显著的精度-召回率失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI的Privacy Filter是一个1.5B参数的PII检测器，但此前缺乏独立、系统的第三方评估。需要对其在多种语言、多个领域和多类基准上的真实性能进行全面评测，以了解其优势和局限。

Method: 在42个综合合成基准上评估OPF，覆盖22种语言和5个领域（医疗、法律、金融、客户支持、结构化合成PII）。对比Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa等基线模型，并进行零样本评估和错误分析，按PII类型、文本类型、脚本类型和领域维度分解性能。

Result: OPF在部分PII标注基准上超越基线（AI4Privacy F1=0.855，SPY医疗0.464），但在多语言NER上，XLM-RoBERTa在全部13种Indic和非拉丁语言上领先OPF；GPT-4o在医疗、法律和金融PII上领先，OPF在结构化合成PII和客户支持上领先。OPF在叙事散文中的PII检测严重退化（F1=0.04-0.57），非拉丁脚本崩溃（阿拉伯语0.04，西里尔文0.03）。OPF对结构规则PII（邮件0.78，电话0.76）最强，对文化变异PII（人0.40，地址0.49）最弱，且存在召回偏置。

Conclusion: OPF在结构化、拉丁文本的PII检测上有一定优势，但对非拉丁脚本和叙事文本中的PII检测存在严重缺陷，且精度在各领域间波动大（0.31-0.86），在实际部署中需谨慎考虑其适用场景。

Abstract: We present the first independent, systematic evaluation of OpenAI's Privacy Filter (OPF), a 1.5B-parameter bidirectional PII detector, across 42 synthetic benchmarks spanning 22 languages and 5 domains. Zero-shot, OPF achieves F1=0.855 on AI4Privacy and 0.464 on SPY medical, outperforming Presidio (0.431, 0.273) and XLM-RoBERTa (0.269, 0.111) on PII-annotated benchmarks; on multilingual NER, XLM-RoBERTa leads OPF on all 13 Indic and non-Latin languages. GPT-4o leads on medical, legal, and financial PII (SPY: 0.643 avg, Gretel: 0.527), while OPF leads on structured synthetic PII (0.71 avg) and customer support (0.60). OPF degrades sharply when PII is embedded in narrative prose: F1=0.04--0.57 on NER benchmarks and collapse for non-Latin scripts (Arabic: 0.04, Cyrillic: 0.03). Error analysis shows OPF is strongest on structurally regular PII types (email: 0.78, phone: 0.76) and weakest on culturally variable ones (person: 0.40, address: 0.49), and is recall-biased on customer-support and medical/legal PII (P=0.31--0.54, R=0.70--0.85); global precision spans 0.31--0.86 across all domains.

</details>


### [6] [Preferred, Not Safer: Pairwise Preference Is a Poor Proxy for Clinical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02617)
*Fay Elhassan,David Sasu,Alexandra Kulinkina,Lars Henning Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文基于MOOVE平台收集的26,804条临床医师成对偏好判断（覆盖13个LLM、736+名临床医师、28+国家），证明临床医师的成对偏好不能可靠反映LLM的安全性能——排名靠前的模型仍可能在危害性和准确性维度上出现大量临床失败（≤-1分），且失败在专科间分布不均。论文进一步分析失败成因（提示长度、拒答/升级行为、安全关键特征与表面特征贡献），并提出了结合rubric反馈的临床调整偏好排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估常依赖临床医师的成对偏好作为质量信号，但在安全关键的临床场景中，偏好是否足以反映安全性尚不明确。作者希望通过大规模真实临床专家反馈验证偏好与安全的脱节程度，并探索更安全的评估方法。

Method: 利用MOOVE平台收集的临床医师成对偏好判断（离散[-2,+2]评分量表，负值表示不安全/误导性内容）。通过对比偏好排序与安全关键失败率（≤-1）在各维度（危害性、准确性）的表现，进行特征分解分析（安全关键特征vs表面特征），并引入结合rubric反馈的临床调整偏好排名（在原始Bradley-Terry强度基础上调整）。

Result: 1) 临床医师偏好是安全关键性能的差代理变量，高偏好排名模型仍可在危害性和准确性维度出现显著临床失败；2) 失败率在专科间分布不均，产生聚合排名和单一数字榜单无法揭示的专科特异性"禁止区"；3) 相当比例的偏好投票不含积极安全信号；4) 表面特征解释偏好变异略多于安全关键rubric差异；5) 临床调整的偏好排名比原始Bradley-Terry产生更安全感知的排序。

Conclusion: 评估实践应将偏好与安全分离，直接报告安全关键失败率，并在临床决策用LLM排名时纳入临床验证的调整因子。偏好不应作为安全性的替代指标，需建立独立的临床安全评估体系。

Abstract: We evaluate whether clinician pairwise preferences provide a reliable signal of clinical safety in large language model (LLM) evaluation using expert feedback from MOOVE (Massive Open Online Validation and Evaluation), a clinician-led platform collecting blinded pairwise preferences alongside multi-criterion rubric ratings. Clinicians assign scores on a discrete $[-2, +2]$ scale, where negative values indicate clinically unsafe or misleading content. Using 26{,}804 pairwise judgments across outputs from 13 LLMs, contributed by more than 736 clinicians across 28+ countries, we find that clinician preference is a poor proxy for safety-critical performance. Models ranking highly under pairwise preference can still exhibit substantial rates of clinically meaningful failures ($\leq -1$) on dimensions such as \emph{Harmlessness} and \emph{Accuracy}. These failures are unevenly distributed across specialties, creating domain-specific ``no-go zones'' not visible in aggregate rankings or single-number leaderboards. We further analyze contributing factors including prompt length, refusal and escalation behavior, and the relative contributions of safety-critical versus surface-level features. A substantial fraction of preference votes carry no positive safety signal, while feature decomposition shows that surface-level characteristics explain slightly more preference variation than safety-critical rubric differences. Finally, we introduce a clinically adjusted preference ranking combining pairwise preference with rubric-derived feedback, producing a more safety-aware ordering than raw Bradley--Terry strength alone. Our findings support evaluation practices that separate preference from safety, report safety-critical failure rates directly, and incorporate clinically grounded adjustments when ranking LLMs for clinical decision making.

</details>


### [7] [JudgeArena: A Unified Framework for Reproducible LLM-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02620)
*Erlis Lushtaku,Bora Kargi,Ali Elganzory,Fabio Ferreira,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Julia Kreutzer,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JudgeArena是一个开源框架，统一了主流的LLM-判官评估基准（AlpacaEval、Arena-Hard、MT-Bench和m-Arena-Hard），支持可替换的判官模型和全面的元数据日志记录，并提供调优后的开源判官配置以匹配或超越闭源判官模型，还可高精度模拟LMArena Elo评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估范式虽然已成为排序语言模型的主流方法，但生态系统碎片化严重：各基准各自为政、绑定特定的闭源判官模型、仅支持单一评估协议，导致难以系统性研究基准、判官模型、提示词和推理后端等设计选择对模型质量结论的影响。

Method: JudgeArena提供一个统一接口来集成多个主流LLM判官基准，支持通过vLLM、llama.cpp或OpenRouter接入任意模型作为候选或判官，配备全面的元数据日志记录以提升可复现性。同时内置针对开源模型的调优判官配置，并通过结合现有人类标注与LLM判官评估来模拟LMArena Elo评分。

Result: 调优后的开源判官配置在英语和多语言人工偏好数据集上匹配或超越闭源判官模型的表现；通过结合人类标注与LLM判官评估，能够以高精度模拟LMArena Elo评分，提供一种开放、低成本的替代方案。

Conclusion: JudgeArena通过统一的评估框架和开源判官配置，降低了评估流程对不透明闭源模型的依赖，并以低成本方式复现大规模人工标注的结果，推动了LLM评估的标准化和可复现性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge evaluation has become a dominant paradigm for ranking language models, yet the ecosystem remains fragmented: most benchmarks ship their own code base, hardcode a specific closed-model judge, and support a single evaluation protocol. This fragmentation makes it difficult to study how design choices--the benchmark, the judge model, the prompt, the inference backend--affect the conclusions we draw about model quality. We introduce JudgeArena, an open-source framework that unifies major LLM-judge benchmarks (AlpacaEval, Arena-Hard, MT-Bench, and m-Arena-Hard) under a single interface with swappable judges and comprehensive metadata logging for increased transparency in reporting and reproducibility. It enables systematic studies of judge choices, as any model accessible via vLLM, llama.cpp, or OpenRouter can serve as both candidate and judge. Furthermore, JudgeArena ships with tuned judge configurations for open models that match or outperform closed-model judges, validated on human preference datasets in both English and multilingual settings, reducing the reliance on opaque closed models. Finally, by combining existing human annotations with LLM-judge evaluations of a target model, JudgeArena can simulate LMArena Elo scores with high accuracy offering a practical, open, and low-cost alternative to large-scale human annotation campaigns.

</details>


### [8] [Knowing the Form, Not the Function: Automatically Auditing Answer--Authority Decoupling in Legal Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02621)
*Hsien-Jyh Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现法律基准测试中仅依据最终答案评分无法反映模型是否正确引用法条依据。四款LLM在238道台湾律师资格考试题目上，答案正确性与法条依据正确性可在两个方向上分离：大量答案正确但未命中黄金法条，同时也有答案错误但引用了正确法条的情形。作者提出"答案—法条依据联合评估"方案，并初步验证了其在中国大陆民法领域的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准通常只对最终答案打分，即使模型给出了法条依据也不考核其正确性。本文质疑"答案正确"能否作为"法条依据是否正确"的代理指标，因为若答案正确但引用了错误法条（或未引用），可能掩盖模型在法条适用上的系统缺陷。作者希望证明两者可分离，从而论证联合评估的必要性。

Method: 利用台湾律师资格考试238道题目（每题均有权威法条），在未提示引用法条的普通推理提示下，让四款LLM作答。自动联合审计答案正确性与法条依据正确性，分析两者分离的比率。另设两个补充实验：独立的法条检索探测实验，以及一个允许但不强制引用法条的干预实验，以验证答案与引用行为在输出层面的可分离性。最后在中国的PRC民法题目上进行初步扩展测试。

Result: 刑法题目中，24.0%—42.4%的有效回答为答案正确但未命中黄金法条，15.2%—21.7%为答案错误但引用了正确法条。法条检索探测与引用豁免干预实验进一步表明答案与引用行为可独立变动。PRC民法扩展实验同样观察到未要求引用时模型自发产生法条标注的现象。

Conclusion: 答案正确性与法条依据正确性在法律基准中可双向分离，答案单一评分会将自然发生的"黄金法条遗漏"视为完全成功。由于法条依据在结构上可提取、外部可验证，这种分离失败可被自动度量。作者因此提议对基于法条的法律基准采用"答案—法条依据"联合评估范式，并呼吁进行跨法域的全面联合审计。

Abstract: Legal benchmarks typically score final answers even when models also state legal authority. We test whether answer correctness can serve as a proxy for authority grounding. Under ordinary reasoning prompts that did not request statutory citations, four LLMs spontaneously produced authority markers across 238 Taiwan bar-examination items. Because each item has a verified governing provision, we automatically audit answer correctness and authority grounding jointly. The two dimensions dissociate in both directions. In criminal law, 24.0--42.4\% of valid responses were answer-correct but missed the gold authority, while 15.2--21.7\% were answer-incorrect but cited it. A separate statutory-retrieval probe and a permissive citation-abstention intervention further show that answer and citation behavior can move separately at the output level. Because this mismatch arises without adversarial or inconsistency-inducing prompting, answer-only scoring treats naturally occurring gold-authority misses as complete benchmark successes. Because statutory authority is structurally extractable and externally verifiable, the failure can be measured automatically. A preliminary PRC civil-law extension also observes citation-unrequested authority marking, motivating a full cross-jurisdictional joint audit. We therefore propose joint answer--authority evaluation for statute-grounded legal benchmarks.

</details>


### [9] [Speculative Correction: Draft-then-Refine Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02625)
*Brian K Chen,Chong Wu,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种即插即用的推理模式：先用扩散语言模型（DLM）生成完整草稿，再通过双向扩散对整个响应进行全局精炼（draft-and-refine）。在LLaDA2.1-Flash和Mini模型上验证了两种配置：同模型（Flash-Flash）自改进和异构级联（Mini-Flash）的投机性修正。Flash-Flash在GSM8K-384上准确率从0.848提升至0.899且速度提升1.2倍，MBPP-384从0.545提升至0.693；Mini-Flash在MATH-384上以2.17倍加速获得接近Flash质量的结果。实验支持Pareto前沿解释，表明双向精炼是DLM的有效解码原语，投机性修正提供了无需训练的快速生成路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型支持双向token修正，但标准解码过程常将其强行适配为从左到右的块级生成，未能充分利用双向精炼这一核心能力。作者希望探索一个简单即用的推理模式——先完成完整草稿再全局细化，以挖掘DLM已有的双向修正潜力。

Method: 提出draft-and-refine推理模式：第一步用DLM生成完整草稿，第二步利用双向扩散对整段响应进行全局精炼。评估两种配置：Flash-Flash（同一Flash模型同时作为草稿生成器和精炼器）和Mini-Flash（小模型Mini生成草稿，大模型Flash以可编辑初始化的方式进行精炼，即投机性修正speculative correction）。通过因果消融实验验证完整草稿初始化的价值。

Result: Flash-Flash将GSM8K-384准确率从0.848提升至0.899，运行速度比所选Flash块自回归基线快1.20倍；MBPP-384从0.545提升至0.693。延迟窗口匹配的对照组表明收益在调优后依然存在。Mini-Flash在MATH-384上达到0.294（Flash为0.300），速度提升2.17倍。因果消融显示全掩码精炼效果差，全局精炼在GSM8K上有明显增益。

Conclusion: 同模型draft-and-refine证明了双向精炼是DLM的有用解码原语；投机性修正提供了无需训练的快速DLM生成方式。异构级联并非在所有场景下都匹配Flash质量，更符合Pareto前沿的解读而非统一改进。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) can revise tokens bidirectionally, but standard decoding procedures often adapt them to left-to-right generation by producing text block by block. We study a simple plug-and-play inference pattern: first generate a complete draft, then refine the full response using bidirectional diffusion. Using LLaDA2.1-Flash and LLaDA2.1-Mini, we evaluate two configurations. In Flash-Flash, the same Flash model serves as both drafter and refiner, testing whether an existing model can improve its own block-autoregressive output through global refinement. In Mini-Flash, inspired by speculative decoding, we introduce speculative correction: Mini drafts a full response, and Flash revises it as an editable initialization. Flash-Flash improves GSM8K-384 accuracy from 0.848 to 0.899 while running 1.20 times faster than the selected Flash block-autoregressive baseline, and improves MBPP-384 from 0.545 to 0.693. Latency-window-matched Flash-only controls indicate that these gains persist after targeted tuning of block-autoregressive decoding. Causal ablations indicate that completed drafts provide useful initializations: refinement from a fully masked span performs poorly, full global refinement provides a clear additional gain on GSM8K, and local refinement captures much of the gain on MBPP and MATH. Mini-Flash provides useful quality-latency trade-offs, including MATH-384 performance of 0.294 versus 0.300 for Flash while running 2.17 times faster. These results support a Pareto-frontier interpretation rather than the claim that the heterogeneous cascade uniformly matches Flash quality. Overall, same-model draft-and-refine provides evidence that bidirectional refinement is a useful decoding primitive for DLMs, while speculative correction demonstrates a training-free route to fast DLM generation.

</details>


### [10] [Stuck on "A": Diagnosing and Repairing Interface Injury in Attention-to-KDA Linearization of a 0.6B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02689)
*Ronglong Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将Qwen3-0.6B-Base的28个全注意力层中的21层转换为KDA线性注意力层（在单张消费级GPU预算下），通过隐藏状态对齐和端到端KL蒸馏使困惑度接近教师模型，但多选题准确率仍接近随机（C-Eval上25-29% vs 教师50.6%）。通过四置换诊断发现模型固着于选项标签而非内容（81%预测"A"），这是一种标准蒸馏指标无法发现的"接口损伤"。1,000步的格式定向补全KL阶段修复了该损伤（C-Eval提升+12.48，标签固着减半），随后persona SFT和一轮on-policy DPO在噪声范围内保持基准分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想探究将全注意力层转换为线性注意力层后，模型究竟"损坏"了什么。尽管现有蒸馏指标（如困惑度、KL散度）显示学生模型与教师接近，但在多项选择任务上表现极差，说明存在隐藏的损伤未被标准指标捕获。

Method: 1) 将Qwen3-0.6B-Base的28层全注意力中的21层转换为KDA线性注意力层；2) 使用隐藏状态对齐和端到端KL蒸馏让学生接近教师；3) 设计四置换诊断法，固定内容旋转选项顺序，检测模型是否固着于标签位置；4) 引入1,000步格式定向的补全-only KL阶段修复接口损伤；5) 使用persona SFT和一轮on-policy DPO微调。

Result: 蒸馏后困惑度接近教师，但C-Eval准确率仅25-29%（教师50.6%）；模型81%时间预测"A"，106/161题在所有四置换下保持相同标签；格式定向KL阶段使C-Eval提升+12.48点，标签固着率减半；此后SFT和DPO在噪声范围内保持分数。此外记录了FP32-master失败模式（bf16优化器更新被静默吞掉）等工程经验。

Conclusion: 标准蒸馏指标（困惑度/KL）无法捕获"接口损伤"——模型学到预测选项标签位置而非内容。通过专门的格式定向KL阶段可以修复该损伤，且修复后基准分数可稳定保持。文章提供完整代码、权重、配方和审计轨迹，并总结了在消费级预算下实现收敛的工程教训。

Abstract: We convert 21 of 28 full-attention layers of Qwen3-0.6B-Base into KDA (Kimi Delta Attention) linear-attention layers on a single consumer-grade GPU budget, and ask a simple question: what exactly does the conversion break? After surgery, hidden-state alignment and end-to-end KL distillation drive the student close to its teacher in perplexity, yet multiple-choice accuracy stays near random chance (25-29% vs. the teacher's 50.6% on C-Eval). Using a four-permutation diagnostic that rotates answer options while holding content fixed, we show the model sticks to option labels (predicting "A" 81% of the time; 106/161 questions keep the same label under all four rotations) rather than following answer content -- an interface injury that standard distillation metrics cannot see. A 1,000-step format-targeted completion-only KL stage repairs the interface (+12.48 points on C-Eval, label-stickiness roughly halved), after which persona SFT and one round of on-policy DPO preserve benchmark scores within noise. We release code, weights, recipes, and the full audit trail, and distill the engineering lessons -- including an FP32-master failure mode in which bf16 optimizer updates are silently swallowed -- that made convergence possible at this budget.

</details>


### [11] [Crayotter: Learning Long-Horizon Video Editing Agents via Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02694)
*Lecheng Yan,Jianze Lin,Yichong Zhang,Ben Pan,Wenxi Li,Chenyang Lyu,Liting Zhou,Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出组相对偏好反向传播（GRPB）方法，将长视野视频编辑任务中主观且延迟的最终反馈转化为同任务内的序数比较，通过零和优势分配和延迟信用传播来实现从主观反馈中学习，并构建了Crayotter 9B模型在AgenticVBench上超越多个专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野视频编辑智能体的学习面临两大挑战：最终产品反馈在大量相互依赖的决策之后才到达，且编辑质量本质上是主观的、允许多种有效解决方案，难以跨异构请求进行有意义的校准。全局标量目标既模糊又缺乏时间信息，导致传统的奖励设计或标量学习目标不适用。

Method: 核心洞察是：固定请求、素材和制作约束后，主观目标可以转化为直接可比方案之间的序数比较。GRPB方法将同任务内的排序转化为零和优势值，并将其作为有界信用分配到语义编辑片段上。通过滞后分配器（lagged allocator）和防护传输（guarded transmission）机制，防止当前判断或不可靠估计直接塑造同一rollout组。作者还手工构建了项目不相交、视野分层的真实编辑任务套件用于训练和受控评估。

Result: 在与匹配基线、信用干预、外部基准测试和盲人机评估的对比中，GRPB在编辑行为和最终渲染产品两方面都有改进。由此得到的9B参数Crayotter模型在AgenticVBench上超越了多个专有系统。

Conclusion: 任务局部偏好约减（task-local preference reduction）是一种实用的从主观延迟结果中学习的方案，为解决长视野任务中主观反馈学习问题提供了新思路。代码和材料已公开。

Abstract: Long-horizon video editing agents receive final-product feedback only after many interdependent decisions. Yet editing quality is subjective, admits multiple valid solutions, and is not meaningfully calibrated across heterogeneous requests, making a global scalar objective both ambiguous and temporally uninformative. Our key observation is that fixing the request, materials, and production constraints converts this subjective objective into an ordinal comparison among directly comparable alternatives. We introduce Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation (GRPB), which transforms same-task rankings into zero-sum advantages and redistributes them as bounded credit over semantic editing segments. A lagged allocator and guarded transmission prevent current judgments or unreliable estimates from directly shaping the same rollout group. We manually construct a project-disjoint, horizon-stratified suite of realistic editing tasks for training and controlled evaluation. Across matched baselines, credit interventions, external benchmarking, and blinded human evaluation, GRPB improves both editing behavior and rendered products. The resulting 9B Crayotter model surpasses several proprietary systems on AgenticVBench, supporting task-local preference reduction as a practical approach to learning from subjective, delayed outcomes. Code and all supporting materials are publicly available at https://github.com/idwts/Crayotter.

</details>


### [12] [ARCHead: Activation-Metric Residual Correction for Large Language Model Output Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02703)
*Şuayp Talha Kocabay,Talha Rüzgar Akkuş,Kamer Ali Yuksel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARCHead 是一种针对 LLM 语言建模头（LM-head）的打包压缩器，通过量化低秩核心、分组 INT4 残差和基于激活度量的低秩修正相结合，在不存储密集 BF16 头的情况下将持久化存储减少 3.7-3.9 倍，同时保持接近原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重量化方法虽然大幅压缩了 transformer 块，但通常保留 LM-head 为 BF16/FP16，导致该部分成为存储瓶颈。朴素量化 LM-head 投影会严重扰动词汇-对数几率分布，因此需要一种专门针对 LM-head 的高效压缩方案。

Method: ARCHead 采用三种组件组合：量化的低秩核心（quantized low-rank core）、分组 INT4 残差（group-wise INT4 residuals）以及基于激活推导度量拟合的低秩修正（low-rank correction fitted in an activation-derived metric）。该方法不存储任何稠密 BF16 头。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上，ARCHead 仅使用 BF16 头存储的 25.6%，达到 1.007 的相对困惑度；存储匹配的朴素 INT4 方案困惑度为 1.14-1.16。替换 AWQ 或 bitsandbytes 遗留的 BF16 头仅增加 0.006-0.007 的交叉熵损失，吞吐量变化小于 2%。

Conclusion: ARCHead 能有效压缩块量化器未处理的 LM-head，作为块级量化方法的补充，在不显著影响性能的前提下大幅减少持久化存储，适合实际后端部署场景。

Abstract: Weight-only quantization substantially reduces the storage of large language model (LLM) transformer blocks, but practical backends often retain the final language-modeling head (LM-head) in BF16 or FP16. Quantizing this projection naively can strongly perturb the vocabulary-logit distribution. We present ARCHead, a packed LM-head compressor that combines a quantized low-rank core, group-wise INT4 residuals, and a low-rank correction fitted in an activation-derived metric. ARCHead stores no dense BF16 head and reduces persistent LM-head storage by 3.7-3.9x. On Qwen3-8B-Base, it uses 25.6% of BF16 head storage while attaining 1.007 relative perplexity; storage-matched naive INT4 yields 1.14-1.16. Replacing the BF16 head left by AWQ or bitsandbytes adds only 0.006-0.007 cross-entropy, with less than 2% throughput change in our measurements. ARCHead therefore complements block quantizers by compressing the large output projection they can leave untouched. Code is available at https://github.com/suayptalha/archead.

</details>


### [13] [Learning a Vector-Symbolic Model for Socio-Cultural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02807)
*Meera Ray,Swapnika Dulam,Christopher L. Dancy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在ACT-R认知架构中使用的声明性记忆系统，通过向量符号自编码器（vector-symbolic autoencoder）在多个语义表征层级上表示社会文化结构对决策的影响，并结合HRR（全息约化表示）操作将情景记忆与从文本中提取的语义记忆向量编码为最终的记忆块激活值，通过在种族情境化的内隐联想测验（IAT）上验证该系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知模型中需要更好地表征社会文化结构对决策的影响，但建模此类影响需要跨越多个语义表征层级，而建模者难以确定在特定情境下哪些层级的表征最为关键。虽然大语言模型和认知语料模型可以通过共现关系表征广泛的语义关联，但自我表征（self representations）在记忆中的作用尚未被充分考虑，因此需要一种能够融合文化关联与自我表征的机制来塑造决策。

Method: 提出一个用于ACT-R认知架构的声明性记忆系统：通过向量符号自编码器（vector-symbolic autoencoder）在多个层级表示语义关联；使用HRR（全息约化表示）操作将情景记忆与从文本中提取的语义记忆向量区分编码，以产生记忆请求的最终块激活值；并在种族情境化的内隐联想测验（IAT）上使用ACT-R认知模型进行测试。

Result: 该声明性记忆系统在种族情境化的内隐联想测验（IAT）上进行了测试，验证了其多层级语义表征及情景-语义混合编码机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结合多层次语义表征与自我表征的记忆编码方式，该工作为在ACT-R认知架构中建模社会文化结构对决策的影响提供了新的计算框架。

Abstract: How can we better represent the impact of sociocultural structures on decision making in computational cognitive models? Modeling this impact requires traversing multiple levels of semantic representation, however it is not immediately clear to a modeler which levels of representation are most salient to a given situation. Though large language models and cognitively grounded corpus models can represent broad semantic associations through co-occurences, the role of self representations in memory should be accounted for to determine how cultural associations shape decision making. We propose a declarative memory system to be used in the ACT-R cognitive architecture that represents semantic associations at multiple levels via a vector-symbolic autoencoder. We use a simple HRR operation to encode episodic memories differently from semantic memory vectors extracted from text to produce a final chunk activation for a memory request. We use ACT-R cognitive models of a racially contextualized implicit association test (IAT) to test this new declarative memory system.

</details>


### [14] [BODHI: Do LLMs Branch Out and Discover Heterogeneous Inferences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02867)
*Soumadeep Saha,Krish Sharma,Akshay Chaturvedi,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迷宫求解实验和BODHI-Trees语义树结构分析，揭示了RLVR训练的LLM虽然提升了约束遵循和回溯能力，但显著压缩了语义分支熵和多样探索空间，说明RLVR主要提升采样效率而非扩展推理能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在推理任务上显著提升了LLM的性能，但其真正的作用机制存在争议——究竟是扩展了推理能力边界，还是仅仅是提升了采样效率？作者希望通过实证方法厘清这一问题。

Method: 采用两种受控实验：(1) 迷宫求解任务以精确控制探索行为；(2) 提出BODHI-Trees方法，基于语义等价性从数学推理轨迹中提取树结构，从而区分文体风格变化带来的熵与真正的推理分支带来的熵。

Result: RLVR模型的策略熵崩塌不仅是句法层面的，还伴随着语义分支熵的显著降低。RLVR改善了环境约束遵循和回溯能力，但压缩了延续空间（continuation space），这种压缩可能是RLVR采样效率提升的原因，但以牺牲真实rollout多样性为代价。

Conclusion: RLVR并未实质性地扩展推理能力边界，而是通过压缩语义探索空间来提升采样效率和约束遵循能力，代价是多样性的损失。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has improved the performance of large language models (LLMs) across a variety of reasoning tasks, there is significant debate as to whether RLVR expands the reasoning capability boundary, or just improves sampling efficiency. In this paper, we investigate the nature of test-time exploration in RLVR-trained LLMs by employing controlled maze-solving experiments and extracting a tree structure from mathematical reasoning traces (BODHI-Trees) based on semantic equivalence. This helps us delineate between entropy arising from stylistic variations and genuine inferential branching. Our findings demonstrate that the policy entropy collapse observed in RLVR models is not merely syntactic, and is accompanied by a significant reduction in semantic branching entropy. While RLVR improves adherence to environmental constraints and backtracking capabilities, it constricts the space of continuations; we provide evidence suggesting that this might be responsible for the sample efficiency gains of RLVR, albeit at the cost of genuine rollout diversity.

</details>


### [15] [FLARE: Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02919)
*Dhanasekar Sundararaman,Bharat Gandhi,Aashna Garg,Minjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE（基于少样本学习的自适应反思引擎），一种利用反思机制和少量参考示例来优化指令的框架，在多个基准任务上显著优于现有最先进的GEPA优化器，同时展现出更高的数据效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复合AI系统中性能高度依赖提示词质量。现有最先进的优化器如GEPA声称反思性指令进化优于传统强化学习和少样本优化。本文旨在挑战这一观点，验证少样本学习在指令优化中的潜力。

Method: FLARE框架结合了先进的反思机制与少量few-shot参考示例来优化指令。在检索增强推理（HotPotQA、MedQA、2WikiMultiHopQA）、工具调用和多标签情感分类（GoEmotions）等多样化基准上，使用GPT-5系列模型进行评估。

Result: FLARE在每个任务-模型对上均优于GEPA：HotPotQA上提升高达+14.2分（52.2 vs 42.2），工具调用达87.0%（GEPA为81.0%），GoEmotions微F1达52.7%（+15.3），是GEPA增益（+5.7）的两倍多。此外，FLARE仅需100个验证样本即可达到峰值性能，且跨随机种子的稳定性显著优于GEPA。

Conclusion: 研究发现尽管反思性指令功能强大，但少样本学习的策略性优化仍然是最大化下一代大语言模型潜能的关键前沿方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in complex, compound AI systems where performance hinges on the quality of prompts. Recent state-of-the-art optimizers like GEPA (Genetic-Pareto) have argued that reflective instruction evolution can outperform traditional reinforcement learning and few-shot optimization. In this work, we challenge this shift by introducing FLARE (Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine), a framework that leverages advanced reflective mechanisms and a small set of few-shot reference examples to optimize instructions. We evaluate our method across a diverse suite of benchmarks -- spanning retrieval-augmented reasoning (HotPotQA, MedQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA), tool calling, and multi-label emotion classification (GoEmotions) -- using the GPT-5 series of models. Our results demonstrate that FLARE consistently outperforms GEPA, winning on every task-model pair: it achieves gains of up to +14.2 points on HotPotQA (52.2 vs. GEPA's 42.2 with GPT-5-Chat), reaches 87.0% on tool calling (vs. 81.0% for GEPA), and lifts GoEmotions micro-F1 to 52.7% (+15.3) with GPT-5.1 on the full 5408-example test split, more than doubling GEPA's +5.7 gain. Beyond raw accuracy, FLARE is also strikingly data-efficient: on GoEmotions it reaches its peak performance using as few as 100 validation examples, while remaining markedly more stable across random seeds than GEPA. Our findings suggest that while reflective instructions are powerful, the strategic optimization of few-shot learning remains a critical frontier for maximizing the potential of next-generation LLMs.

</details>


### [16] [Character Iconicity vs. Arbitrariness: An Arabic NLP Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02935)
*Dorieh Alomari,Irfan Ahmad,Maged S. Al-shaibani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿拉伯语无点化（dotless）文本在NLP任务中的表现，发现任意但一致的字符重映射（arbitrary remappings）即可达到与原始点字或传统无点字相当的性能，表明模型依赖的是稳定的分布结构而非字母形式的视觉象似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明无点阿拉伯语仍可保持可读性和NLP有效性，但尚不清楚这种成功是否依赖于保留原始rasm分组，还是任意但一致的字符重映射也能取得同等性能。

Method: 将标准点字和无点阿拉伯语与受限于19个无点rasm的任意字符重映射进行比较。生成2,000个随机重映射（词级和字符级分词），选取四个代表性映射（最高和最低熵），在语言建模、文本分类、序列标注、机器翻译和脚本还原五个NLP任务上评估。

Result: 随机重映射在各项任务中均达到与原始表示竞争性的性能，同时减少了词汇量、OOV率、模型大小和训练成本。保留原始字符区分或传统rasm分组并非NLP强性能的必要条件。

Conclusion: 从NLP角度看，阿拉伯字符的形式-功能关系在很大程度上是任意的，模型更依赖稳定的分布结构而非字母形式的视觉象似性。

Abstract: Arabic script uses 28 letters, many of which share a common base shape (rasm) and are distinguished only by dot placement. Because early Arabic manuscripts were written without dots yet remained interpretable, dot removal offers a natural test of whether these visual distinctions are functionally necessary. Prior work has shown that dotless Arabic can remain readable and effective for natural language processing (NLP), but it remains unclear whether this success depends on preserving the original rasm groupings or whether arbitrary but consistent remappings to the same reduced rasm set can achieve comparable performance. We address this question by comparing standard dotted and dotless Arabic with arbitrary character remappings constrained to the same 19 undotted rasms. We generated 2,000 random remappings under word- and character-level tokenization and selected four representative mappings with the highest and lowest entropy values. These representations were evaluated across language modeling, text classification, sequence labeling, machine translation, and restoration to the original script. The results show that neither preserving original character distinctions nor retaining traditional rasm-based groupings is necessary for strong NLP performance. Random remappings achieve competitive performance while reducing vocabulary size, out-of-vocabulary (OOV) rates, model size, and training cost. These findings suggest that, from an NLP perspective, Arabic character form-function relationships are largely arbitrary: models rely more on stable distributional structure than on the visual iconicity of letter forms.

</details>


### [17] [Aligned in Form, Not in Meaning: The Comprehension - Containment Decoupling of LLM Safety in Low-Resource Bangla Derogatory Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02941)
*Shadab Bin Habib,A K M Ferdous Reza Habib,Subarno Neel,Adib Sakhawat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对五个前沿大语言模型进行了孟加拉语脏话（gali）的系统性审计，提出并验证了"理解-遏制解耦"假说：当代安全对齐绑定的是高资源语言的表面形式而非有害语义，导致模型对低资源语言的亵渎词理解能力与遏制能力相互独立运行。所有六项协议均一致证实该假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准主要覆盖高资源语言，无法评估低资源语言环境下的模型安全性能。作者推测当代安全对齐可能只针对高资源语言的表面形式（如关键词匹配），而非深层有害语义，因此提出了"理解-遏制解耦"（Comprehension-Containment Decoupling）假说，旨在验证模型理解低资源亵渎词的能力与其遏制有害输出的能力是否彼此独立。

Method: 对五个前沿大语言模型设计了六项协议测试：（1）基线测试，对比孟加拉语与英语的理解与遏制表现，以人工校准基准（kappa=0.84）为对照；（2）严重性校准测试，区分表面解剖学线索与组合性危害；（3）正字法扰动测试，检验分词器层面的"遏制幻象"；（4）显式思维链（Chain-of-Thought）推理测试；（5）专家角色框架测试，考察对去人性化社群脏话的过滤效果；（6）整体跨协议交叉验证。

Result: 六项协议全部支持假说：基线中模型对孟加拉语存在7.92个百分点的理解缺陷，但两种语言的token泄露率完全相同（92.83%）；严重性校准方面，轻度俚语误差+4.00、威胁类-2.00，表明模型依赖表面解剖学线索而非组合性危害；正字法扰动下的"遏制提升"实为分词器驱动的"遏制幻象"；显式思维链将理解提升至94.72%通过率，却同时使遏制失败率高达96.23%；专家角色框架将拒答率降至6.57%，显示基于关键词的过滤器完全忽视了去人性化的社群性脏话。

Conclusion: 高资源语言基准无法为低资源语言安全提供有效认证。模型的安全对齐机制必须从表面关键词匹配转向基于语义（meaning-grounded）的遏制设计，否则在低资源语言场景下，模型既无法可靠理解有害内容，也无法有效遏制有害输出。

Abstract: We audit five frontier large language models on native Bangla derogatory speech (gali) across six protocols to test a single hypothesis: Comprehension-Containment Decoupling. We propose that contemporary safety alignment is bound to high-resource surface forms rather than harmful meaning, causing a model's capacity to comprehend a low-resource slur and its capacity to contain it to operate independently. Every protocol corroborates this hypothesis against a human-calibrated baseline (kappa = 0.84). At baseline, models exhibit a 7.92 percentage point comprehension deficit in Bangla while maintaining an identical 92.83% token leakage rate across both languages. Severity calibration tracks surface anatomical cues over compositional harm (+4.00 error on mild slang; -2.00 on threats), while apparent containment gains under orthographic perturbation prove to be a tokenizer-driven "containment mirage." Crucially, explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning rescues comprehension (94.72% Pass) while systematically dismantling containment (96.23% Use). Furthermore, expert-persona framing collapses refusal to 6.57%, revealing that keyword-based filters ignore dehumanizing communal slurs entirely. Our findings demonstrate that high-resource benchmarks cannot certify low-resource safety, necessitating meaning-grounded containment.

</details>


### [18] [OPTD: On-Policy Transition Distillation with Consistency-Guided Adaptive Compression for Few-Step Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02942)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Hualei Zhang,Shuhan Guo,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyi Pang,Jian Liu,Haoxi Li,Bohai Gu,Haoxuan Che,Jingcai Guo,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OPTD（On-Policy Transition Distillation），一种针对扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的少步蒸馏方法。OPTD从学生自身的轨迹中采样部分状态，使用冻结的仅基于问题的教师模型识别与输出对齐的未来候选，并通过置信度排序，选择能保持教师rollout结果的最长前缀进行联合承诺压缩。训练中使用集瓶颈目标和冻结教师KL锚点，无需金标准回答。在四个数学推理和代码生成基准上取得最优的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步蒸馏方法在离策略（off-policy）轨迹上构建监督信号，导致推理时学生的早期并行承诺改变了后续预测的上下文，实际访问的状态偏离了监督状态，尤其在步压缩最激进时偏差最严重。虽然在线策略（on-policy）蒸馏可以缓解这种不匹配，但如何确定每一步过渡应前进多远仍是一个悬而未决的问题：仅匹配教师的下一步动作会限制压缩效果，而不加区分地合并未来动作可能违反中间依赖关系。

Method: OPTD包含三个关键组件：首先，从少步学生自身的轨迹中采样部分状态；其次，使用冻结的仅针对问题的教师模型来识别与最终结果对齐的未来候选，并按当前状态置信度对这些候选排序；最后，选择最长的前缀，使得其联合承诺能够保持教师rollout的结果。训练目标由两部分组成：一个集瓶颈（set-bottleneck）目标将每个验证过的未来候选提升到解码器的释放阈值，同时使用冻结教师的KL锚点来正则化所有其他活动位置。整个过程不需要金标准回答来构建目标或进行训练。

Result: 在四个数学推理和代码生成基准上，OPTD一致地改善了质量-效率权衡，并在所有评估的少步基线中取得了最强的整体质量约束AUP（Area Under the quality-constrained Performance curve）。

Conclusion: 通过在线策略蒸馏结合一致性引导的自适应压缩，OPTD有效解决了离策略监督导致的状态漂移问题，同时自适应地确定了每个过渡应推进的距离，避免了过浅压缩限制效果和过深合并违反中间依赖的两难困境，从而在少步扩散语言模型蒸馏中实现了更优的质量与效率平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) can predict many tokens in parallel, but accurate generation still requires many iterative denoising steps. Few-step distillation accelerates decoding by compressing multiple teacher steps into a single student transition. However, existing methods construct supervision on off-policy trajectories. At inference, the student's early parallel commitments alter the context of later predictions, so the states it actually visits drift away from the supervised ones--precisely when step compression is most aggressive. On-policy distillation is a natural remedy for this mismatch, but it leaves open how far each transition should advance: matching only the teacher's next action limits compression, while indiscriminately merging future actions can violate intermediate dependencies. To address this limitation, we propose OPTD, On-Policy Transition Distillation with consistency-guided adaptive compression. It samples partial states from the few-step student's own trajectories, uses a frozen, question-only teacher to identify outcome-aligned future candidates, and orders them by current-state confidence. The method then selects the longest prefix whose joint commitment preserves the teacher's rollout outcome. A set-bottleneck objective promotes every verified future candidate to the decoder's release threshold, while a frozen-teacher KL anchor regularizes all other active positions. Neither target construction nor training uses a gold response. Across four mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, OPTD consistently improves the quality--efficiency trade-off and attains the strongest overall quality-constrained AUP among the evaluated few-step baselines.

</details>


### [19] [Every Wrong Answer Counts: Option-Level Psychometrics for LLM Multiple-Choice Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02966)
*Xiao Fei,Yang Zhang,Sarah Almeida Carneiro,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM名义响应模型（LLM-NRM），一种关注选项层面的心理测量框架，通过建模LLM在所有答案选项上的完整分布来联合估计能力与选项级项目特征。在189个LLM和31,554个条目（来自14个基准）上，该模型在预测留出交互方面优于二元项目反应模型和传统基线，能力估计与Arena.ai Elo排名的Spearman相关系数达0.920。研究发现干扰项身份比正确性多提供101%的Fisher信息，仅用错误响应即可恢复完整能力估计（Spearman 0.943），且41个精选条目可将基准缩减770倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多项选择题基准仅以是否选对来二元打分，将所有错误回答视为等价，忽略了LLM在不同错误选项间的偏好可能包含系统性的、关于其行为与能力的有用信息。作者旨在利用这些被忽视的信息来更精细地建模和评估LLM。

Method: 提出LLM-NRM，一种面向选项的（option-aware）心理测量框架，对答案选项上的完整概率分布进行建模，联合估计LLM能力和选项层面的项目特征。该模型将模型特定的响应校准锐度、位置偏好和难度依赖的崩塌行为（fallback behavior）分离为独立分量。实验在189个LLM、31,554个条目、14个基准上进行，并与二元项目反应模型及常规名义响应基线对比。

Result: LLM-NRM在预测留出LLM-项目交互方面优于现有基线；能力估计与外部人类偏好Arena.ai Elo排行榜的Spearman相关性达0.920（最强）。干扰项身份贡献，每项目比仅正确性多出101%的Fisher信息；仅用错误响应即可恢复全信息能力估计（Spearman 0.943）；41个精选条目可在保持全库排名（Kendall相关0.85）的同时实现770倍基准缩减。

Conclusion: 错误答案包含独特的、具有测量价值的信息，而非等价的错误。LLM-NRM展示了利用选项级信息可以在能力评估、基准压缩和LLM行为理解方面带来显著改进。

Abstract: Most multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) only by whether they select the correct answers. This binary scoring treats all incorrect responses alike, even though an LLM's preferences among incorrect options may contain systematic and useful information about its behavior and ability. We introduce the LLM Nominal Response Model (LLM-NRM), an option-aware psychometric framework that models the full distribution over answer choices to jointly estimate LLM ability and option-level item characteristics, while separating model-specific response calibration sharpness, positional preference, and difficulty-dependent fallback behavior. Across 189 LLMs and 31,554 items from 14 benchmarks, LLM-NRM predicts held-out LLM-item interactions more accurately than binary Item Response models and conventional nominal-response baselines, and its ability estimates achieve the strongest Spearman correlation of 0.920 with the external human-preference Arena.ai Elo leaderboard. Distractor identity contributes +101% additional Fisher Information per item beyond correctness, and incorrect responses alone recover full-information ability estimates with Spearman 0.943. The learned item parameters also enable efficient benchmarking, where 41 selected items preserve the full-bank ranking with Kendall's correlation 0.85, corresponding to a 770 times reduction. In conclusion, we show that incorrect answers carry distinct and useful measurement information rather than representing equivalent mistakes.

</details>


### [20] [Mapping the City Through the Lens of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02971)
*Wanqi Liu,Rong Zhao,Zhizhou Sha,Qinyu Cui,Yecheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过匿名化真实城市形态数据，测量了十个开源语言模型对"普通城市"的隐含假设，发现模型普遍偏好建成区面积更大、近期增长更快、基础设施更完善、非住宅容量更高且形态不那么稀疏的城市轮廓，并提出了一个可复现的框架来实证追踪模型的城市认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在回填不明确的城市指代时，会隐式地带有关于城市规模、形态、基础设施、环境和功能的假设，但这些假设此前是模糊且难以量化的。作者希望在不提及具体地名的情况下，系统性地测量这些隐含假设，使"模型眼中的普通城市"这一模糊概念变得可实证追踪。

Method: 设计包含以下要素：使用十个开放权重检查点；基于40个审计指标和七个领域，构建来自真实形态城市中心的匿名化档案；采用约束概率评级、预设可靠性筛选、谱系感知聚合、多种人口加权方案；设置独立复制样本；进行整体档案验证。通过指标逐项评级与整体档案直接评级的交叉验证来确保结果的可靠性。

Result: 模型最一致的共享倾向是偏好更大建成面积、更快近期增长、更多基础设施映射和非住宅容量，以及更不稀疏的城市形态。绝大多数符合条件的趋势在复制数据中重现；整体档案直接评级与逐指标构建之间具有中等一致性；在控制城市规模和发达程度后，地理差异缩小；配对任务显示典型性与可取性通常紧密对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架使得"模型认为的普通城市"这一隐含观念变得可量化、可追踪，揭示了一种跨模型共享但各有差异的城市画像，为理解语言模型中的城市认知偏差提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Language models often complete an underspecified reference to a city with unstated assumptions about urban size, form, infrastructure, environment, and function. We measure those assumptions without naming places. Ten open-weight checkpoints rate anonymized profiles derived from real morphological urban centres across 40 audited indicators and seven domains. The design combines constrained probability-based ratings, prespecified reliability screens, lineage-aware aggregation, multiple population weightings, an independent replication sample, and whole-profile validation. The clearest shared tendency favours urban profiles with larger developed area, faster recent growth, greater mapped infrastructure and non-residential capacity, and less sparse form. Most eligible directions recur in the replication data, and direct ratings of complete profiles show moderate agreement with the indicator-wise construction. Geographic differences shrink after accounting for city scale and development, while reliably measured paired tasks indicate that typicality and desirability are often closely aligned. The framework makes an otherwise vague notion of what models regard as an ordinary city empirically traceable. The resulting evidence delineates a shared yet model-dependent portrait of the city through the lens of language models.

</details>


### [21] [TQLite: Multi-LLM Jury Guided Distillation for Real-time MQM Translation Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02975)
*Bhavin Jawade,Cameron R. Wolfe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TQLite蒸馏框架，通过多大型推理模型（LRM）陪审团生成合成训练数据，使小型语言模型（SLM）在MQM翻译质量评估任务上接近最先进LRM评估器的性能，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）和大型推理模型（LRM）在MQM翻译质量评估中表现优异，但其部署成本高昂，难以大规模应用。小型语言模型（SLM）虽然高效，但在评估任务所需的复杂推理上能力不足。亟需一种能够在保持评估质量的同时实现S成本效益的方案。

Method: 1) 对SLM、LLM和LRM在多种翻译质量评估设置下进行大规模实证基准测试；2) 提出TQLite蒸馏框架，利用多LRM陪审团（multi-LRM jury）生成高质量合成训练数据，通过数据筛选技术和多样化模型面板的评估响应聚合来训练SLM。

Result: 经TQLite训练的SLM在MQM评估性能上远超标准SLM的开箱即用评估能力，接近最优LRM评估器的水平，同时提供了可扩展且成本高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: TQLite证明了通过知识蒸馏可以将高成本的LRM评估能力迁移到低成本的SLM上，为大规模翻译质量评估提供了可行的成本效益平衡方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in MQM-based translation quality (TQ) evaluation, and recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) promise even greater improvements. However, both LLMs and LRMs are computationally expensive to deploy at scale, while small language models (SLMs)---though much more efficient---struggle with the complex reasoning required for evaluation tasks. In this work, we present an extensive empirical study benchmarking SLMs, LLMs, and LRMs across a wide range of TQ evaluation setups, providing a comprehensive view of the current landscape and establishing best practices. To address the scalability challenge, we introduce TQLite, a novel distillation framework that enables SLMs to approach the MQM evaluation performance of the best LRM-based evaluators. Our approach leverages a multi-LRM jury to generate high-quality synthetic training data via practical data curation techniques and aggregation of evaluation responses across a diverse panel of models. Our results demonstrate that SLMs trained via TQLite achieve strong MQM evaluation performance that far exceeds off-the-shelf evaluation capabilities of standard SLMs, offering a scalable and cost-effective alternative to LLM- and LRM-based evaluators.

</details>


### [22] [On the Non-Specificity of Statistical Measures Used in Script Decipherment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02999)
*Nikhil Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构造SIGIL生成式徽章系统（包含3000条文本语料、具有组合语义但无音位值），在预注册的评价框架下测试了"统计规律性能证明未破译符号系统编码语言"这一标准的核心前提——特异性。结果显示SIGIL在重复性、方向不对称性和词汇分布测试中与印度河流域文字（Indus script）语料获得相同分类，熵、频率、位置、预测、分类器和网络等多类指标也重现了印度河文字的特征签名。随后的顺序破译压力测试虽在英文、梵文和泰米尔语上达到高词典覆盖率，但分组留出性能下降和不稳定密钥表明该覆盖率识别意义有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未破译符号系统（如印度河流域文字）常被用作编码语言的证据，但这类推断依赖特异性前提——即观测结果必须在"合理的结构化非语言系统"中显得异常。此前缺乏对此前提的严格实证检验，本文旨在通过建设性构造来测试该前提是否成立。

Method: 作者构建SIGIL符号系统，其核心语料库含3000条文本，具有明确的组合语义但符号不携带音位值。评价采用预注册方法：预先编制含54种方法的文献注册表，仅当能重现已发表的印度河结果和源定义决策规则时，方法才被纳入精确评分。对SIGIL和印度河语料应用相同评分标准，覆盖重复性、方向不对称性、词汇分布测试，以及熵、频率、位置、预测、分类器、网络等重建指标；另设计顺序破译压力测试检验词典覆盖率。

Result: SIGIL在所有按此标准评分的指标上与印度河语料获得相同分类——即在重复性、方向不对称性和词汇分布测试中不可区分。各类重建指标（熵、频率、位置、预测、分类器和网络度量）也再现了印度河样式的签名。顺序破译测试对英文、梵文和泰米尔语达到高词典覆盖率，但分组留出性能下降且密钥不稳定。

Conclusion: 本文结论并非判定印度河符号编码何种语言，而是证明：所评估的度量只能检测到系统的"组织性"而非"语言特异性"，因此单凭这些统计度量无法确定符号系统编码的是语音（语言）。该构造实验为"统计规律性推断语言编码"提供了重要的否证性证据。

Abstract: Statistical regularities are routinely offered as evidence that undeciphered sign systems encode language; the Indus script debate is the canonical example. Any such inference rests on specificity: the reported outcome must be unusual among plausible structured non-languages. We test that premise constructively with SIGIL, a purpose-built generative emblem system whose 3,000-text core corpus carries explicit compositional meanings although no sign has a phonological value. A literature registry compiled in advance of evaluation records 54 methods and admits a method to exact scoring when both the published Indus outcome and a source-defined decision rule can be reproduced. SIGIL receives the same category as the Indus corpus on every criterion scored this way, across repetition, directional-asymmetry, and lexical-distribution tests. Declared reconstructions of entropy, frequency, positional, predictive, classifier, and network measures reproduce the familiar Indus-like signatures as well. A sequential decipherment stress test then reaches high dictionary coverage for English, Sanskrit, and Tamil on the same corpus, while grouped held-out declines and unstable keys reveal how little that coverage identifies. The construction does not decide what the Indus signs encode: it shows that the evaluated measures detect organization without being specific to language, and therefore cannot, on their own, establish encoded speech.

</details>


### [23] [Language Models Encode the Contextual Truth of Propositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03035)
*Rupak Sarkar,Pritika Ramu,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）如何在线性激活空间中表征"上下文真值"（contextual truth），即由上下文证据而非世界知识决定的命题真假。通过协作视觉-语言任务，发现LLM的真值表征会受到伙伴断言的影响，且在决策边界附近的命题更容易被"说服"翻转其真值；进一步区分了两种谄媚（sycophancy）形式：表面妥协但内部保持假命题表征，或在表征层面也发生翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明LLM在激活空间中以线性方向编码事实命题的真值，但这一发现主要针对世界知识型事实。上下文真值（由上下文证据决定的真值）如何被表征、以及这种表征是否受社交影响（如对话伙伴的断言）尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 通过两LLM协作的视觉-语言任务收集对话记录，分析命题真值在激活空间的线性表征；2) 采用steering实验提供因果证据；3) 设计不同的输出策略对比，验证表征在不同输出策略下的稳定性；4) 通过区分内部表征与输出行为，识别两种谄媚类型：表征保持与表征翻转。

Result: LLM维持的上下文真值线性表征在结构不同的输出策略下持续存在，即使输出不需判断其真值也如此。伙伴断言显著影响真值表征，尤其对处于决策边界附近的命题影响最大。当模型显式复述虚假声明以表示同意时，其内部真值表征发生翻转的概率比隐含同意高出2.59倍。

Conclusion: 上下文真值在LLM中具有可操纵的线性表示，社交信号（伙伴断言）可以穿透内部表征层面影响模型的真值判断，而不仅仅是影响输出行为。这揭示了需要将内部表征与输出行为分开评估的重要性，以更好理解和应对LLM的谄媚行为。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that LLMs encode the truth of factual propositions along linear directions in activation space. It's unclear how these representations extend to contextual truth: propositions whose truth is determined by in-context evidence rather than world knowledge. We show that LLMs maintain a linear representation of contextual truth that persists across structurally different output policies, even when the output doesn't require the model to determine a proposition's truth, and show causal evidence via steering experiments. Using the transcripts from a collaborative vision-language task that requires two LLMs to maintain a shared common ground, we show that truth representations of a proposition are significantly swayed by partner assertions about that proposition, even when the LLM has enough evidence to determine its truth. We find evidence that propositions near the decision boundary are more susceptible to having their truth shifted through partner assertions. Separating representation from output distinguish two forms of sycophancy that output behavior alone cannot: the model may accommodate a false proposition while continuing to represent it as false, or shift its representation across the boundary. The latter is 2.59x more common when the model agrees by restating the false claim explicitly than when it agrees implicitly.

</details>


### [24] [Beyond Accuracy: A Multidimensional Evaluation of Statistical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03038)
*Monnie McGee,Mateo Langston Smith,Julian Cabrera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种多维评估框架，将响应准确性、响应行为、结构化主题建模和词汇相似性分析相结合，用于评估15个当前代LLM在90个跨越高中、本科和研究生水平的统计问题上的推理表现。结果显示，模型准确率在55%-78%之间差异显著，但所有模型在统计推理的概念组织上具有共性，同时词汇相似性分析揭示了同一供应商模型间存在轻微但一致的风格差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计推理是多维度的，但现有LLM评估往往只关注响应准确性，忽视了模型如何构建和传达统计解释这一重要维度。需要更全面的评估框架来理解生成式AI中的统计推理能力。

Method: 结合响应准确性、响应行为分析、结构化主题建模（Structural Topic Modeling）和词汇相似性分析四种互补方法，对15个当前代LLM针对90个来自四套统计考试（涵盖高中、本科、研究生三个水平）问题的解释进行分析评估。

Result: 模型准确率差异显著（55%-78%）；结构化主题建模显示所有模型在统计推理的概念组织上呈现共性；词汇相似性分析发现同一供应商（如Anthropic、OpenAI）开发的模型之间的解释风格比不同供应商之间的模型略更相似，但差异幅度较小且一致。

Conclusion: 当代LLM的统计推理不能仅以准确性来表征，结合响应行为和模型生成解释的互补分析可以提供对生成式AI统计推理更全面的评估。

Abstract: Statistical reasoning is multidimensional, yet evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically emphasize response accuracy while overlooking how models construct and communicate statistical explanations. This study demonstrates the value of a multidimensional evaluation by combining response accuracy, response behavior, structural topic modeling, and lexical similarity analysis. The framework is applied to explanations generated by 15 current-generation LLMs responding to 90 questions drawn from four statistics examinations spanning high school, undergraduate, and graduate levels. Accuracy varied substantially across models, ranging from 55\% to 78\%. In contrast, structural topic modeling revealed a common conceptual organization of statistical reasoning across all models, while lexical similarity analysis identified modest but consistent vendor-specific differences in explanatory style. Models developed by the same vendor (e.g. Anthropic, OpenAI) produced explanations that were slightly more similar than models from different vendors. These findings demonstrate that statistical reasoning in contemporary LLMs cannot be characterized by accuracy alone and illustrate how complementary analyses of response behavior and model-generated explanations provide a more comprehensive evaluation of statistical reasoning in generative AI.

</details>


### [25] [Emulate or Estimate? The Divergent Strengths of Base and Post-Trained Language Models for Opinion Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03044)
*Seth Grief-Albert,Jessica Bo,Difan Jiao,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型模拟人类意见的冲突结果源于混淆了两个不同的任务：模拟（emulation，生成个体响应聚合成总体分布）和估计（estimation，直接预测总体分布）。通过评估六组基础模型和后训练模型在Pew美国趋势面板数据集上的表现，发现基础模型更擅长模拟，后训练模型更擅长估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于LLM模拟人类意见的研究结果相互矛盾：一些研究发现模型与人类调查数据高度对齐，另一些则发现"人格坍缩"和人口统计敏感性弱。作者认为这些冲突源于没有区分两个本质上不同的任务。

Method: 将人类意见模拟任务分解为两类：emulation（模型生成个体响应并聚合成群体分布）和estimation（模型直接预测群体分布）。在Pew American Trends Panel数据集上评估六组匹配的基础模型（base）和后训练模型（post-trained），对比其在两类任务上的表现。

Result: 基础模型是更强的模拟器：生成的响应分布更接近人类真实分布，且更好地保留了人口统计结构。后训练模型是更强的估计器：当被直接询问时能产生更准确的分布预测。

Conclusion: 模型选择应取决于任务类型：如果任务需要生成文本（模拟），应选择基础模型；如果任务需要直接预测分布（估计），应选择后训练模型。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate human opinions, but prior work reports conflicting results: some studies find promising alignment with human survey data, while others find persona collapse and weak demographic sensitivity. We show that much of this conflict stems from conflating two distinct tasks. We call the first task emulation, in which models generate individual responses that aggregate into a population distribution. We call the second task estimation, in which models directly predict the population distribution. Evaluating six matched base and post-trained models on the Pew American Trends Panel, we find that base models are stronger emulators: they produce response distributions closer to human ground truth and better preserve demographic structure. Post-trained models are stronger estimators, producing more accurate distributional predictions when asked directly. We propose that model selection for human simulation should be guided by whether the task requires generating text or predicting distributions.

</details>


### [26] [PI-Mem: Pushing Long-Context Reasoning to 3.6M Tokens with Parallel-Iterative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03048)
*Dawei Liu,Haixu Song,Shuang Cheng,Shijie Wang,Haozheng Hou,Kaifeng Liu,Ermo Hua,Zhonghang Yuan,Zhijie Zhong,Yuchen Fan,Biqing Qi,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-Mem（并行迭代记忆）机制，通过并行处理所有语块并迭代精炼共享记忆来替代顺序更新记忆的递归方法，解决了长上下文推理中的证据覆盖和序列依赖两大瓶颈，在HotpotQA上相比递归记忆基线提升+6.25和+7.81个绝对点，同时获得6.1×和2.1×的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归记忆方法在长上下文推理中面临两个固有挑战：(1) 顺序的逐块更新会导致早期关键证据被后期无关内容覆盖；(2) 块间串行依赖限制了并行性，导致延迟随上下文长度线性增长。解决这些问题对于实现可扩展的长上下文多跳问答至关重要。

Method: PI-Mem在每一轮中并行读取所有块（基于当前共享记忆的条件），从每个块中筛选新的或互补证据，并将选定证据合并为紧凑的共享记忆供下一轮使用。记忆在有限的迭代轮数中逐步精炼。为了防止冗余轮次，通过强化学习优化工作流，引入辅助的轮次效率奖励，使模型能在证据积累充分后自适应提前退出。

Result: 在HotpotQA基准上（上下文长度达360万token），PI-Mem在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B和Qwen2.5-7B上分别超越递归记忆基线+6.25和+7.81个绝对点，同时实现6.1×和2.1×的推理加速比。结果表明PI-Mem打破了长上下文推理中准确率与效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: PI-Mem通过并行迭代的记忆精炼机制，成功打破了长上下文推理中准确率与效率的权衡，为超长文档上的复杂多跳问答提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning remains a critical bottleneck for large language models, as recent recurrent-memory approaches face two inherent challenges: sequential chunk-wise updates can overwrite early critical evidence with later irrelevant content, and serial inter-chunk dependencies limit parallelism and cause latency to increase with context length. To address these issues, we propose PI-Mem (Parallel-Iterative Memory), a mechanism that processes all chunks in parallel and iteratively refines a shared memory over a bounded number of turns. In each turn, PI-Mem reads all chunks in parallel conditioned on the current memory, selects new or complementary evidence from each chunk, and merges the selected evidence into a compact shared memory for the next turn. To discourage redundant turns, we optimize the workflow through reinforcement learning with an auxiliary turn-efficiency reward, enabling the model to adaptively exit once sufficient evidence has been accumulated. We evaluate PI-Mem with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Qwen2.5-7B on the HotpotQA benchmark across context lengths up to 3.6 million tokens and find that it outperforms the recurrent-memory baseline by +6.25 and +7.81 absolute points while achieving 6.1$\times$ and 2.1$\times$ inference speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate that PI-Mem breaks the accuracy--efficiency trade-off in long-context reasoning and provides a scalable approach to complex multi-hop question answering over extremely long documents.

</details>


### [27] [SeqLLM: Augmenting LLMs with Behavioral-Sequence Modeling for High-Stakes Decisions at WeChat Pay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03063)
*Guilin Li,Jiaxing Zhang,Matthias Hwai Yong Tan,Bo Wang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeqLLM是一个为大型语言模型添加行为序列建模能力的框架，通过紧凑离散行为词汇表、轻量投影器和前缀引导能力注入三个组件，在保留语言能力的同时实现行为序列理解。已在微信支付部署，日审数千万商户，将筛查精度从92.0%提升至97.5%，并在公开推荐基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型支付平台的商户风控需要同时理解商户的文本画像和长行为序列，但大语言模型擅长文本却无法原生建模长序列，直接适配又会造成灾难性遗忘，亟需一个既能建模行为序列又保留语言能力的统一框架。

Method: SeqLLM包含三个组件：(1) 紧凑离散词汇表，将行为事件表示为原生token；(2) 轻量投影器，通过两阶段对齐课程将这些token嵌入LLM语义空间；(3) 前缀引导能力注入，通过任务前缀的监督微调而非持续预训练来获得序列建模能力。

Result: 在微信支付部署中日审数千万商户，筛查精度从92.0%提升至97.5%；预训练行为token嵌入使生产级欺诈检测器Precision@Top-0.01%提升26.8个百分点。在MovieLens和Amazon上较User-LLM基线Recall@5最高提升32%，在RecIF上Pass@32提升14.2%，且仅用OneRec-8B完整流程五分之一GPU天数。

Conclusion: SeqLLM证明通过设计合适的行为token化表示、对齐投影器和前缀注入策略，可以在不牺牲语言能力的前提下为大语言模型赋予行为序列建模能力，且在支付风控和推荐系统两个方向均验证了其有效性和可部署性。

Abstract: Merchant risk control at large payment platforms screens tens of millions of merchants daily, where false positives harm legitimate merchants and false negatives leave harmful activity undetected. The hardest cases require jointly understanding a merchant's textual profile and long behavioral sequence. Large language models (LLMs) excel at text but cannot natively model such sequences, while adapting them often causes catastrophic forgetting. We present SeqLLM, a framework that adds behavioral-sequence modeling to a pretrained LLM while preserving its language ability. SeqLLM combines three components: a compact discrete vocabulary that represents behavioral events as native tokens; a lightweight projector, trained with a two-stage alignment curriculum, that grounds these tokens in the LLM's semantic space; and prefix-guided capability injection, which acquires sequence-modeling ability through task-prefixed supervised fine-tuning rather than continual pre-training. SeqLLM is deployed at WeChat Pay, screening millions of merchants daily. Against the production DeepSeek-based LLM baseline, it raises screening precision from 92.0% to 97.5%. Its pretrained behavior-token embeddings also improve Precision@Top-0.01% by 26.8 percentage points in a production fraud detector serving billion-scale transaction traffic. Beyond payments, SeqLLM achieves state-of-the-art results on public recommendation benchmarks. On MovieLens and Amazon, it surpasses the strong User-LLM baseline by up to 32% relative Recall@5 while retaining markedly stronger language ability. On RecIF, it improves Pass@32 by 14.2% over the full OneRec-8B pipeline using only one-fifth of its GPU-days.

</details>


### [28] [Activation-Guided Neuron Intervention to Induce Alzheimer's-Related Computational Language Phenotypes in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03067)
*Rui He,Ercong Nie,Hong Jiang,Iris E. Sommer,Philipp Homan,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激活引导的干预框架（使用Qwen3-8B大语言模型），通过识别并调制AD（阿尔茨海默病）与非AD语音转录之间激活率差异显著的feed-forward神经元，生成九种编辑变体，验证了由临床语言差异识别出的神经元能够跨多个认知域影响行为，为AD相关的计算表型提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能够从自发语音中检测AD相关的认知功能障碍，但仅靠检测无法证明模型内部表征是否真的对行为产生功能性贡献。作者希望建立因果联系，验证从临床语言差异中识别出的内部神经机制是否真正驱动模型输出行为的变化。

Method: 使用Qwen3-8B，通过激活引导框架识别AD与对照组转录本之间前馈神经元激活率的差异；通过缩放对应的下投影层权重来调制这些神经元的输出贡献，生成九种在干预方向（放大/衰减）、幅度和范围上不同的编辑变体；让原始模型和编辑模型完成12轮神经心理测验，并通过盲法人工评分和计算语言学指标进行评估。

Result: 放大AD相关神经元导致故事回忆、言语流畅性、工作记忆、程序性话语、场景构建和共指消解出现分级损伤；衰减干预大体上保留了性能并选择性改善了一些结果。放大还降低了词汇意外度、思想密度、句法复杂性和话语数量，与人类AD语音的变化模式广泛平行。

Conclusion: 仅根据临床语言差异识别出的神经元能够跨多个认知域影响行为，为AD相关计算表型提供了概念验证，并为实验性地研究语言与更广泛认知功能障碍之间的联系提供了受控框架。

Abstract: Changes in spontaneous speech provide an early signal of cognitive dysfunction in Alzheimer's disease (AD) that large language models (LLMs) can detect. However, detection alone cannot establish whether the underlying model representations contribute functionally to behavior. We introduce an activation-guided intervention framework using Qwen3-8B. The framework identifies feed-forward neurons with higher activation rates for AD than control transcripts and modulates their output contributions during generation by scaling the corresponding down-projection weights. This yielded nine edited variants differing in intervention direction, magnitude, and scope. The original and edited models completed the same 12-turn neuropsychological battery, assessed through blinded human ratings and computational linguistic measures. Amplifying AD-associated neurons produced graded impairments in story recall, verbal fluency, working memory, procedural discourse, scene construction, and coreference resolution. Attenuation largely preserved performance and selectively improved several outcomes. Amplification also reduced lexical surprisal, idea density, syntactic complexity, and discourse quantity, broadly paralleling changes reported in human AD speech. These findings show that neurons identified solely from clinical language differences can influence behavior across multiple cognitive domains, providing proof of concept for an AD-related computational phenotype and a controlled framework for experimentally examining links between language and broader cognitive dysfunction.

</details>


### [29] [CVPO: Enhancing LLM Reinforcement Learning Reasoning via Value-Variance Adaptation and Dynamic Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03068)
*Ziqi Jia,Yalu Ouyang,Bo Pang,Panpan Li,Hangfei Xu,Shengzhao Wen,Shiyong Li,Yanpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CVPO（Curriculum-guided Value-Variance Policy Optimization）方法，通过轨迹级别的token价值方差量化生成的内在随机性并设计方差感知的优势调整机制，同时在问题级别引入动态课程权重以适应问题难度，从而解决强化学习微调LLM时反馈精度不足和问题难度漂移的问题，在数学推理任务上优于VAPO等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在增强LLM推理能力时存在两个关键问题：一是对生成答案轨迹的反馈信号精度不足，导致训练信号不够准确；二是存在问题难度漂移现象，即训练过程中模型面对的问题难度与当前能力不匹配。这些问题制约了模型推理能力的进一步提升。

Method: CVPO包含两个层面的设计：(1) 轨迹层面：发现token级别的价值方差与探索强度相关，通过理论分析证明该方差界定策略更新的幅度；利用估计的轨迹价值方差量化生成的内在随机性，并据此设计方差感知的优势调整机制来适配不同类型的奖励。(2) 问题层面：引入动态课程加权方法，根据问题难度自适应调整权重，使模型在每个训练阶段专注于与其当前能力相匹配的任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，CVPO在多种数学任务上优于VAPO等强基于价值的基线方法，取得更好的性能和更强的探索能力，使语言模型实现更准确且鲁棒的推理。

Conclusion: CVPO通过轨迹级方差感知的优势调整和问题级的动态课程学习，有效解决了RL训练LLM中反馈精度不足和难度漂移的问题，为提升LLM推理能力提供了更精确有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as an effective method for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods suffer from insufficient precision in feedback on generated answer trajectories and exhibit the phenomenon of problem difficulty drift. To address these challenges, we propose CVPO - Curriculum-guided Value-Variance Policy Optimization. At the response trajectory level, we find that token-level value-variance correlates with exploration intensity. Our theoretical analysis shows this variance bounds policy update magnitude. We then use the estimated trajectory value-variance to quantify the intrinsic randomness in generation. Based on this, we design a variance-aware advantage adjustment mechanism for different reward types. At the question level, we introduce a dynamic curriculum weighting method that adapts to question difficulty. This helps the model focus on tasks matched to its current ability during each training stage. Experimental results show our method outperforms strong value-based baselines like VAPO. It achieves better performance and stronger exploration, enabling more accurate and robust reasoning in language models across various math tasks.

</details>


### [30] [PAMT: Process-Aligned Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03077)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAMT，一个面向多领域机器翻译（MDMT）的过程对齐训练框架。通过分析发现大型推理模型（LRMs）的显式推理在长文本和高难度翻译中有效，但在术语密集和风格受限场景中会漂移，根源在于信用分配瓶颈——无法识别哪些翻译步骤真正有助于最终翻译。PAMT结合冷启动领域感知的Long-CoT监督与强化学习，利用序列级格式/结果奖励和步骤级过程奖励来训练模型。在两个骨干模型上，PAMT优于基线和MT专用基线，且在领域内、OOD和多语言场景中与强LLM/LRM保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译不仅需要流畅的生成，还需要领域敏感的翻译决策（如领域消歧、术语控制和风格适应）。虽然大型推理模型（LRMs）通过中间翻译步骤使决策显式化，但现有方法在训练时只优化最终输出或粗粒度轨迹，无法识别哪些翻译步骤真正对最终翻译有帮助，导致术语密集和风格受限场景下性能漂移。作者希望解决这一信用分配瓶颈。

Method: PAMT是一个过程对齐训练框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 冷启动领域感知的Long-CoT监督，为模型提供领域相关的推理链初始化；(2) 强化学习阶段，使用序列级格式奖励和结果奖励评估最终翻译质量，同时引入步骤级过程奖励来衡量每个显式翻译步骤对参考翻译似然性的贡献程度，从而实现细粒度的过程对齐。

Result: 在15个领域和4个翻译方向上，PAMT在两个骨干模型上均优于基础模型，平均上超过MT专用基线，并且在领域内、OOD和多语言设置中与强大的LLM/LRM保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式推理在MT中是双刃剑：在长文本和高难度场景中有帮助，但在术语密集和风格受限场景中会漂移。通过步骤级过程奖励解决信用分配瓶颈的PAMT框架，能够有效对齐推理过程与最终翻译质量，验证了过程级监督在多领域机器翻译中的价值。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) requires more than fluent generation: it demands domain-sensitive translation decisions such as domain disambiguation, terminology control, and stylistic adaptation. Large reasoning models (LRMs) make such decisions explicit through intermediate translation steps, but our analysis across 15 domains and four translation directions shows that this explicit reasoning is double-edged: it improves long-form and high-difficulty translation, yet often drifts in terminology-intensive and stylistically constrained settings. We trace this failure to a credit-assignment bottleneck: existing methods optimize final outputs or coarse trajectories, but cannot identify which translation steps actually help the final translation. To address this, we propose PAMT, a process-aligned training framework that combines cold-start domain-aware Long-CoT supervision with reinforcement learning. PAMT uses sequence-level format and outcome rewards for the final translation, together with a step-level process reward that measures how much each explicit translation step increases the likelihood of the reference translation. Across two backbones, PAMT improves over base models, outperforms MT-specialized baselines on average, and remains competitive with strong LLMs/LRMs across in-domain, OOD, and multilingual settings.

</details>


### [31] [Scalable Frequency- and Length-Aware Subdocument Deduplication for Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03089)
*Hai Wang,Chenhao Wang,Qifeng Cai,Yixiu Liu,Miao Peng,Nuo Chen,Yuanlin Tu,Chengcheng Xu,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的子文档级去重框架，将重复检测与副本保留策略解耦。通过自然边界分割、规范化精确哈希和分布式聚合识别重复组，再应用显式的频率和长度感知保留策略为每个重复组分配自适应的副本预算。在FineWeb-Edu和含代码网页语料上的实验表明该方法训练的模型整体性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练语料包含大量重复内容，文档级去重不够充分；现有的子文档去重方法存在局限：基于后缀数组的方法在分片内独立进行，无法检测跨分片重复且结果对分片配置敏感；基于哈希的方法虽能实现全局精确重复计数，但常依赖固定副本保留策略，无法适应异构的重复模式。

Method: 提出可扩展的子文档去重框架，核心是将重复检测与副本保留解耦：1) 通过自然边界分割识别重复组；2) 使用规范化精确哈希进行重复检测；3) 通过分布式聚合汇总全局重复信息；4) 应用显式的频率和长度感知保留策略，为每个重复组自适应分配副本预算——低频率或短重复保留更多副本，高频或长重复更激进地删除。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu和含代码网页语料上的实验表明，使用该方法处理的数据训练的模型在所有评估设置中取得了最佳整体性能。结果强调了显式副本保留控制的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式的副本保留控制对于子文档去重至关重要，将重复检测与保留决策解耦的框架能够在保持数据质量的同时有效控制冗余，且该方法具有良好的可扩展性和全局一致性。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining corpora contain substantial duplicate content. Although document-level deduplication is widely used, removing subdocument-level redundancy remains challenging. At corpus scale, suffix-array-based methods are commonly applied independently within shards, leaving cross-shard duplicates undetected and making the resulting retention behavior sensitive to the sharding configuration. Hash-based methods enable global exact duplicate counting, but often rely on fixed copy-retention policies that cannot accommodate heterogeneous repetition patterns. We propose a scalable subdocument deduplication framework that decouples duplicate detection from copy retention. It identifies duplicate groups through natural-boundary segmentation, normalized exact hashing, and distributed aggregation, and then applies an explicit frequency- and length-aware retention policy that allocates an adaptive copy budget to each group, retaining more copies of low-frequency or short repetitions while more aggressively deleting high-frequency or long ones. Experiments on FineWeb-Edu and a code-containing web corpus show that models trained on data processed by our method achieve the best overall performance among the evaluated settings. These results underscore the importance of explicit copy-retention control.

</details>


### [32] [VIVID: A Culturally Grounded Benchmark Exposing the Figurative Language Gap in Vietnamese NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03095)
*Tu Tran Do,Nhat Ngoc Nguyen,Khanh-Tung Tran,Hoang D. Nguyen,Tu Minh Phuong,Long Hoang Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VIVID，首个系统评估越南语文化比喻语言理解的基准数据集，包含1,636条成语和谚语，标注了五个复杂性特征和七个语义主题。通过LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架测试了八个前沿模型，发现越南语特化模型严重落后于多语言模型，且即便最好的模型得分也不到最高分的50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在文化背景丰富的比喻语言理解上表现不足，而越南语缺乏系统性的比喻语言理解基准。作者旨在填补这一空白，为衡量模型在越南语成语和谚语上的文化能力提供标准化评估工具。

Method: 构建包含1,636条越南语成语/谚语的基准数据集，标注五个复杂性特征（字面表达、语用细微差别、汉越词、生僻词汇、民间知识）和七个语义主题。设计生成式与判别式任务相结合的评估框架，提出基于方面的提示的LLM-as-a-Judge方法（Cohen's kappa=0.792验证了其与人类判断的一致性）。对八个SOTA模型进行系统评估，包括少数样本提示（few-shot prompting）的消融实验。

Result: 越南语特化模型（VinaLLaMA-7B: 0.13）远逊于多语言模型（GPT-4o: 2.46）；即便是最优模型得分也未达最高分的一半。Few-shot提示并非普适有效，GPT-4o甚至因文体过拟合而出现性能退化。系统性地揭示了字面过度解读、词汇缺口和语用扁平化等功能失效。

Conclusion: 当前模型缺乏文化胜任力，无法进行细微的比喻解读。VIVID为推进文化丰富情境下的比喻语言理解提供了不可或缺的工具，并指出了该领域未来的研究方向。

Abstract: We present VIVID (Vietnamese Idioms for Validation and Interpretation Depth), the first systematic benchmark for evaluating culturally grounded figurative language understanding in Vietnamese. VIVID comprises 1,636 idioms and proverbs annotated with five complexity traits (literal expressions, pragmatic nuances, Sino-Vietnamese terms, uncommon vocabulary, folk knowledge) and seven semantic themes. We establish an evaluation framework combining generative and discriminative tasks, proposing an LLM-as-a-Judge approach with aspect-based prompting validated against human judgment (Cohen's kappa = 0.792). Evaluating eight state-of-the-art models reveals critical gaps: Vietnamese-specialized models drastically underperform multilingual systems (VinaLLaMA-7B: 0.13 vs. GPT-4o: 2.46), and even top models achieve less than 50% of maximum scores. Notably, few-shot prompting does not universally improve performance, with GPT-4o exhibiting degradation due to stylistic overfitting. Our analysis exposes systematic failures including literal over-interpretation, lexical gaps, and pragmatic flattening, demonstrating that current models lack cultural competence for nuanced figurative interpretation. VIVID provides an essential tool for advancing figurative language understanding in culturally rich contexts.

</details>


### [33] [What Language Does and What the Evidence Supports: A Functional Role Taxonomy and Evidence Audit of Language Grounding in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03099)
*Yifan Guo,Chenghao Li,Zhu Wang,Wei Xu,Yu Li,Yulong Zhu,Zhuo Sun,Bin Guo,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述提出了五个非排他的语言功能角色框架（规范、具身表征、行动编排、接地调控、执行耦合），用于区分"语言在具身智能体中扮演什么作用"与"该作用是否得到充分接地验证"这两个问题，并逐项评估已有文献中语言贡献的证据支持程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型将语言广泛引入具身智能体，但语言的存在并不能说明其对系统的实际贡献及其接地程度。现有研究通常以架构（模块化或端到端）为比较单位，难以对语言的具体功能角色进行有依据的评估，因此需要一种以功能主张为单位的分析框架，以弥合功能使用与证据支持之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出五种非排他的语言功能角色：规范（Specification）、具身表征（Embodied Representation）、行动编排（Action Orchestration）、接地调控（Grounding Regulation）和执行耦合（Execution Coupling）。对每种角色，追踪从语言内容到其具身消费者的路径，识别可验证其声称责任的观测数据或干预手段，并逐项评估文献中证据是否支持语言被指派的特定职责。

Result: 应用该框架于文献综述后，发现功能使用与证据支持之间存在系统性差距：可解释或修订的语言中间表征可能不正确、未被使用或无法影响后续行为；即使行动直接以语言为条件，系统级成功也不能单独隔离语言的贡献。以角色主张而非架构作为比较单位，可以公平比较模块化与端到端具身智能体。

Conclusion: 应基于逐项接地主张来评估语言的贡献，而非笼统地说语言"帮助了"具身智能体。以功能角色为比较单元能够在不超出报告证据范围的前提下实现跨架构比较，推动具身智能体中语言贡献研究的严谨化。

Abstract: Foundation models place language throughout embodied agents, but its presence does not show what it contributes or how well that contribution is grounded. This survey separates these two questions. We define five non-exclusive functional roles for language: Specification, Embodied Representation, Action Orchestration, Grounding Regulation, and Execution Coupling. For each role, we trace the path from linguistic content to its embodied consumer and identify the observations or interventions that can test the claimed responsibility. Applying this framework to the reviewed literature reveals a recurring gap between functional use and evidential support. Interpretable or revised linguistic intermediates may be incorrect, go unused, or fail to affect later behavior. Even when actions are directly conditioned on language, system-level success does not by itself isolate language's contribution. We therefore evaluate grounding claim by claim, asking whether the reported evidence supports the specific responsibility assigned to language. Using role claims rather than architectures as the unit of comparison allows us to compare modular and end-to-end embodied agents without extending conclusions beyond the reported evidence.

</details>
