Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）的位置上下文与自回归模型根本不同：AR解码暴露连续前缀，而MDLM去噪产生动态、非连续的揭示与掩码token配置。现有位置编码如RoPE仅捕获序列顺序和成对位移，对这种演化的token可用性结构不敏感，缺乏对这种分布感知的建模能力。

Method: MDLMPE将token可用性表示为二元序列，应用距离感知的高斯加权，通过余弦基投影得到分布感知的位置特征；将其加到token嵌入中，并由轻量级MLP映射为角偏移量来调制标准RoPE相位。

Result: 在LLaDA和DREAM上的大量实验表明，MDLMPE在监督微调、预训练、零样本评估和block-diffusion设置中普遍优于传统位置编码方法。消融实验证明可用性状态、高斯局部性、谱基和嵌入注入的完整组合效果最强。

Conclusion: 演化中的token可用性分布是掩码扩散语言模型中一种有效的位置信号，可显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling, but their positional context differs fundamentally from that of autoregressive (AR) models. Whereas AR decoding exposes a contiguous prefix, MDLM denoising produces dynamic, non-contiguous configurations of revealed and masked tokens. Conventional positional encodings such as RoPE capture sequence order and pairwise displacement but remain insensitive to this evolving token-availability structure. To address this limitation, we propose MDLMPE, a positional encoding designed specifically for masked diffusion. To the best of our knowledge, MDLMPE is the first method to make positional representations explicitly aware of the changing revealed/masked configuration. It represents token availability as a binary sequence, applies distance-aware Gaussian weighting, and projects the resulting pattern through a cosine basis to obtain distribution-aware positional features. These features are added to token embeddings and mapped by a lightweight MLP to angular offsets that modulate the standard RoPE phases. Extensive experiments on LLaDA and DREAM demonstrate that MDLMPE generally outperforms conventional positional encoding methods across supervised fine-tuning, pretraining, zero-shot evaluation, and block-diffusion settings. Further ablations show that the complete combination of availability state, Gaussian locality, spectral basis, and embedding injection yields the strongest result. These results establish the evolving token-availability distribution as a useful positional signal for masked diffusion language models.

</details>


### [71] [Efficient Knowledge Distillation for LLMs: Offline Top-K Logits and a Fused Chunked KL Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03796)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一项面向实践者的蒸馏训练效率研究。作者提出两个系统性贡献：(1) 离线KD（缓存教师模型top-K logits）在训练损失上与在线蒸馏几乎一致，但省去教师模型内存占用，单次迭代快约29%，在单张H200 GPU上吞吐量最高提升41%；(2) 引入融合的分块KL损失（fused chunked KL loss），避免物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值内存与序列长度呈线性关系，从而在单GPU上将上下文长度扩展4倍（32,768 tokens）。作者还报告了损失设计和序列打包的消融实验，并开源了实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型因延迟、成本和本地部署约束常是唯一选择，但很少从头训练，通常通过知识蒸馏（KD）从压缩模型中恢复。这个恢复步骤很大程度上决定最终质量，但代价昂贵。作者希望提升蒸馏训练的效率和可扩展性，使大规模修复（healing）和大量消融实验变得负担得起。

Method: (1) 离线KD方法：一次性缓存教师模型的top-K logits，让学生仅对缓存进行训练，移除教师在训练过程中的内存与计算开销；(2) 融合分块KL损失：设计不物化完整词表大小logit张量的损失计算，使峰值内存仅与序列长度线性相关；(3) 构建输出头独立的toy benchmark来隔离评估损失核的显存和迭代速率扩展性；(4) 进行损失设计和序列打包相关的消融实验。

Result: 离线KD与在线蒸馏在训练损失上几乎一致，但迭代速度快约29%，单张H200 GPU吞吐量最高提升41%；分块KL损失消除了内存尖峰，使上下文长度可扩大4倍（32,768 tokens）在单GPU上训练；toy benchmark验证了从4K到256K tokens的显存与迭代速率扩展性；所有改进使大规模修复和数百次消融实验变得可行。

Conclusion: 通过离线KD缓存策略和融合分块KL损失的结合，蒸馏训练可以在不损失质量的前提下大幅提升效率和吞吐量，并突破上下文长度限制。这些系统层面的优化使得小模型蒸馏训练在单一消费级/数据中心GPU上也变得高效可行。

Abstract: Small language models are often the only option for deployment under tight latency, cost, and on-premises constraints, but they are rarely trained from scratch: a compressed model is usually recovered through knowledge distillation (KD). This recovery step largely decides the final quality, yet it is expensive. We present a practitioner's study of how to make distillation training efficient, organised around two systems contributions. First, we show that offline KD (caching the teacher's top-$K$ logits once and training the student against the cache) matches online distillation at near-identical training loss while removing the teacher from memory, running about 29\% faster per iteration, and reaching up to 41\% higher throughput on a single H200 GPU. Second, we introduce a \emph{fused, chunked KL loss} that never materialises the full vocabulary-sized logit tensor, making peak memory linear in the sequence length. This removes the memory spike that otherwise caps context length and lets us train at four times the context (32{,}768 tokens) on a single GPU. A separate output-head-only toy benchmark isolates the loss kernel and confirms its memory and iteration-rate scaling from 4K to 256K tokens. Together these make large-scale healing and hundreds of ablations affordable. We also report supporting ablations on loss design and sequence packing. We release our chunked-loss implementation: https://github.com/CompactifAI/Full-Chunked-KL-Loss.

</details>


### [72] [M-GATE: Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03803)
*Tomáš Burkert,Angelika Peljak-Łapińska,David Zelený*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: M-GATE是一个覆盖30种类型多样语言的 linguistic proficiency 基准，包含语法错误检测、往返翻译和分词效率三个任务。在50+模型的评估中发现流畅性（fluency）与精通度（proficiency）显著分离：模型翻译表现尚可但在对抗性语法项上接近随机水平（最佳MCC仅0.36），且倾向于漏报而非误报；翻译质量与预训练数据份额高度相关（r=0.86），低资源惩罚虽在缩小但仍陡峭；推理能力能稳定提升翻译但对话法错误检测影响较小甚至为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言基准大多测试模型"用某种语言执行任务"的能力，混淆了语言流畅性与真正的语言掌控力（proficiency）。作者希望构建一个真正衡量模型语言能力本身的基准，以区分"能翻译"和"真正掌握这门语言"。

Method: 构建M-GATE基准，包含三个任务：(1) 语法错误检测，使用语言学家手工构建、对抗性挑选的句子，针对难且语言特异的语法现象；(2) 往返翻译，将共享英文源翻译成29种目标语言，由三个LLM评审组评分并与专业标注者验证；(3) 分词效率补充指标。在50多个模型、80多种配置上评测。

Result: 流畅性与精通度显著分离：模型翻译称职但在对抗性语法项上接近随机，最佳MCC仅0.36，且系统性偏向漏报（接受不合语法文本）。翻译质量与语言在预训练数据中的份额密切相关（r=0.86 vs log Common Crawl份额），低资源惩罚陡峭但随模型发布逐渐收窄。启用推理稳定提升翻译，但对错误检测影响较小甚至部分模型为负。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了多语言模型的流畅性（翻译能力）与语言精通度（语法掌握）之间的实质差距，强调任务依赖性配置选择（推理开启与否因任务而异），并指出低资源语言仍面临显著挑战。为防止数据污染，测试项保持私有并配以持续更新的公开leaderboard。

Abstract: Multilingual language models are deployed across a hundred or more languages, yet most benchmarks test whether a model can perform a task _in_ a language rather than whether it commands the language itself, conflating fluency with proficiency. We introduce M-GATE (Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency), a benchmark of linguistic proficiency spanning 30 typologically diverse languages from high- to low-resource. M-GATE comprises three tasks: grammatical error detection on linguist-crafted, adversarially selected sentences that turn on hard, language-specific phenomena; round-trip translation of shared English sources across 29 target languages, scored by a three-provider LLM judge panel validated against professional annotators; and a supplementary tokenizer-efficiency measure. We evaluate over 50 models in more than 80 configurations. Fluency and proficiency come apart sharply: models that translate competently sit near chance on the adversarial grammar items, the best reaching a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of only 0.36, and their errors lean systematically toward under-flagging, accepting ungrammatical text rather than raising false alarms. Translation quality closely tracks a language's share of pretraining data (r = 0.86 against log Common Crawl share), producing a steep low-resource penalty that is nonetheless narrowing with successive model releases. Enabling reasoning reliably improves translation, while its effect on error detection is smaller and for some models negative, so the best configuration is task-dependent. To resist contamination, test items are kept private behind a continuously updated public leaderboard, with illustrative examples released (https://m-gate.ai).

</details>


### [73] [VIBE: A VAD-Informed Benchmark for Entity-Centered Affective Profiling of Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03810)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Mikhail Solovev,Danil Sazanakov,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VIBE基准，用于在Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD)空间中开展实体中心的LLM输出情感画像评测，其核心是一个测量契约，将生成与外部打分分离，区分标量好感度、响应级VAD和目标定向VAD，并通过情感护照报告画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在描述社会显著目标（政治人物、国家、宗教、组织、历史事件及社会群体）时，会在事实内容之外编码情感框架，使目标呈现为有利或威胁、平静或冲突、强大或脆弱。现有工作通过情感、好感度和情绪基准仅捕获了该空间的部分维度，缺乏同时结合目标定向VAD归因、显式打分契约和护照报告格式的统一方案。

Method: VIBE引入核心测量契约：将生成与外部打分分离；区分标量好感度、响应级VAD与目标定向VAD三种不同的测量维度；通过情感护照报告画像。并设置三层实证验证：H1验证标量好感度不能包含唤醒度和支配度；H2验证整响应与目标定向VAD是不同契约；H3为协议漂移诊断，考察诱发条件对画像的影响。

Result: H1表明效价发现可交叉验证（rV=0.944评分者-人类，rV=0.954跨评分者），而唤醒度和支配度仅为单评分者的方向性估计而非精确值（人类标注者rA=0.495, rD=0.702）；H2显示同一文本的整体情感基调与命名目标的情感画像可以不同；H3说明诱发条件会改变画像，需要在情感报告中包含上下文元数据。

Conclusion: 这些结果论证了实体中心情感画像应作为有文档记录的操作实践：画像发布时须附上评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议及解释限制等信息。

Abstract: Large language models routinely describe socially salient targets, including political figures, countries, religions, organizations, historical events, and social groups, encoding affective framing alongside factual content: a target may appear favorable or threatening, calm or conflictual, powerful or vulnerable. Existing work captures parts of this space through sentiment, favorability, and emotion benchmarks, but none combines target-directed VAD attribution, an explicit scorer contract, and a passport reporting format. We introduce VIBE, a benchmark for entity-centered affective profiling of LLM outputs in Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) space. Its core contribution is a measurement contract: VIBE separates generation from external scoring, distinguishes scalar favorability, response-level VAD, and target-directed VAD, and reports profiles through an Affective Passport. Three empirical layers support the contract. H1 shows scalar favorability does not subsume arousal and dominance: valence findings are cross-validated (rV = 0.944 judge-human, rV = 0.954 inter-scorer); arousal and dominance are single-scorer directional estimates, not point-precise, consistent with known inter-annotator difficulty on these axes (rA = 0.495, rD = 0.702 among human annotators). H2 shows whole-response and target-directed VAD are different contracts: the same text can carry one affective tone overall while representing the named target differently. H3 is a protocol-drift diagnostic: elicitation conditions shift profiles, motivating context metadata in every affective report. These results motivate entity-centered affective profiling as a documented practice: profiles should be released with scorer identity, coverage, protocol, and interpretation limits.

</details>


### [74] [Sensitivity, Causality, and Repair Dissociate: A Layer-Wise Analysis of Perturbation Robustness and Its Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03842)
*Nathan Labiosa,David Buff,Ena Nayak,Erica Donno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在面对表面扰动输入（拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）时，"哪个层负责"这一问题的三种操作化定义——敏感性（表征发散最剧烈处）、因果性（恢复干净激活可恢复预测处）和补偿能力（小型适配器可修复损伤处）——并证明这三张层图相互分离。在五模型面板中发现两种传播模式（尖峰-抑制与晚期累积），并且在满足80%恒等补丁门槛的两个模型上，敏感性与因果性呈反相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）。Qwen2.5家族（1.5B至14B）的缩放实验显示晚期累积特征随规模单调增强，并在第二个家族中得到佐证。作者提出级联破坏作为这一分离的机制：放置在因果相关早期层的适配器会破坏完好的下游计算，使诊断标记位置成为最差的适配器放置点。在四个模型（3.8-8B）上的固定装置层扫描通过链式思维GSM8K证实了核心预测——标记位置在每一个可判定模型上都是最有害的适配器窗口——在多项选择对照上符号一致但强度大幅衰减，与损伤随生成长度累积的机制一致。该扫描还提供实用指导：无训练的LRD预筛和默认最深放置规则，尽管相对于无适配器基线的绝对增益仍较小。最后，表征稳定性损失带来的表面增益在充足生成预算下发生逆转——被截断的链式思维曾被评分空——这是对任何在链式思维任务上评估干预方法的方法论警告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当大语言模型面对表面扰动输入（如拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）而失败时，研究者希望定位"哪个层出了问题"。然而论文指出，这个看似简单的问题有三种不同的操作化定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力），而现有文献往往混用这些概念。作者的目标是厘清这三种层定位方案是否一致，并探究它们背后的机制以及实际应用中的适配器放置策略。

Method: 论文在五模型面板（Phi-3.5、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5-7B）上，分别用三种方式定位故障层：(1) 敏感性——测量干净与扰动输入下表征发散的层分布；(2) 因果性——恢复各层干净激活能否恢复预测；(3) 补偿能力——在各层放置小适配器修复损伤的效果。结合家族内缩放实验（Qwen2.5 1.5B-14B）、跨家族验证，以及四模型固定装置的层扫描（链式思维GSM8K与多项选择对照），验证级联破坏机制假说，并检验训练方法（表征稳定性损失）的实际效果。

Result: 三种层定位方法相互分离而非一致。模型呈现两种传播模式：尖峰-抑制型（Phi-3.5、Gemma-2-9B）与晚期累积型（Llama-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5-7B）。满足门槛的两个模型中，敏感性与因果性显著反相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）。家族缩放显示晚期累积特征随规模单调增强。级联破坏机制得到验证：因果标记的早期层是最差的适配器窗口。实用指导包括无训练LRD预筛和默认最深放置规则，但绝对增益有限。表征稳定性损失的表面收益在充足生成预算下反转。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了"哪层负责"这一诊断问题没有单一答案——敏感性、因果性与补偿能力三者系统性分离，且存在两种模型架构的传播模式。级联破坏解释了为何直觉上的"因果修复位点"实际上是最差的适配器位置。研究提供了实用的适配器放置策略（LRD预筛与默认最深规则），并对链式思维任务上的干预评估方法提出了方法论警告：生成预算不足会导致虚假结论。

Abstract: When a language model fails on surface-perturbed input (typos, OCR noise, homophones), "which layer is responsible" has three natural operationalizations: where representations diverge most (sensitivity), where restoring clean activations recovers the prediction (causality), and where a small adapter can repair the damage (compensatory capacity) - and we show these three layer maps dissociate. Across a five-model panel we identify two propagation regimes - spike-and-suppress (Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B) and late-accumulation (Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B) - and on the two models meeting an 80% identity-patch gate, sensitivity and causality are anti-correlated (rho = -0.72 to -0.88). Within-family scaling on Qwen2.5 (1.5B to 14B) shows the late-accumulation signature strengthening monotonically with scale, corroborated on a second family. We propose cascade disruption as the mechanism behind the dissociation: adapters placed at causally implicated early layers break intact downstream computation, making diagnostic-flagged sites the worst adapter placements. A fixed-harness layer sweep across four models (3.8-8B) confirms the core prediction on chain-of-thought GSM8K - the flagged sites are the most damaging adapter windows on every adjudicable model - and is sign-consistent but strongly attenuated on a multiple-choice control, consistent with damage that compounds with generation length. The sweep yields practical guidance: a training-free LRD pre-screen and a default-deepest placement rule, though absolute gains over no-adapter baselines remain small. Finally, apparent gains from a representation-stability loss reverse under an adequate generation budget - truncated chain-of-thought had been scored as empty - a methodological warning for any intervention evaluated on chain-of-thought tasks.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Representational Similarity: Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain for Generative Plagiarism Detection and Candidate Source Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03859)
*Peijia Guo,Wenxuan Xie,ZiGuang Li,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCDG（源条件描述长度增益），一种无需训练的定向框架，用于检测生成式抄袭（生成式文本重写后的源复用）。通过对比可疑文档在有无候选源条件下的冻结语言模型描述长度差异，获得token级对数似然增益以衡量源提供的增量预测证据。在PAN基准上超越所有基线，实现高精度和高召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对学术诚信和同行评审构成挑战，但生成式抄袭检测仍是一个未被充分探索且基本未解决的难题。已有工作关注AI参与度检测（而非源复用），基于相似度的方法在大量改写和多源综合后失效。需要一种能检测源复用且在文本被广泛改写后依然有效的框架。

Method: SCDG基于概率预测的描述长度视角：相关边信息可缩短目标序列的编码长度。该框架对比冻结语言模型在有无候选源S条件下对可疑文档P的描述长度差异，计算token级对数似然增益，以此衡量源S对P提供的增量预测证据。这是一个方向性（源定向）、无需训练（training-free）的方法。

Result: 在PAN 2025派生的成对基准上，SCDG达到0.92精确率、0.97召回率和0.94 F1，优于所有基线；在PAN 2026多源检索任务上达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100，优于所有基线。在同类主题、同事件Multi-News测试中，校准后的增益分布SCDG分类器仅对0.125%的配对预测源复用，验证了对主题重叠的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDG被确立为一种统一且可token分解的信号，能够在广泛改写下检测特定源的文本内容复用，为生成式抄袭检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) pose challenges to academic integrity and peer review. Yet generative plagiarism detection remains an underexplored and largely unresolved challenge. Prior work on LLM-generated-text detection targets AI involvement, which may be permissible, rather than source reuse, while similarity-based methods struggle after extensive rewriting and multi-source synthesis. Motivated by the description-length view of probabilistic prediction, in which relevant side information can reduce a target sequence's code length, we introduce Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain (SCDG), a directional, training-free framework that contrasts a frozen language model's description length of a suspicious document $P$ with and without a candidate source $S$. This contrast yields token-level log-likelihood gains that measure the incremental predictive evidence supplied by $S$. We evaluate SCDG on the PAN at CLEF benchmarks for generative plagiarism. On a PAN 2025-derived pairwise benchmark, SCDG achieves 0.92 Precision, 0.97 Recall, and 0.94 F1, outperforming all baselines; on PAN 2026's multi-source retrieval task, it reaches 0.83 nDCG@10 and 0.96 Recall@100, surpassing all baselines. On a same-topic, same-event Multi-News test, the calibrated gain-distribution SCDG classifier predicts source reuse for only $0.125\%$ of pairs, supporting robustness to topical overlap under this evaluation protocol. These results establish SCDG as a unified and token-decomposable signal for source-specific content reuse under extensive transformation.

</details>


### [76] [SciRet: A Compute-Aware Empirical Study of Retrieval and Reranking for Scientific RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03860)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Rofiqul Alam Shehab,Asab Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SciRet，一个面向CORD-19科学问答的检索增强生成（RAG）计算感知实证研究。研究在1K、5K、15K三种语料规模下评估固定科学RAG流水线（句窗分块、BM25、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合、可选交叉编码器重排序、接地答案生成），发现混合检索比单一检索更稳健（Recall@10在1K和15K达到1.000），MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器重排器因领域不匹配降低精度，生成忠实度随语料规模增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG研究往往聚焦提出新模型，缺乏对计算资源（语料规模）如何影响RAG各组件性能的系统性实证理解，尤其是在科学问答场景中。

Method: 采用固定科学RAG流水线，在三种语料规模（1,034/5,160/15,480个chunk）下进行控制变量实验。流水线包含句窗分块、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合（RRF）、可选cross-encoder重排序和接地答案生成。使用RAGAS评估生成忠实度，用混合系统的伪相关性标签进行检索评估。

Result: 1）混合检索在所有规模下优于单一稀疏或稠密检索，Recall@10在1K和15K达到1.000；2）MS MARCO训练的cross-encoder重排器在科学语料上降低精度，说明领域不匹配的负面影响超过更强交互建模的收益；3）RAGAS度量的生成忠实度随语料规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: SciRet提供了可复现的、计算感知的RAG实证基线，揭示语料规模、混合检索策略和领域匹配对科学问答RAG性能的关键影响。由于使用伪相关性标签，结果定位为受控比较证据而非基准声明，并开放代码、索引和评估输出以支持后续研究。

Abstract: We introduce SciRet, a compute-aware empirical study of retrieval-augmented generation for scientific question answering over CORD-19. Rather than proposing a new model, we evaluate a fixed scientific RAG pipeline across three corpus scales: 1,034 chunks (1K papers), 5,160 chunks (5K papers), and 15,480 chunks (15K papers). The pipeline combines sentence-window chunking, BM25, BGE-M3 dense retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion, optional cross-encoder reranking, and grounded answer generation. Across these settings, hybrid retrieval is more robust than either sparse-only or dense-only retrieval in our setting, reaching Recall@10 of 1.000 at 1K and 15K. In contrast, an MS MARCO-trained cross-encoder reranker reduces precision on the scientific corpus, suggesting that domain mismatch can outweigh the benefits of stronger query-passage interaction. Generation faithfulness measured with RAGAS increases with corpus scale in our setup. Retrieval evaluation uses pseudo-relevance labels derived from the hybrid system, so we treat the results as controlled comparative evidence rather than a benchmark claim. We release code, indexes, and evaluation outputs to support replication and follow-up studies.

</details>


### [77] [MultiGlobeQA: A Multilingual and Globally Diverse Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03882)
*Martin Böckling,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiGlobeQA，一个包含46,060对问答、覆盖14种空间函数家族和15种答案格式的多语言地理空间推理基准，基于三个知识图谱执行验证生成ground truth，覆盖201个国家/地区并以收入与密度分层抽样，包含英语及16种高低资源语言。实验发现LLMs在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现崩溃，检索与工具使用虽有提升但即使提供黄金事实性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算能力而非知识获取是瓶颈；模型在低收入地区表现更差且黄金事实反而扩大了这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间推理基准存在不足：合成或小规模、大多单语、对地理覆盖控制有限，无法准确定位LLMs在几何与拓扑计算上的失败模式。需要构建更大规模、多语言、可控制地理覆盖的基准来系统评估和诊断LLMs的地理空间推理能力。

Method: 构建MultiGlobeQA多语言基准：包含46,060个问答对，覆盖14种空间函数家族和15种答案格式；基于三个知识图谱以执行方式生成ground truth；采用收入与密度分层抽样覆盖201个国家和地区；提供英语并行版本及16种高低资源语言版本。在参数化、推理和智能体三种设置下系统评估LLMs表现。

Result: LLMs在需要网格索引和形状计算的任务上表现崩溃，而拓扑关系和方向类任务表现最好。检索和工具使用带来显著提升，但即使在提供黄金事实的情况下性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算瓶颈而非知识瓶颈。模型在低收入地区表现不佳，且提供黄金事实反而扩大了这一差距。

Conclusion: LLMs地理空间推理的核心瓶颈在于几何与拓扑计算能力本身，而非地理知识获取；现有模型在处理复杂空间计算时存在根本性局限，对低收入地区的空间推理能力差距需要通过计算能力改进而非简单提供知识来解决。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning, i.e., computing distances, containment, and other spatial relations over real-world entities, is central to navigation and logistics, yet large language models (LLMs) struggle with the required geometric and topological computation despite storing considerable geographic knowledge. Existing benchmarks localize these failures only partially: they are synthetic or smallscale, largely monolingual, and offer limited control over geographic coverage. We introduce MultiGlobeQA, a multilingual benchmark of 46,060 question-answer pairs spanning 14 spatial-function families and 15 answer formats, with execution-based ground truth over three knowledge graphs. It covers 201 countries and territories via income- and density-stratified sampling, with parallel questions in English and 16 additional high- and low-resource languages. Across parametric, reasoning, and agentic settings, LLMs collapse on tasks requiring grid indexing and shape computation, while topological relations and directions fare best. Retrieval and tool use yield considerable gains, yet performance plateaus below two thirds even when gold facts are supplied, indicating that computation, not access to knowledge, is the bottleneck. Models also underperform on low-income regions, a gap that gold facts widen rather than close.

</details>


### [78] [DS@GT-ARC at eRisk 2026 Task 3: Sparse, Semantic, and LLM Reranking for ADHD Symptom Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03883)
*David Guecha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了团队在eRisk 2026任务3（ADHD症状句排序）中的提交方案。由于任务未提供标注训练数据，团队采用零样本实验、人工验证及无监督/弱引导检索管线，结合BM25稀疏检索、证据感知重打分、嵌入重排序、查询原型扩展和基于LLM的重排序，最终LLM重排序器取得最佳官方成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eRisk 2026任务3要求系统根据成人ADHD自我报告量表（ASRS-v1.1）中的18个症状，对Reddit候选句子进行相关性排序。由于这是该任务的首届，未发布人工标注的训练数据，因此需要在零样本条件下构建有效的检索和排序系统。

Method: 提出分阶段检索架构：首先使用BM25在大规模候选中进行稀疏检索，随后采用多级精化策略，包括（1）针对自我指涉症状报告的证据感知重打分，（2）基于嵌入向量的重排序，（3）查询原型扩展，以及（4）基于大型语言模型（LLM）的重排序。所有提交的系统均遵循BM25检索+语义/LLM重排序的分阶段设计。

Result: 在所有提交系统中，LLM重排序器获得最强的官方评分，其次是原型查询扩展方案。人工对top-10结果的分析与官方专家评分趋势一致。

Conclusion: 分阶段重排序（staged reranking）是ADHD症状句排序任务中有前景的发展方向，语义检索与LLM精化的组合能够在零样本条件下有效工作，值得进一步开发。

Abstract: This paper describes our submissions to eRisk 2026 Task 3, ADHD Symptom Sentence Ranking. The task requires systems to rank candidate Reddit sentences according to their relevance to each of the 18 symptoms in the Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS-v1.1). Because no annotated training data were released for this first edition of the task, we relied on zero-shot experimentation, manual validation, and unsupervised or weakly guided retrieval pipelines. Our systems combine sparse BM25 retrieval, evidence-aware rescoring for self-referential symptom reports, embedding-based reranking, query-prototype expansion, and LLM-based reranking. All submitted systems follow a staged retrieval design in which BM25 retrieves candidates at scale and semantic or LLM rerankers refine the final rankings. Among our submissions, the LLM reranker achieved the strongest official scores, followed by the prototype query-expansion run. Our manual top-10 analysis aligned with the official expert scoring trend, suggesting that staged reranking is a promising direction for further development.

</details>


### [79] [ANNOTARES: A Dataset for Extracting Logical Structures from German Statutory Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03898)
*Ronja Schwarz,Jannik Strötgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了德国法律文本中法律要件（Tatbestand）与法律效果（Rechtsfolge）的自动识别与切分任务，并发布了ANNOTARES数据集（包含三个法律法典的span级标注），系统比较了从规则基线到CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、BERT变体及LLM方法等多种架构，实验表明BERT和基于LLM的模型在捕捉法律语言复杂句法结构方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本的结构化自动分析是法律技术的基础，但提取其逻辑成分（法律要件与法律效果）仍面临重大挑战。该工作旨在填补德语成文法中法律要件与法律后果自动抽取这一任务领域的空白。

Method: 作者提出了法律条件（Tatbestand）与法律后果（Rechtsfolge）的识别与切分任务；构建了ANNOTARES数据集，包含三个不同德语法律法典的span级标注，用以评估领域内表现与跨法典泛化能力；并系统性地基准测试了规则基线、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF以及基于Transformer的模型（BERT变体和LLM方法）等多种架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，BERT和基于LLM的模型在捕捉法律语言复杂句法结构方面取得了最优性能，超越了传统的机器学习和规则方法。

Conclusion: 作者公开了ANNOTARES数据集以促进自动化法律推理领域的后续研究，并确认了基于Transformer的预训练模型在处理法律文本结构化任务上的优势。

Abstract: The automatic structural analysis of legal texts is a cornerstone of legal technology, yet the extraction of their logical components remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce the task of identifying and segmenting legal conditions (Tatbestand) and legal consequences (Rechtsfolge) within German statutory texts. To support this task, we present ANNOTARES (Annotations of Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge Sequences), a novel dataset comprising German law texts with span-level annotations. Spanning three distinct legal codes, the dataset is designed to evaluate both domain-specific performance and cross-statute generalizability. We benchmark diverse architectural approaches: a rule-based baseline, CRFs, BiLSTMs, BiLSTM-CRF, and modern Transformer-based models, including BERT variants and LLM-based methods. Our results demonstrate that BERT and LLM-based models achieve superior performance in capturing the complex syntactic structures of legal language. We release our dataset to facilitate further research in automated legal reasoning.

</details>


### [80] [Logic Before Language: Pre-pretraining on Formal Derivations Fosters Skill Acquisition and Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03930)
*Jo-Ku Cheng,Nikolaos Aletras,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT）方法，利用形式化推导作为语言模型的初始化策略，在100B token规模下显著加速语言技能习得，实现以更少token达到同等的语言任务精度，并揭示其内部表征呈现低秩、谱集中的几何特性，从而支持更高的模型压缩度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预预训练任务（如Dyck语言和程序化算法）依赖过于狭窄的原语，无法捕捉自然语言的表达能力；且以往研究局限于较小token预算，难以观察到技能涌现和表征动态。作者希望设计一种更富结构偏置的初始化策略，并扩大评估规模以更深入地理解预预训练的影响。

Method: 提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT），基于形式化推导（formal derivations）作为预预训练数据，这些推导需要同时进行变量绑定、量词与关系依赖的连接以及跨长上下文的谓词-论元结构组合，从而赋予模型更接近自然语言的结构性和语言性偏置。将评估规模扩展至100B token

Result: 在100B token规模下，逻辑预预训练显著加速了LM技能习得：在语言任务上以比标准初始化少36B token达到80%准确率，优于其他预预训练基线。机制层面，形式化推导促使模型产生持续的结构性重组，表征空间呈现低秩、谱集中的特征。此外，这种内部几何结构使模型可通过剪枝获得更好的可压缩性，在约33%稀疏度下仍能匹配稠密基线的性能。

Conclusion: 逻辑预预训练是一种有效的初始化策略，能够通过形式化推导注入丰富的结构偏置，不仅加速语言技能习得，还赋予模型更利于压缩的内部几何结构，为预预训练范式的设计与理解提供了新视角。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining language models (LMs) on symbolic data can accelerate and improve natural language acquisition. However, existing pre-pretraining tasks, such as Dyck and procedural algorithms, rely on narrow primitives that fail to capture the expressive capacity of natural language. Moreover, prior studies remain restricted to relatively small token budgets, offering limited insight into skill emergence and representational dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose logic pre-pretraining (Logic-PPT) as a principled initialization strategy, leveraging formal derivations to impart richer structural and linguistic biases. Formal derivations require abstract mechanisms that are central to natural language, simultaneously binding variables, connecting quantifiers and relational dependencies, and composing predicate-argument structures over long contexts. Scaling our evaluation to a 100B-token regime, logic pre-pretraining substantially accelerates skill acquisition in LMs, achieving 80\% accuracy on linguistic tasks with 36B fewer tokens than standard initialization, and outperforming alternative pre-pretraining baselines. Mechanistically, formal derivations induce persistent structural reorganization, distinctively characterized by a lower-rank, spectrally concentrated representation space. Crucially, we show that this internal geometry enables improved model compressibility via pruning, matching the dense baseline performance even at $\approx$33\% sparsity.

</details>


### [81] [HalluTruthQA-4K: A Fine-Grained Corpus and Annotation Process for Arabic Hallucination Detection and Truth Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03966)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Hichem Telli,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HalluTruthQA-4K，一个包含4,000条专家策展的阿拉伯语问答实例的幻觉检测数据集，涵盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个知识密集型领域。该数据集为每条幻觉响应提供字符级错误片段、人工解释和分层错误类型标注，作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的阿拉伯语回答虽流畅但常包含难以识别和验证的事实错误。现有的阿拉伯语幻觉资源仅对整个回答分配二元标签（幻觉或非幻觉），无法提供有关确切错误内容、错误原因或正确事实答案的详细信息，迫切需要更细粒度的标注资源。

Method: HalluTruthQA-4K将原有语料扩展到4,000个实例，每个实例包含阿拉伯语问题、模型生成回答、经过验证的参考答案和五个干扰选项。幻觉回答额外标注了字符级错误片段、人工编写的解释和分层幻觉类型。构建过程包括问题选择、受控回答生成、候选构建、专家标注、独立验证、仲裁和质量控制，并记录了标注指南、分类法、数据格式、标注者间一致性及语料统计。

Result: 语料包含1,643条幻觉回答和2,357条非幻觉回答，共标注了1,843个错误片段。数据集覆盖四个知识密集型领域，为幻觉检测、片段级错误定位、解释生成和事实验证提供资源。

Conclusion: HalluTruthQA-4K作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集，为阿拉伯语大模型幻觉检测与事实可靠性评估提供了可复用的细粒度标注资源，推动了该领域的标准化评估。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent Arabic answers while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify and verify. Existing Arabic hallucination resources often assign a binary label to an entire response, indicating whether it is hallucinated or non-hallucinated, but provide limited information about the exact erroneous content, the reason for the error, or the correct factual answer. We present HalluTruthQA-4K, an expanded version of the HalluTruthQA resource containing 4,000 expert-curated Arabic question-answering instances across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Serving as the official dataset for Track 2 of the HalluScoring 2026 shared task, HalluTruthQA-4K extends our original corpus to 4,000 instances. Each instance pairs an Arabic question with a model-generated response, a verified reference answer, and five plausible distractors. Hallucinated responses are additionally annotated with character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and hierarchical hallucination types. The corpus contains 1,643 hallucinated and 2,357 non-hallucinated responses, with 1,843 annotated erroneous spans. We describe the resource construction and annotation methodology, including question selection, controlled answer generation, candidate construction, expert annotation, independent verification, adjudication, and quality control. We also document the annotation guidelines, taxonomy, data format, inter-annotator agreement, and corpus statistics. HalluTruthQA-4K provides a reusable resource for hallucination detection, span-level error localization, explanation generation, factual verification, and the broader evaluation of factual reliability in Arabic language models.

</details>


### [82] [string2string Studio: An Interactive, In-Browser Platform for String-to-String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03984)
*Mirac Suzgun,James Zou,Stuart M. Shieber,Dan Jurafsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了string2string Studio，一个基于浏览器交互式字符串到字符串分析平台，涵盖自然语言处理、计算生物学和数字人文领域，支持对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标和BLAST同源搜索六大模块。基于C++算法编译为WebAssembly，核心操作默认本地运行，无需安装和数据上传。基准测试显示相比Python版本最高提速2500倍，并提供证据可视化使方法可检查、可调试、可比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字符串分析工具往往存在性能瓶颈、方法黑箱不可检查、跨领域工具碎片化等问题，缺乏一个统一、交互式、可在浏览器本地运行且能可视化证据（对齐路径、编辑轨迹、同源追踪等）的平台。

Method: 将C++算法编译为WebAssembly在浏览器中本地运行，构建六大功能模块（字符/词/词元/行/残基级别），通过展示评分证据（对齐、编辑路径、度量匹配、搜索命中、同源痕迹）实现方法的可检查性和可调试性，并包含Learn模式和策展案例库。

Result: 内部基准测试显示相比Python前身最高提速2500倍，全局/局部对齐速度快于通用原生C对齐器，在声明设置下与独立参考精确一致；scope内的客户端blastn路径在匹配参数下与NCBI BLAST+的排名和统计紧密匹配。

Conclusion: string2string Studio是一个开源、免费、无需安装的交互式字符串分析平台，通过WebAssembly实现高性能本地执行和证据可视化的可解释性，适用于多学科用户。

Abstract: We present string2string Studio, an interactive in-browser platform for string-to-string analysis across natural language processing, computational biology, and the digital humanities. The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [83] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示ALiBi位置编码的一个被忽视的失败模式：线性偏置在浮点精度下发生下溢，导致大量注意力权重归零，使相关注意力头"部分失明"。文章分析了该问题的成因与影响，提出四种训练期缓解策略，并通过1.48亿参数的预训练实验验证了其对token检索的损害及对标准基准的轻微影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALiBi（Attention with Linear Biases）位置编码被广泛使用，但其线性偏置缩放存在浮点精度下溢问题，会导致大量注意力权重被置零，使部分注意力头失效。这一问题此前未被充分认识，且可能影响模型的token检索能力，因此需要系统分析并提出缓解方案。

Method: 本文首先分析ALiBi线性偏置在浮点数（如bfloat16/float16）下发生下溢的机制，表征其影响范围。随后在148M参数的decoder模型上进行全面的预训练实验，将ALiBi失效效应与超出上下文的退化效应分离。提出四种训练期缓解策略（包括log缩放距离等），并分别及组合评估这些策略，重点考察passkey检索与needle-in-a-haystack等任务的表现。

Result: 实验发现ALiBi的失败模式会显著损害token检索能力，但对标准decoder基准的影响轻微。在所提出的四种缓解策略中，log缩放距离方法在passkey检索上取得最一致的改进。尽管存在此问题，默认ALiBi斜率在needle-in-a-haystack检索任务中仍是令人惊讶的强基线。

Conclusion: 基于以上发现，文章为使用ALiBi训练模型提供了具体建议，包括如何避免浮点下溢导致的注意力头失明问题，以及在实际部署中选择合适的缓解策略。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [84] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAST-Bench，一个用于系统评估个人 AI 智能体是否通过保留经验实现递归自我改进的基准测试，并基于该基准开发了 Hermes+ 框架，通过五个针对性干预提升智能体从保留经验中获益的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进要求智能体将积累的经验转化为未来更好的行为，个人 AI 智能体是研究这一能力的理想场景（因其保留偏好、任务历史、工具惯例和习得技能）。然而，保留的经验是否真正随时间提升智能体性能从未被系统测试过。

Method: 构建 PAST-Bench 基准：让每个智能体在匹配条件下运行有序的新会话任务序列，通过开关保留经验来隔离其影响。基准涵盖 26 个场景和 204 个episode，覆盖记忆、程序复用、信息收集和更新四类能力。同时报告后期任务增益及增益是否遵循预期的保存、检索、更新路径。基于发现，开发 Hermes+，在智能体循环的各个阶段加入五个针对性干预措施。

Result: 在七个基础模型和四个智能体框架上，改进是真实的但不均衡。具有相同显著增益的智能体在增益是否由预期路径证据支持方面可能差异显著。Hermes+ 提高了保留经验的平均增益并提供更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上改进最强，但效果仍依赖能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench 与 Hermes+ 共同为研究持久智能体如何从保留经验走向系统性改进提供了评估和诊断基础，有助于推动递归自我改进领域的研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [85] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TurnSight提出了一种基于回合级后见之明的自蒸馏框架，通过跨水平方向一致性选择可靠监督信号，并用于自适应调制强化学习优势，解决了工具集成推理中长程轨迹的细粒度信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成推理的强化学习方法依赖轨迹级监督，难以在长程TIR场景中进行细粒度信用分配。虽然在线策略自蒸馏可通过带特权上下文的教师分支提供更密集的信号，但现有方法通常从真实答案或检索技能中获取上下文，不能反映智能体实际访问的状态；且token级监督无法捕捉工具交互的回合级结构。

Method: TurnSight框架包含三个关键步骤：(1)从执行条件后见之明中直接推导回合级监督信号；(2)构建多个不同前瞻视野的后见视图，通过跨视野方向一致性筛选可靠监督；(3)将选中的后见信号在兄弟rollout之间进行归一化，用于自适应调节RL优势值，同时保留其原始优化方向。

Result: 在三个基准上的大量实验证明了TurnSight的有效性。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过回合级后见自蒸馏与跨视野方向一致性筛选，在保留优化方向的同时提供了更细粒度的监督信号，为长程TIR场景中的强化学习提供了一种有效的信用分配方案。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [86] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一个前瞻性（非回顾性）的大语言模型预测能力基准测试：在2026年FIFA世界杯的39天内，六个前沿LLM在每场比赛开赛前对全部104场比赛及12个小组头名和赛前冠军池进行共7个市场的预测，共产生4,494条评分预测。由于提问时答案尚不存在，评测天然无泄漏。结果显示：系统在比赛结果上平均准确率63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门持平（其行为也正如此）；系统间互相一致远高于各自正确率，多数投票无增益；系统欠押平局和进球，比分预测过度集中于单一原型结果；预测准确率与比赛悬殊程度正相关而非与信息量正相关；各系统间差异不大，排名首尾稳定、中间波动，差距始终很小。论文作为基准发布了简报档案、赛程和官方结果及评分代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有衡量LLM预测能力的基准几乎都是回顾式的：事件已发生、答案已在网络某处可查，评测必须费力防御记忆泄漏。本文旨在设计一种相反的前瞻性评测——在事件发生前提问，从根本上消除记忆污染，从而更真实地考察前沿LLM的实际预测能力。

Method: 以2026年FIFA世界杯为场景，在39天内于每场比赛开球前，向六个具备扩展思维和原生服务端网络搜索的前沿LLM逐一询问比赛，要求其填写七项预测卡片（覆盖全部104场赛事、12个小组头名及一个赛前冠军池）。提问时答案尚不存在，故评测无泄漏。共收集4,494条已评分预测，并分析各系统在结果、比分、小组头名、冠军等市场的行为模式。

Result: 比赛结果预测平均准确率63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门持平（系统通常就是押热门）。系统间一致性远高于正确率，多数投票无增益。系统对平局和进球预测不足，比分预测集中于单一原型结果。准确率随比赛悬殊程度上升而非随信息丰富度上升：在最接近、情报最丰富的比赛中准确率崩塌，而关于大赛整体的宏观问题回答良好。前沿系统间差异不大：首尾排名稳定、中间名次波动，且差距始终很小。

Conclusion: 当前一代前沿LLM在真实世界前瞻性预测任务上表现趋同，预测行为存在系统性偏差（欠押平局与进球、比分过度集中），且其准确率更多地取决于事件的确定性而非可获得的信息量。该研究以无泄漏的前瞻设计为评估LLM预测能力提供了可靠基准，并将简报档案、赛程、官方结果及评分代码一并公开。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [87] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SocietyBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型理解和预测真实社会事件演变的能力。该基准从多平台新闻和社交媒体帖子中提取事件时间线并生成可审计的预测问题，通过实体替换和日期偏移构建反事实社会世界，对模型在概率校准和时间准确性两个正交维度上打分。在五个事件125个预测点上，最强模型仅得75分（满分100），且两个维度表现相互独立，基于同一基座模型的三个智能体框架未能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试主要衡量模型"完成任务"的能力（如修bug、驱动浏览器、操作GUI），但缺乏对模型理解并预测真实社会事件轨迹这一互补性社会能力的系统评估。

Method: SocietyBench包含端到端流程：从一行事件主题出发，收集五个平台的Web新闻和社交媒体帖子，提炼为按日期索引的时间线（将事实事件与公众舆论层分离），每个截止日期转化为经过审计的预测问题库。评分在概率校准和时间准确性两个100分轴上正交进行。关键创新是三步匿名化过程：替换所有命名实体并按每事件常数平移日期，创建与真实事件结构相同但剥离表面标签的反事实社会世界，防止模型匹配预训练记忆。

Result: 在五个异构事件和125个预测点（中英文版本）上，六个前沿LLM中最强者仅达到75.0/100分（平凡基线为50）。校准和时间两个维度可分离，模型可能在某一维度强而另一维度弱。基于同一基座模型的三个智能体框架未能超越基座模型，两个无模型启发式方法落后于所有LLM。单个维度上的事件间差距达21.4分。

Conclusion: SocietyBench揭示了现有LLM在预测社会事件演变方面仍有显著提升空间，两个评分维度独立且不可互相替代，评估应覆盖多个事件而非单一事件。所有匿名化时间线、问题库、真实答案和评分代码均已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Stochastic Saddle Avoidance Beyond Unit Excitation and Smoothness: A Pathwise Lyapunov-Perron Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03001)
*Junwen Qiu,Bohao Ma,Andre Milzarek,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了无需单位激励（UE）假设的随机递归几乎必然避开鞍点的抽象定理，通过路径条件替代UE要求，并将其应用于随机镜像下降、随机重排和近端随机梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UE假设要求随机误差在每个方向上的期望都有一致正分量，但这一假设在过参数化/插值模型（噪声在驻点附近消失）和有限和问题（噪声位于低维权变子空间中）中均不成立。现有避鞍理论无法覆盖这些常见场景，需要更一般的理论框架。

Method: 提出一个无需UE的抽象几乎必然避鞍定理，将UE型要求替换为可验证的路径条件。由于随机采样映射通常不共享固定点，经典的中心稳定流形论证不适用，因此采用依赖路径的变量替换结合路径Lyapunov–Perron证明策略。

Result: 获得了随机镜像下降（含SGD）和随机重排的严格鞍点避让结果；对非光滑复合目标，证明了近端型随机梯度方法的避鞍结果。结合适当的迭代收敛保证，可建立原始目标函数收敛到局部极小值的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为无UE假设的随机优化避鞍分析提供了统一的理论框架，扩展了随机递归避鞍理论的适用范围，覆盖了过参数化、有限和等实际重要场景。

Abstract: Unit excitation (UE) is a common assumption in stochastic saddle avoidance: the stochastic error must have a uniformly positive component along every direction, in expectation. This condition gives a direct way to rule out convergence to strict saddles, but it also oversimplifies the actual noise structure, and does not match many stochastic optimization regimes. In overparameterized or interpolation models, the noise may vanish near stationarity. In finite-sum problems, the stochastic gradient noise may lie in a low-dimensional, data-dependent subspace. In these (common) scenarios, UE is naturally not satisfied. In this paper, we prove an abstract almost sure avoidance theorem for stochastic recursions without UE. The theorem replaces UE-type requirements by verifiable pathwise conditions. In applications, these conditions follow, e.g., from local smoothness and finite-moment assumptions under standard i.i.d. sampling, or from the finite-sum structure under without-replacement sampling. Since the stochastically sampled maps generally do not share a fixed point, the celebrated center-stable manifold argument used in deterministic analyses is not directly applicable. Instead, we use a path-dependent change of variables together with a pathwise Lyapunov--Perron-based proof strategy. As applications, we obtain strict saddle avoidance for stochastic mirror descent (including SGD) and for random reshuffling. For nonsmooth composite objectives, we prove avoidance results for a proximal-type stochastic gradient method. Combining these insights with suitable iterate convergence guarantees, this allows establishing convergence to local minimizers of the original objective function.

</details>


### [89] [Oracle-Based Distributionally Robust Optimization under Optimal Transport Ambiguity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03075)
*Guixian Chen,Salar Fattahi,Soroosh Shafiee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效求解最优传输模糊集下分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题的方法。通过将内层最坏情况期望问题精确化简为标量预算分配任务，绕过大规模提升型重构，并开发了基于oracle的分布最佳响应框架来计算近似原始-对偶解。同时将分析扩展到对偶DRO形式，证明了最小有利分布支撑集大小的上界，并给出了高效的凸规划还原方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DRO问题通过将极大极小问题重构为单层凸规划来求解，虽然理论上可处理，但这类重构引入了大量辅助变量和严格的锥约束，在实际中扩展性差，计算代价高昂。

Method: 1) 将内层最坏情况期望问题精确化简为标量预算分配任务；2) 提出绕过大规模提升型重构的高效算法，以及快速后处理方案恢复最优最坏情况分布（支撑集至多N+1个点）；3) 将该过程嵌入基于oracle的分布最佳响应框架中，直接计算近似原始-对偶解；4) 扩展到对偶DRO形式，证明最小有利分布支撑集上界为min{N+n+1, KN}，并提供高效的凸规划还原方法。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的方法显著优于基于重构的最先进求解器，在计算效率上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 通过结构化洞察（内层问题等价于标量预算分配）避免了大规模凸重构，同时提供了对偶问题的理论支撑和高效求解方案，为大规模DRO问题提供了实用且可扩展的求解路径。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) with optimal transport ambiguity sets is traditionally solved by reformulating the minimax problem into a single-level convex program. While theoretically tractable, these reformulations introduce numerous auxiliary variables and demanding conic constraints that scale poorly in practice. In this paper, we address this challenge by reducing the inner worst-case expectation problem exactly to a scalar budget allocation task. This structural insight yields an efficient algorithm that bypasses large lifted reformulations, alongside a fast post-processing scheme to recover an optimal worst-case distribution supported on at most $N+1$ points, where $N$ denotes the sample size. We embed this procedure within an oracle-based distributional best-response framework to directly compute an approximate primal-dual solution to the overall DRO problem. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to the dual DRO formulation, proving the existence of a least-favorable distribution supported on at most $\min\{N+n+1, KN\}$ atoms, where $n$ and $K$ denote the decision dimension and number of loss components, respectively, and provide an efficient convex programming reduction to extract it from the solution of the primal DRO. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art reformulation-based solvers.

</details>

Abstract: We develop inexact augmented Lagrangian (AL) methods for linearly constrained convex composite optimization problems whose objective is the sum of a smooth convex function and a possibly nonsmooth closed proper convex function with compact domain. Unlike primal accuracy guarantees based on objective-value error, our methods target verifiable approximate KKT solutions. In the convex setting, we propose three inexact AL schemes with optimal primal-dual complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1})$, improving prior AL bounds such as $\mathcal O(ε^{-4/3})$, $\mathcal O(ε^{-7/4})$, and $\mathcal O(ε^{-2})$, and improving the $\mathcal O(ε^{-1}\log(ε^{-1}))$ guarantees of proximal augmented Lagrangian (PAL) methods. Two of the three convex variants are parameter-free. We also establish non-ergodic convergence guarantees, including a stronger last-iterate guarantee for one variant. In the strongly convex setting, our methods achieve near-optimal complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1/2}\log(ε^{-1}))$, with two parameter-free variants. Numerical experiments on six important problem classes, including elastic-net least-squares regression, group-sparse Huberized support vector machines, and a quantum semidefinite program (SDP), demonstrate substantial computational advantages of our inexact AL methods over a representative PAL method, with speedups frequently ranging from $5$ to $50$ times.

Result: 证明了下界Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)与已知最佳确定性和随机一阶上界在万能常数意义下匹配，确立了最优确定性oracle复杂度，并展示了光滑非凸（Θ(ε⁻²)）与非光滑弱凸（Θ(ε⁻⁴)）之间的ε⁻²因子分离源于非光滑几何而非随机性。

Conclusion: 非光滑弱凸优化的额外ε⁻²复杂度因子是问题的固有几何性质造成的，反映了光滑与非光滑情形之间的根本性复杂度分离。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of $ρ$-weakly convex and $G$-Lipschitz functions, where stationarity is measured by the gradient of the Moreau envelope. We consider a first-order oracle that returns both the function value and the full subdifferential at every query point. We prove that every deterministic first-order algorithm requires $ Ω({ρG^2Δ}/{ε^4})$ oracle queries whenever $Δ\leq {G^2}/ρ$, where $f(\bz)-\inf f \leq Δ$. This lower bound matches the best known deterministic and stochastic first-order upper bounds, up to universal constants, and establishes the optimal deterministic oracle complexity. The result reveals a fundamental complexity separation between smooth nonconvex and nonsmooth weakly convex optimization. While smooth nonconvex minimization admits a $Θ(ε^{-2})$ oracle complexity, nonsmooth weakly convex optimization incurs an intrinsic additional $ε^{-2}$ factor arising from nonsmooth geometry rather than stochasticity.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"投影-提升"（project-then-lift）方法来为非凸二次约束二次规划（QCQP）生成割平面。通过对扩展QCQP公式中两条基不等式进行两步松弛投影，分析其在二维空间中的联合值域，分别针对非凸与凸联合值域给出闭式凸包描述与半定表示，从而产生新的联合值域不等式，并可通过提升回到原问题。该方法还扩展出更灵活的割线混合联合值域不等式，保持稀疏性，并在几何实验中显著缩小RLT松弛投影的面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸QCQP是NP-hard问题，现有割平面方法（如MIR不等式）主要面向混合整数线性或凸结构，难以有效处理非凸二次约束中的"混合"项与联合值域结构。作者希望通过推广MIR思想，系统性地从两条基不等式的联合值域中挖掘新的有效割平面，以收紧松弛、提高求解效率。

Method: 1) 从扩展QCQP公式中选取两条基有效不等式，将其关联的两行松弛投影到一个二维集合中；2) 分析两条基不等式中二次函数的联合值域——对非凸联合值域给出闭式凸包描述，对凸联合值域给出半定表示；3) 由此生成联合值域不等式，并将其提升回扩展QCQP公式；4) 提出更灵活的割线混合联合值域不等式，以更好地暴露和利用非凸联合值域；5) 整个方法保持稀疏性，因为提升不等式的支撑由基不等式的支撑控制。

Result: 初步几何实验表明，联合值域不等式能在RLT（reformulation-linearization-technique）构造的投影松弛上带来显著的面积缩减，证明该方法能有效收紧非凸QCQP的松弛。

Conclusion: 该项目-提升框架为生成非凸QCQP割平面提供了一种新的、普适的途径，既推广了MIR不等式的思想，又能处理混合项并保持稀疏结构，具有进一步开发为通用求解器的潜力。

Abstract: We study cutting planes for nonconvex quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) through a project-then-lift approach inspired by mixed-integer rounding (MIR) inequalities. Given two base valid inequalities for the extended QCQP formulation, we project the associated two-row relaxation into a two-dimensional set and analyze the joint range of quadratic functions in two base inequalities. For the nonconvex joint range, we give a closed-form convex hull description of the projected set; for the convex joint range, we give its semidefinite representation. This yields a new family of joint-range inequalities, which can be lifted back to the extended QCQP formulation. MIR inequalities can handle ``mixed'' terms: continuous variables or fractional linear combinations of integer variables. Similarly, we propose more flexible secant mixed-joint-range inequalities, which better expose and exploit the nonconvex joint range. The proposed approach preserves sparsity, since the support of each lifted inequality is controlled by that of the base inequalities. In preliminary geometric experiments, the joint-range inequalities yield substantial area reduction of the projected relaxation constructed via reformulation-linearization-technique.

</details>


### [93] [Dynamic Proximal Point Method for Unconstrained Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03349)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无约束优化的新型动态近端点算法。该算法在每次迭代中自适应更新对角加权矩阵来构造近端子问题，并通过内层牛顿法与线搜索相结合的方式求解每个子问题，同时在外层根据内层求解器表现调整参考点和正则化参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近端点算法通常使用固定或简单的正则化参数，难以适应不同优化问题的曲率特性，导致收敛速度受限。本文旨在通过动态调整对角加权矩阵和正则化参数，提升近端点算法的实际性能与全局收敛性。

Method: 算法采用双层结构：外层动态更新参考点并调整正则化参数（基于内层牛顿求解器的表现）；内层使用结合线搜索的牛顿法求解带对角加权二次正则项的子问题。论文推导了计算牛顿步的简化线性系统，定义了相应的价值函数（merit function），并讨论了基于导数信息构造对角缩放矩阵的实用方法，同时提供了实现导向的伪代码和停止准则。

Result: 论文给出了完整的算法框架，包括牛顿步的线性系统推导、价值函数定义、对角缩放矩阵的构造策略、以及实现层面的伪代码和与算法一致的停止准则，为算法的理论分析和实际实现提供了基础。

Conclusion: 该动态近端点算法通过自适应对角加权和动态正则化参数调节，为无约束优化提供了一种兼顾全局收敛性与实用性的新框架，其内层牛顿-线搜索机制确保了非线性求解器的全局收敛。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel dynamic proximal point algorithm for unconstrained optimization. The method generates a sequence of proximal subproblems, where the quadratic regularization term is weighted by a diagonal matrix that is updated adaptively at each iteration. Each subproblem is solved using an inner Newton's method combined with a line search, which provides a global convergence mechanism for the nonlinear solver. At the outer level, the algorithm updates the reference point and adjusts the regularization parameter based on the performance of the inner Newton solver. We derive the reduced linear system used to compute the Newton step, define the corresponding merit function, and discuss practical approaches for constructing the diagonal scaling matrix from derivative information. The paper also provides implementation-oriented pseudocode and stopping criteria that are consistent with the proposed method.

</details>


### [94] [Fast and slow mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03376)
*Roxana Dumitrescu,Julian Gutierrez Pineda,Peter Tankov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于双时间尺度结构的框架，用于在公共噪声下的平均场博弈（MFG）中构造近似Nash均衡。通过利用快变量的遍历动力学和慢变量的最优控制/停止，将含公共噪声的完整MFG近似为无公共噪声的"有效"MFG，并从有效MFG的均衡显式构造完整MFG的ε-MFG均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共噪声下的平均场博弈求解通常非常复杂，因为公共噪声引入了额外的维度，使得均衡的存在性和计算都变得困难。本文旨在通过双时间尺度分解，将复杂的含公共噪声MFG问题简化为无公共噪声的平均场博弈问题，从而降低求解难度并提供显式的近似均衡构造方法。

Method: 利用双时间尺度结构：公共噪声由快速变化的遍历动力学变量承载，慢变量则被最优控制或停止。关键思想是将含公共噪声的完整MFG的近似解，通过"有效"MFG（无公共噪声，系数对快速过程的平稳测度取平均）来构造。具体步骤包括：从有效MFG的均衡出发，利用随机化控制和停止构造完整MFG的显式ε-MFG均衡；为此需要建立随机化停止的MFG均衡存在性的新结果，并依赖两尺度扩散的强收敛结果。

Result: 论文取得了以下结果：(1) 建立了带随机化停止的MFG均衡存在性的新理论结果；(2) 证明了可以从有效MFG（无公共噪声）的均衡显式构造完整MFG（含公共噪声）的ε-MFG均衡；(3) 证明了时间尺度分离参数可以控制Nash均衡条件中的误差。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个系统化的近似框架，使得高维复杂的公共噪声MFG问题可以通过更低维度的有效平均场博弈来近似求解，误差由时间尺度分离参数显式控制。这一结果对求解含公共噪声的MFG应用问题（如大规模分布式控制、经济学等）具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: We propose a framework for constructing approximate Nash equilibria in mean-field games (MFG) with common noise based on a two-time-scale structure. In our model, the common noise is carried by a fast variable evolving under ergodic dynamics, while the slow variable is either optimally controlled or stopped. The fast variable enters the dynamics of the slow one through the drift coefficient. The key idea is to construct an approximation to the solution of the full MFG with common noise using an ``effective'' MFG without common noise, where the coefficients are averaged with respect to the stationary measure of the fast-scale process. We construct an explicit $\varepsilon$-MFG equilibrium for the full MFG from the equilibrium for the effective MFG with randomized control and stopping. To this end, we obtain new results on existence of MFG equilibria with randomized stopping. We rely on strong convergence results for two-scale diffusions under structural assumptions on the MFG, and show that the time-scale separation parameter controls the error in the Nash equilibrium condition.

</details>

Method: 提出近端差分凸（DC）算法框架，融合以下关键机制：(1) Barzilai-Borwein (BB)步长自适应调整；(2) 非单调线搜索策略以增强稳定性；(3) 外推机制加速收敛同时保证全局稳定性。(4) 在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质框架下建立全局收敛性理论。

Result: 在SCAD正则化最小二乘问题和图Ginzburg-Landau图像分割模型上的数值实验表明，所提出的算法相比现有DC算法在效率和精度上均具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法框架有效解决了标准近端DC算法的步长保守性和稳定性问题，在理论收敛保证与实际性能之间取得了良好平衡，为一般非凸非光滑优化问题提供了强有力的求解工具。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel proximal difference-of-convex (DC) algorithmic framework to solve general non-convex, non-smooth optimization problems. By combining Barzilai-Borwein (BB) step sizes with nonmonotone line search strategies, our approach effectively overcomes the conservative step sizes and stability issues inherent in standard proximal DC algorithms. Furthermore, we develop extrapolation mechanisms to accelerate convergence while ensuring global stability. The global convergence of the proposed algorithms is rigorously established under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property. Numerical experiments on the SCAD-regularized least squares problem and graphic Ginzburg-Landau image segmentation models demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve highly competitive efficiency and accuracy compared to existing DC algorithms.

</details>


### [96] [Pursuing Optimal Stepsize in Adaptive Gradient-Based Quadratic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03546)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验全局参数知识、基于局部曲率估计的自适应步长策略，用于二次函数的快速梯度下降收敛，并证明其能收敛到最优常数步长。

Method: 受光滑凸函数自适应步长算法的启发，提出一种计算轻量级的策略：利用运行时对最小和最大局部曲率的估计来动态调整步长。作者进一步证明了该算法能够收敛到最优常数步长，即理论上能达到最快收敛速度的步长值。

Conclusion: 所提算法在无需全局参数先验知识的前提下，通过局部曲率估计实现了接近最优的收敛性能，为实际应用中自适应步长的选择提供了计算高效且理论有保证的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of achieving fast convergence in gradient descent for quadratic functions without relying on a priori knowledge of global function parameters. Inspired by adaptive stepsize algorithms for smooth convex functions, we propose a computationally lightweight strategy based on running estimates of minimal and maximal local curvatures. We prove that our proposed algorithm converges to the optimal constant stepsize which achieves the fastest convergence. Simulations show that the convergence rate achieved by our proposed algorithm is comparable or superior to recent adaptive approaches both in the quadratic case under consideration and in a preliminary test on logistic classification.

</details>


### [97] [Adaptive Stepsizes With Certified Convergence in Distributed Gradient Tracking With Quadratic Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03548)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Andrea Iannelli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对标量二次型问题提出了一种自适应步长规则，用于分布式梯度跟踪算法，保证收敛且无需问题先验知识，能够显式捕获网络拓扑与曲率异质性之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式梯度算法通常需要合适的步长选择，但理论界往往过于保守，实际实现依赖经验调参。虽然近期出现了在线自适应策略，但它们的性质和性能仍未被充分理解。作者希望在对信息丰富的标量二次成本设置中提供分析性见解。

Method: 聚焦于标量二次成本问题，推导了一个仅由一致性矩阵的必要谱半径和局部成本曲率异质性参数化的收敛界，两者均可在线计算且无需问题先验知识。通过优化该收敛界，获得计算上易处理的收敛率和最优常数步长的替代指标。所得的步长具有解析解释，保证任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性下收敛，并在完全图和均匀曲率情况下被证明是紧的。

Result: 推导了与网络拓扑和曲率异质性明确相关的收敛界；提出了一个可解析解释、能在线计算的自适应步长规则；在完全图与均匀曲率情形下该步长为紧界；大量数值仿真表明其显著优于现有离线和在线步长选择规则。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应步长规则在标量二次问题上具有可证明的收敛保证、解析可解释性，并在实践中优于现有方法，为分布式优化的自适应步长设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive stepsize rule with guaranteed convergence for Distributed Gradient Tracking applied to scalar quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvatures. Most distributed gradient-based algorithms require a suitable stepsize selection. Available theoretical bounds are often overly conservative, while practical implementations typically rely on empirically tuned heuristics. Online adaptive strategies have only recently emerged for general distributed convex optimization, but their properties and performance remain only partially understood. To gain analytical insight, we focus on the informative setting of scalar quadratic costs, which allows us to explicitly capture the interplay between network topology and curvature heterogeneity. We derive a convergence bound parameterized only by the essential spectral radius of the consensus matrix and the heterogeneity of the local cost curvatures, both computable online without any prior knowledge of the optimization problem. Optimizing this bound yields a computationally tractable surrogate for the convergence rate and the optimal constant stepsize. The resulting stepsize admits an analytical interpretation, guarantees convergence for arbitrary network topologies and curvature heterogeneity, and is provably tight for complete graphs and homogeneous curvatures. Finally, extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed distributed adaptive strategy significantly outperforms existing offline and online stepsize selection rules in the considered setting.

</details>


### [98] [Sliding Methods for Hölder-Smooth Convex--Concave Minimax Optimization with Bilinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03846)
*Nhat Trung Nguyen,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双线性耦合的凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出一种利用问题复合结构的滑动方法，通过按各组件自身属性调节的查询频率来调用f、g和双线性耦合算子的预言机。在Hölder连续条件下建立收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数、常数、强凸参数和耦合矩阵谱性质，并涵盖非强凸和部分强凸情形，同时处理了随机问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸-凹极小极大优化问题广泛出现在机器学习、博弈论和成像等领域。现有方法通常以最差组件的平滑性作为复杂度基准，但实际问题中各组件（f、g和双线性耦合项）的平滑性往往不同。作者希望设计一种能"各取所需"的算法，使每个组件的梯度评估次数按其自身平滑度分层，而非统一受限于最差组件，从而提升计算效率。

Method: 提出一种滑动方法（sliding method），基于单调变分不等式的递归滑动方案。方法根据f、g各自的Hölder连续性和强凸性参数以及耦合矩阵的谱性质，以不同频率查询各组件预言机。对随机问题，在退化情形证明均匀期望间隙界，在环境光滑且正有效曲率下证明收敛到显式噪声底线。

Result: 建立了Hölder连续条件下的收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数、Hölder常数、强凸参数和耦合矩阵谱性质。数值实验复现了理论预测的Hölder指数，确认各函数所需梯度评估次数按其自身平滑度分离而非取二者中最差者。断层成像基准测试显示当梯度评估比矩阵-向量乘积更昂贵时带来运行时收益。

Conclusion: 该滑动方法有效利用了问题的复合结构，使各预言机的调用频率与其个体属性匹配，在理论和实验上都证明了比"一刀切"方法更优的复杂度表现，特别适用于各组件平滑性差异大且梯度评估昂贵的实际问题。

Abstract: We study convex-concave minimax optimization problems with bilinear coupling of the form $\min_{x\in \mathcal X}\max_{y\in \mathcal Y} \; f(x)+\langle y,\mathbf{B}x\rangle-g(y),$ where the functions $f$ and $g$ have Hölder continuous (sub)gradients. This setting covers a broad range of regimes, from nonsmooth problems with bounded subgradient variation to smooth problems with Lipschitz continuous gradients; for a smooth component used in the coupling-induced regularizer, its Lipschitz-gradient constant is assumed to hold in the ambient space. We propose a sliding method that exploits the composite structure of the problem by querying the oracles associated with $f$, $g$, and the bilinear coupling operator at prescribed frequencies determined by their individual properties. The method is based on a recursive sliding scheme for monotone variational inequalities. We establish convergence guarantees under Hölder continuity and show how the resulting complexity bounds depend explicitly on the Hölder exponents, Hölder constants, strong convexity parameters, and spectral properties of the coupling matrix. Our analysis covers nonstrongly convex and partially strongly convex regimes. For stochastic problems, we prove a uniform expected-gap bound in the degenerate regime and, under ambient smoothness and positive effective curvature, convergence up to an explicit noise floor. Numerical experiments reproduce the predicted Hölder exponents and confirm that the number of gradient evaluations required for each function separates according to its own smoothness level rather than the worse of the two. A tomographic benchmark shows runtime gains when gradient evaluations are more expensive than the additional matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [99] [A Robust Chance Constrained Approach to Surgery Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03931)
*Marco Caserta,Antonio García Romero,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于择期手术排程的鲁棒机会约束框架，将不确定性量化（缓冲引擎）与排程优化解耦，使手术级别的可靠性成为内生决策变量。在HLA Moncloa医院10个实例（45-227台手术）上，该方法将超过90分钟的延误减少97%，第95百分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班减少42%，优于确定性均值基线和均匀按比例缓冲基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术时长具有显著不确定性，基于平均时长或固定缓冲规则的排程方案在事前看似高效，但执行中容易因早期手术超时沿时间轴传播产生累计延误，使后续手术暴露于延迟风险中。现有方法将可靠性作为固定参数，无法根据手术间的异质性自适应分配保护，导致要么过度保守、要么保护不足。

Method: 提出鲁棒机会约束框架：（1）缓冲引擎将分布信息转化为依赖可靠性的缓冲时长；（2）排程模型从离散可靠性选项菜单中联合决策手术分配、排序、开始时间和手术级可靠性水平；（3）支持平均可靠性、最差天和硬目标三种风险姿态；（4）在滚动时间源（rolling-origin）机制下应用于实际医院案例。

Result: 与确定性均值基线和均匀按比例缓冲基准相比：延误超过90分钟的手术减少97%，第95百分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班时间减少42%。方法的价值来自利用手术级别异质性，将缓冲分配给运营价值最高的手术，同时避免不必要的保守性。

Conclusion: 可靠性应作为内生的排程决策而非固定的服务水平参数。通过根据不确定性和运营暴露度分配缓冲，框架能将异构的时长数据和风险偏好转化为执行中更可靠、且比一刀切规则更少保守缓冲的排程方案。

Abstract: We study elective surgery scheduling under uncertain procedure durations. Schedules based on mean durations or fixed buffering rules may appear efficient ex ante but become fragile in execution, as early overruns propagate through the day and expose later surgeries to accumulated delay. We propose a robust chance-constrained framework that separates uncertainty quantification from schedule optimization. A buffer engine converts distributional information into reliability-dependent buffered durations, while the scheduling model jointly selects assignments, sequences, start times, and surgery-level reliability levels from a discrete menu. Reliability therefore becomes an endogenous scheduling decision rather than a fixed service-level parameter. The framework accommodates average-reliability, worst-day, and hard-target risk postures. Comparisons with common-reliability and uniform proportional-buffer benchmarks show that the menu derives its value from exploiting surgery-level heterogeneity, allocating protection where it has the greatest operational value while avoiding unnecessary conservatism. In a rolling-origin case study at HLA Moncloa Hospital in Madrid, covering 10 instances with 45 to 227 surgeries, the approach reduces delays exceeding 90 minutes by 97%, lowers the 95th-percentile delay by approximately 620 minutes, and reduces total overtime by 42% relative to a deterministic mean-based baseline. By allocating buffers according to uncertainty and operational exposure, the framework translates heterogeneous duration data and risk preferences into schedules that are more reliable in execution and less conservatively buffered than one-size-fits-all rules.

</details>


### [100] [Simulation-Based Neural Policies for Portfolio Choice: Architecture, Training, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03933)
*Jules Viard,Alexander Michaelides,Panos Parpas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了低维生命周期消费-储蓄与动态组合选择问题中神经网络策略的训练方法，通过可求解的动态规划参考解来隔离和诊断不同架构与优化方法的贡献，比较了四种架构（单一时间条件网络、跨制度切换的双网络拼接、逐日后向训练网络及其约束变体），并从福利、贝尔曼残差、形状约束和政策函数等多维度进行联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维随机控制问题中神经网络策略被广泛使用，但由于缺乏参考解，无法隔离和诊断任何单一架构或训练选择的贡献。为了系统研究架构与求解方法本身，需要一个具有足够低维归一化状态空间、可得到精确动态规划解的问题作为评估基准。

Method: 采用生命周期问题，其状态空间维度足够低以获得精确的动态规划参考解。比较四种架构：(1) 单一时间条件网络；(2) 跨制度切换两个网络串联；(3) 逐日期独立网络，按时间反向训练，冻结下游策略；(4) 逐日期架构的约束变体。逐日解耦使每个日期目标短且适定，并搭配方向主导优化（normalizes away gradient magnitude）。

Result: 达到相近实现效用目标的架构可能在是否尊重底层经济问题经济学特性上存在差异。研究从福利、无解的贝尔曼残差、形状限制及最终策略函数四个维度对每种设计进行联合评估。

Conclusion: 仅以效用目标衡量架构是不够的，需要结合经济学约束（如形状限制、贝尔曼残差）进行多维度联合评估，才能诊断架构与训练方法的好坏。

Abstract: Many economic decision problems, lifecycle consumption-saving and dynamic portfolio choice, are finite-horizon stochastic control problems with continuous states and actions. When the state is low-dimensional these problems are solved by dynamic programming on a grid. The grid cost grows exponentially in the state dimension, known as the curse of dimensionality, which motivates replacing the value-function grid with a neural policy optimized directly through simulation. Such policies are usually studied in the high-dimensional settings that motivate them, precisely where no reference solution exists. So the contribution of any single architectural or training choice cannot be isolated and diagnosed. We therefore take a step back and treat both the architecture and the solution method as the objects of study. To this end, we consider a lifecycle problem with a sufficiently low-dimensional normalized state space to admit an accurate dynamic programming solution, which is used for evaluation. We compare four architectures. The simplest consists of a single time-conditioned network. We then consider two networks concatenated across the regime switch, followed by one network per date trained backward against frozen downstream policies. Finally, we evaluate a constrained variant of the per-date architecture. Decoupling the policy across time gives each date a short, well-posed objective, which we pair with direction-dominant optimization that normalizes away gradient magnitude. Architectures that lead to similar realized utility objective can nevertheless differ in whether they respect the underlying problem's economics. We therefore evaluate each design jointly based on welfare, a solution-free Bellman residual, shape restrictions, and the resulting policy functions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ISEE（交互式语义增强系统），通过评分系统衡量数据字段描述质量、收集领域知识并与用户协作丰富语义，从而提升LLM智能体在数据相关任务（如实体链接）中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在数据任务中依赖字段描述的清晰度和完整性，但现实中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，关键上下文源于用户领域知识且很少被公开记录，这种语义鸿沟限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的性能表现。

Method: ISEE系统包含三个核心模块：通过评分系统衡量数据字段描述的质量，用于收集领域知识，以及通过与用户的协作交互来丰富语义信息。

Result: 通过用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究，实验表明ISEE显著降低了认知负荷，提升了描述质量，并增强了下游任务的性能。

Conclusion: ISEE有效弥合了数据字段语义鸿沟，通过人机协作的语义丰富机制，验证了其在提升LLM智能体数据任务性能方面的实用价值。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits），将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT），扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，使电路能够从零自组装功能逻辑，并在面对永久性硬件故障时快速重路由逻辑。对软错误，策略在远超训练条件的损伤规模下仍能达到>99.99%的恢复精度，且表现出跨电路规模的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算中的容错依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统通过围绕损伤的动态重组织来维持功能，展现出自适应可塑性。作者受此启发，希望将生物自组织原则引入数字硬件领域，实现动态、自适应的容错机制。

Method: 该方法将功能逻辑生成与维护形式化为图上的元学习问题。核心架构是拓扑掩码Transformer，用于配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT）。研究扩展了神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，在退化的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务，而非再生固定目标状态。

Result: 实验表明：1）电路能从零开始自组装功能逻辑；2）对永久性、未见过的硬件故障能快速重路由逻辑；3）对软错误，在远超训练条件的损伤规模下达到>99.99%的恢复精度；4）表现出跨电路规模的泛化，在比训练时显著更宽的图上精度有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作架起了生物自组织原则与数字硬件实际应用之间的桥梁，为数字电路的自适应容错提供了一种新范式，展示了在无需静态冗余的前提下通过动态重组实现高可靠性硬件维护的可行性。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM中"人工蜂群思维"效应导致的语义坍缩问题，提出了"元人格锚定+滤波温度缩放(FTS)"的两阶段生成框架，将模型输出的平均余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著提升了生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在"人工蜂群思维"效应，即使在高温度采样下，模型也会在开放性问题上收敛到狭窄而同质化的共识，导致语义坍缩，表现为极高的响应间相似度（约0.80-0.90）。这种语义多样性缺失限制了AI部署的创造性和多样性。

Method: 提出两阶段生成框架：(1) 元人格锚定：首先提示模型自我选择独特的个性化人格作为生成起点；(2) 滤波温度缩放(FTS)：采用双重采样筛，先用Top-p滤波保留语法有效性，再对幸存候选施加极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）以探索拓宽后的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上、约20B参数的开放权重模型上验证，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，且大多数问题得分低于0.7阈值，有效缩小了人工模式坍缩与人类类型学多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 元人格锚定结合滤波温度缩放可有效缓解LLM的语义坍缩问题，显著提升生成多样性，并已作为开源框架发布以促进更多样化、更具创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [104] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PULSE——一种受Object-Process-Methodology启发的语言，将知识图谱工程中分散的四种操作角色及其写效应本地化到单一类型化运行时中，通过执行契约固定证据不覆盖、分支隔离等六项安全属性，并在大规模基准（NOAA IBTrACS 147万+过渡对）上实现了痕迹对等性与变异区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，可接受状态、观测、约束、过程与假设场景分散在不同构件中，其组合执行契约是外部的、非形式化的。这种外部化导致安全属性难以验证、执行行为难以保证，缺乏一个统一的运行时来本地化这些操作角色及其写效应。

Method: PULSE语言将四种操作角色（modes）本地化在一个类型化运行时中。执行契约从六个维度加以固定：证据不覆盖、分支隔离、多主体定时器接地、状态变更守卫、基于声明的时序与空间事件排序。外部运行器决定证据是否成为权威动作。GeoSPARQL、SOSA、SHACL作为生成视图。提出核心演算并给出效应隔离引理与六项安全属性，Lean 4验证内核类比，配合88项测试、3534项有界检查及32项Lean/Python运行时-内核用例。

Result: 在37,440条生成的时序轨迹中，PULSE与独立工作流匹配并能区分十个单字段变异体。在NOAA IBTrACS自1980年以来的完整子集上，与GEOS及事件扫描在1,476,290个过渡区对上一致，涵盖4,800个采样和12,831个限时事件。首个作者实现的标准组合与Sismic状态图复现了测试的冷链痕迹，项目特定GeoSPARQL探针衡量了接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在测试片段范围内，PULSE支持契约本地化、安全论证与痕迹对等性；但语言优越性与可用性不在评估范围之内。证据主要停留在测试片段内的实现声明确证层面。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events.
