These findings demonstrate that relying solely on intra-modal evaluation, particularly text-only evaluation, may substantially overestimate the effectiveness of knowledge unlearning in VLMs, underscoring the need for cross-modal unlearning and evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentGuard，一个高效且可检查的LLM安全防护框架。它将文本推理压缩为连续潜在状态，使安全判定直接从连续表征中预测，同时通过辅助解码器按需生成审计内容以保持可检查性。在8B规模下相较GuardReasoner-8B将加权F1从83.95提升至84.91，并将关键路径推理成本从268.56个生成的推理token降至1.60个潜在推理token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的防护模型虽能增强LLM安全能力，但对每个交互解码显式推理成本高昂；潜在推理方法虽能减少token生成，但在安全审核领域探索不足且缺少可检查界面。因此需要一种既能保持效率（压缩推理为连续状态）又能保持可检查性（提供审计接口）的安全防护框架。

Method: LatentGuard采用分段式课程学习（staged curriculum），逐步将任务对齐的文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，使安全判定可直接从连续表征预测。为保证可检查性，引入一个隔离的辅助解码器（auxiliary decoder），按需生成精简的审计产物，且推理生成不占用标准推理路径。

Result: 在8B参数规模下，LatentGuard的相对GuardReasoner-8B将平均加权F1从83.95提升至84.91；关键路径推理成本从268.56个生成推理token显著降至1.60个潜在推理token。审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数（audit utility score）。

Conclusion: LatentGuard展示了潜在推理与可检查性结合的有效路径，在提升安全审核性能的同时大幅降低推理成本，为可部署的LLM安全防护提供了一种高效且可审计的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [204] [Oilbird: Training-Free Speculative Decoding with Keys the Verifier Already Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03839)
*Tao Jin,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Zhenzhu Yan,Teeradaj Racharak,Naoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究训练无关的投机解码（speculative decoding）中基于前缀查找的草稿生成失败问题，指出其并非覆盖率不足而是寻址（addressing）问题。作者提出一种语义草稿源，即用验证器在已提交token上已计算的隐藏状态对同一token池重新建键，并与现有词汇草稿器的树结构合并。在三个已发布草稿器上，在相同池和预算下可将接受长度提升24-29%；在API-Bank上Oilbird达到4.4倍自回归解码加速，优于最强训练无关基线（3.9倍）和EAGLE-3（2.0倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练无关的投机解码通过将上下文的精确后缀与早期上下文池匹配来生成草稿，但该查找方式会漏掉池中已有的正确草稿，尤其在工具调用场景中最为明显——请求几乎重复全部内容但只有少数新生成的值不同，且一个被拒绝的token会丢弃其后正确的续写。作者在十个基准上逐位置诊断该失败，发现是寻址问题而非覆盖问题：在最密集的工具调用基准上，最强精确匹配草稿器漏掉的草稿中约一半存在于池中却无法通过精确匹配访问。

Method: 提出第二种语义草稿源：使用验证器在每个已提交token上已计算的隐藏状态作为键对同一token池重新索引，并与现有的词汇草稿器树结构合并，使语义草稿能够嵌套在词汇草稿器内部运行。

Result: 在三个已发布的草稿器中，在匹配的池和预算条件下，该方法将接受长度提升24-29%。Oilbird在API-Bank上达到4.4倍自回归解码速度，相比之下本测试环境中最强训练无关基线为3.9倍，EAGLE-3为2.0倍。

Conclusion: 通过将语义寻址与精确匹配寻址结合，可以有效解决训练无关投机解码在工具调用及类似高重复场景下的草稿查找失败问题，显著提升解码加速效果。

Abstract: Training-free speculative decoding drafts by matching an exact suffix of the context against a pool of earlier context. That lookup misses correct drafts already in the pool, most visibly on tool-calling traffic, where a request repeats almost everything but the few values minted for it, and where one rejected token discards the correct continuation behind it. We diagnose the failure position by position across ten benchmarks and find it to be a problem of addressing rather than of coverage: on our densest tool-calling benchmark, about half of what the strongest exact-match drafter misses is present in the pool yet unreachable by exact matching. We therefore propose a second, semantic draft source: the same pool, re-keyed by the hidden state the verifier has already computed at each committed token, together with a merge that lets it ride inside an existing lexical drafter's tree. In three published drafters, at matched pool and budget, it lifts accepted length by 24-29%. Oilbird reaches 4.4x autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank, against 3.9x for the strongest training-free baseline in our harness and 2.0x for EAGLE-3.

</details>


### [205] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAFIA框架，一种针对内存增强型LLM智能体的查询仅攻击方法，通过探测和事实注入策略，在大型良性内存池和主动输入审计场景下实现高成功率的记忆投毒攻击，同时有效规避审计检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存增强型LLM智能体的记忆模块是恶意记录的攻击面，记忆投毒威胁亟需研究。然而现有查询仅攻击方法在两种实际且普遍的场景（大规模良性内存池、主动输入审计）中难以保持有效性，无法同时应对高检索竞争力和严格语义检查的双重挑战。

Method: MAFIA包含两个核心组件：(1) 放置策略——通过内存探测、预算分配和调度确保注入记录在检索中具有竞争力；(2) 载荷设计——利用紧凑的事实伪装绕过审计，在保持高语义相似度的同时保留恶意效果。

Result: 广泛评估表明MAFIA的攻击成功率最高达90.7%，同时将审计检测率从峰值83.3%抑制至最多7.4%，暴露了智能体记忆系统的关键漏洞。代码将公开。

Conclusion: MAFIA揭示了现有智能体记忆系统在面对查询仅、双重挑战（检索竞争与审计规避）条件下的严重安全脆弱性，表明需要更加强健的记忆安全防护机制。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [206] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContinualSkillBench，一个用于上下文持续技能学习的动态评估框架，涵盖五个领域各100个相互关联的子任务。实验发现：顺序执行普遍提升性能但收益因模型和领域而异；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均表现相当，说明提升主要来自先前上下文和反馈的适应而非可复用技能抽象；显式技能仅在需要可复用流程或精确输出的任务中提供选择性优势；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架为大型语言模型配备外部技能库以解决复杂任务，但这些系统能否有效进化其技能、以及进化后的技能是否能真正提升任务解决能力，目前尚不明确。作者希望弥合这一认知空白。

Method: 构建ContinualSkillBench动态评估框架，覆盖五个代表性领域，每个领域包含100个按难度递增排列且存在跨任务技能复用机会的相互关联子任务，通过对比顺序执行、显式技能维护等策略评估上下文持续技能学习能力。

Result: 顺序执行总体上有助于性能提升但收益因模型和领域而异；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均表现相当；显式技能在需要可复用程序或精确输出的任务上有选择性优势；较弱模型会积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。

Conclusion: 当前上下文技能进化机制能支持持续适应，但在将经验持续整合为稳健且可迁移的技能方面仍存在不足。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [207] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStream框架，将智能体基准组织为可配置任务流，在隔离、顺序和交错三种流式场景下系统评估自进化LLM智能体，揭示自进化可靠性随场景变化、收益受模型能力门控且非单调，且没有单一方法在所有场景中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要采用独立评估方式评估自进化LLM智能体，忽略了现实流式设置下智能体需要适应多样复杂任务流的真实情况，导致自进化智能体在流式环境中的行为仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出AgentStream统一框架：将智能体基准组织为可配置任务流，实例化Isolated、Sequential和Interleaved三种流式场景（流范围和领域组成逐步变化），并在三个前沿基础模型上组合评估五种代表性自进化方法，解耦模型能力、方法架构和流式场景对自进化的联合影响。

Result: 研究表明：自进化的可靠性在不同流式场景间存在差异；自进化的收益受模型能力门控，且与模型强度呈非单调关系；没有任何单一方法在所有模型和场景中占优。这些发现为跨模型和场景选择自进化方法提供了具体指导。

Conclusion: 作者主张自进化智能体应在真实任务流中进行评估，而非孤立的单任务设置。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [208] [ADMITBench: A Safety-Governed Reference Framework for Evaluating the Admissibility of Industrial LLM Advisories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03866)
*Yash Misra,Javal Vyas,Siddharth Gutta,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADMITBench 是一个用于评估工业大语言模型（LLM）在"拟议行动"层面所提供建议的参考框架。该框架实现了一个带版本控制、受安全治理约束的评估契约，通过三类非补偿性检查（证据支持、权限与程序符合、工厂特定后果检查）来判断建议是否合格。值得注意的是，框架中的"安全治理"指通过明确、非补偿性检查来确定合格性，而非表明评估器、模型或工厂已通过安全认证。0.1.0版本为公开参考实现，仅供技术研究用途，不授权实际执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中LLM生成的建议若直接应用于实际操作可能带来安全风险，需要一个结构化的评估框架来系统性地检验建议的合格性，而不依赖传统模型层面的安全认证。

Method: 该框架实现了一个版本化的、受安全治理约束的评估契约，包含三项核验：建议是否得到现有证据支持、是否在声明的权限和程序范围内被允许、以及是否通过所选评估配置中编码的工厂特定后果检查。所有检查均为显式且非补偿性的（即任一检查失败即判定不合格）。

Result: 发布了0.1.0版本的公开参考实现，作为用于技术和研究评估的公开基准，而非物理执行的授权。

Conclusion: ADMITBench提供了一个在行动层面评估工业LLM建议的参考框架，明确了"安全治理"的边界（仅指非补偿性检查机制，不涉及安全认证），并强调其参考实现仅用于研究目的，不构成实际执行的许可。

Abstract: This white paper presents ADMITBench, a reference framework for evaluating industrial LLM advisories at the level of the proposed action. The framework implements a versioned, safety-governed evaluation contract that checks whether a recommendation is supported by the available evidence, permitted under the stated authority and procedure, and acceptable under the plant-specific consequence checks encoded in the selected evaluation profile. In this report, \emph{safety-governed} means that eligibility is determined through explicit, non-compensatory checks derived from a versioned plant profile; it does not mean that the evaluator, model, or plant has been safety-certified. Release 0.1.0 is a public reference implementation for technical and research evaluation, not an authorisation for physical execution.

</details>


### [209] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在大语言模型Qwen3-32B中识别出时间视界的线性表征方向，并通过对比激活添加（contrastive activation-addition）技术，实现了对模型时间相关偏好、推荐和规划能力的双向操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在涉及延迟收益与成本的建议中表现出时间相关的偏好，但这些偏好是否可测量和可操控尚不清楚，这对涉及长期规划的安全性问题至关重要。

Method: 在教师强制的时间选择答案上训练对比线性探针（contrastive linear probes），在模型的残差流中识别短期与长期方向，然后使用对比激活添加（steering）技术进行干预，并在多个任务上评估效果。

Result: 时间视界方向可通过简单线性探针识别，steering能在双向诱导大幅偏好变化；在货币跨期选择中可双向改变模型在更小更早与更大更晚奖励之间的无差异阈值；适度的steering还能在规划相关能力指标上带来改进。

Conclusion: 模型的时间偏好在表征层是可测量的且可通过方向性干预加以操控，这一发现对AI系统提供延迟成本收益建议以及长期规划的安全性问题具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [210] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了自适应Transformer跟踪器中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性这一结构性缺陷，并基于此提出了Adversarial Path-Inversion (API)攻击框架，通过微小的输入扰动操纵门控决策，迫使推理进入篡改的计算路径，从而瓦解模型表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机平台资源受限推动了自适应Transformer跟踪器的发展，其通过输入依赖的动态路由在精度和效率间取得平衡。但作者发现这类架构存在一个被忽视的关键安全问题：门控模块在离散层跳转决策边界处具有无界的局部Lipschitz常数，导致微小的输入扰动可能被放大，造成推理拓扑的剧烈变化。这一数学上的不连续性构成了一个新的可被直接利用的攻击面，此前从未被系统性地研究过。

Method: 作者首先在数学上形式化表征了自适应跟踪架构中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性，将其识别为一种新的拓扑路径空间攻击面。基于此提出了Adversarial Path-Inversion (API)框架，该框架生成不可感知的对抗扰动来精确操纵门控模块的决策，迫使模型推理转向被篡改的计算路径。通过原始路径与反转路径之间的严重不一致性来瓦解模型的表征能力，实现高效攻击。

Result: 在多个最先进的自适应跟踪器上的大量实验表明，API框架相比于现有方法具有更优的扰动隐蔽性、更强的攻击有效性以及更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: 本工作为动态跟踪网络的安全分析开辟了新的维度，并为未来构建鲁棒的自适应跟踪架构提供了理论层面的警示。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [211] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"多元对齐"（pluralistic alignment）重新定位为一个社会性协调问题，而非单纯输出多样化问题。作者借鉴社会学理论，认为价值观由角色结构、互动塑造和权力场域分布所组织，并将其转化为AI系统设计的具体启示，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调和情境敏感评估。对于智能体系统，对齐不仅需要关注最终输出，还需对齐角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署在日益多样的社会情境中，对齐不能再被视为对单一统一价值集的优化。系统需要识别、表征并响应多种合理视角。然而当前多元对齐方法缺乏对价值观在实践中如何被社会组织、竞争和协调的清晰说明，亟需理论和设计资源来解决这一挑战。

Method: 作者从社会学成熟传统出发，论证视角可被理解为受角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并分布在权力和专业场域中。基于此，将社会理论洞见转化为具体AI系统设计含义，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调、情境敏感评估，并针对智能体系统提出了将角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环纳入对齐范围的设计空间。

Result: 文章重新定位多元对齐为社会性协调（socially grounded coordination）问题，而非输出多样化问题；勾画了支持多元视角以结构化且可问责方式进行交互的系统设计空间，并识别了未来在真实环境中实现和实证评估这些方法的研宄方向。

Conclusion: 社会理论为多元对齐提供了必要的概念与设计资源，多元对齐应被重新概念化为基于社会结构的协调过程。未来的工作需要在真实世界环境中实现并实证评估这些方法，以验证其有效性和可行性。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [212] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现因果感知（Causal Perception）框架，将Álvarez和Ruggieri（2025）提出的纯理论框架落地为可计算算法。该框架描述的是持有不同结构因果模型（SCM）的智能体对同一系统推断出不同概率分布（包括反事实和干预分布）的现象。研究者设计了结构性和参数性两种因果感知的操作化方法，提出计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法及衡量分歧的距离指标，并在德国信用数据集上展示了因果感知如何影响多专家决策场景中的准确性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知是一个有前景的概率框架，用于解释不同智能体因持不同结构因果模型而对同一系统产生不同认知的现象，但该框架此前仅停留在纯理论层面，缺乏实际实现和实证验证。为了让这一概念能够应用并揭示其对公平性评估的实际影响，需要首个实现和验证。

Method: 将因果感知操作化为两类：结构性因果感知（智能体对因果图结构存在分歧）和参数性因果感知（智能体同意因果图但对其权重存在分歧）。设计计算干预分布（interventional）和反事实分布（counterfactual）的算法，并提出合适的距离度量来量化智能体之间的分歧程度。在德国信用数据集上进行多专家决策场景的实验。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）因果感知的判断对距离度量选择和阈值设定敏感；（2）因果感知会改变公平性评估结果和基于阈值的决策；（3）偏差相对于智能体的SCM具有情境性（situated），说明公平性问题中竞争性世界观不可忽视。

Conclusion: 因果感知框架的首次实现证实了其有效性，并揭示了公平性评估中对不同智能体因果模型的敏感性。该研究证明在公平性问题中，持有不同因果模型的智能体间的竞争性世界观不能被忽略，偏差是相对于所采用的SCM而言具有情境性的。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [213] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对Transformer推理过程的新解释——SIDPP（序列级交互动态并行处理），挑战"随机鹦鹉"观点，认为Transformer在推理时动态生成和应用依赖于提示（prompt）的变换参数，并推测人脑语言处理可能也是类似SIDPP形式的神经实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳大型语言模型仅仅是"随机鹦鹉"（复现训练中统计规律）的流行观点，提出Transformer在推理阶段具有更强计算能力的解释框架，并建立与人脑语言处理的联系。

Method: 提出SIDPP计算范式：将token向量视为"被变换的概念"，将矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换视为"变换的概念"；变换分为训练固定的静态变换和从输入序列动态生成的动态变换。从机制上，Transformer由多个简单神经网络组成，其架构新意在于输出-权重互联（output-weight interconnections），即某些网络的输出决定其他网络的权重，同时存在常规的输出-输入互联。通过这种互联结构，系统从提示中构建变换并用于修改token表示。

Result: 研究发现动态处理的贡献随提示长度增长而增加，可能等于甚至超过静态处理的贡献，这一现象被称为"强提示敏感性"（strong prompt sensitivity）。该框架对可解释性、可预测性、可控性以及设计更小更可持续的AI系统具有意义。

Conclusion: 由于人脑神经系统具备实现SIDPP所需的机制，论文推测人类语言处理可能本身也是SIDPP的一种形式，由与Transformer功能架构相关似的大脑皮层网络实现。

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [214] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACT是一个基于人类教学研究的分层框架，用于训练和评估具教学适应性的ESL对话辅导系统。它包含两套互补的分类体系（辅导策略分类学和学生行为分类学），构建了包含260段真实师生对话和32,379条标注的TACTCorpus数据集，并通过监督微调加分层对齐的GRPO优化得到TACTutor模型。在TACTBench基准上，TACTutor相比基础模型提升20.30%，超过所有商业基线，并在50名学习者的盲审研究中获得最高综合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于ESL学习者对话练习，但有效辅导不仅需要流畅生成，还需根据学习者行为和对话上下文选择适当的教学动作。人类辅导研究提供了适应性支持的原理，但这些原理常与具体任务绑定，尚未充分融入基于LLM的ESL辅导训练和评估中。

Method: 1) 基于已有文献开发两套互补分类体系：Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy（13种辅导响应策略）和Student-Move Taxonomy（按动作类型和状态刻画学习者行为）；2) 构建TACTCorpus，对260段真实师生对话进行32,379条标注并加入质量控制后的增强训练数据；3) 对Qwen3.5-4B进行监督微调及分层对齐的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）后训练，优化脚手架质量而非仅参考模仿；4) 构建包含78个真实辅导情境的策略平衡诊断基准TACTBench进行评测。

Result: TACTutor在TACTBench上相比基础模型提升20.30%，在相同协议下超过所有评估的商业基线，同时在既有外部教育基准上保持基础模型性能；在50名学习者的盲审研究中获得最高的总体平均评分。

Conclusion: 该研究公开了数据、基准和模型权重，为开发教学适应性ESL对话辅导系统提供了开放基础，表明基于教学分类体系的后训练与评估方法能显著提升LLM辅导的教学质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [215] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基础模型驱动的AI代理在重复社会困境中通过最优规划会收敛到稳定合作，这与经典博弈论预测的相互背叛相矛盾。作者提出"嵌入贝叶斯代理"理论模型和"嵌入均衡"解概念，为现代AI代理的社会行为提供新的博弈论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型驱动的自主代理越来越多地融入社会和经济系统，理解它们集体行为的安全与合作原则至关重要。经典博弈论建立在"解耦代理"假设上，即代理将自己的决策视为独立于环境和他人，但现代AI代理会联合预测自身未来行为与外部观察。这种范式不匹配需要新的理论框架来理解AI代理的社会行为。

Method: 作者通过实证实验展示基础模型代理在典型社会困境中的最优规划会收敛到稳定合作；在此基础上提出"嵌入贝叶斯代理"理论模型，将代理视为栖息于其所处的宇宙之中，并对其自身决策算法保持认知不确定性。代理通过推断他人行为是否与自己相似，将规划过程中的自我推演视为证据——合作决策预测相似伙伴的类似决策。这一"相似性推断"机制被形式化为"嵌入均衡"这一全新解概念。

Result: 基础模型代理在经典博弈论预测相互背叛的情况下，通过最优规划稳定地收敛到合作行为，直接用实证反驳了经典博弈论的预测。作者进一步以"嵌入均衡"替代纳什均衡，为现代AI代理的社会行为建立了形式化的理论基础。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，从"解耦能动性"转向"嵌入能动性"是理解现代AI代理社会行为的根本性范式转变。该研究为以基础模型为驱动的自主系统在安全和合作方面提供了新的理论基石，对人类社会与AI系统交互的治理具有重要启示。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [216] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊控制器的自适应测试时扩展方法，利用可解释信号（提示复杂度、模型置信度）动态分配每个查询的采样预算，在减少平均采样数的情况下接近全预算基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展（test-time scaling）方法使用固定的每查询预算，对简单和困难提示花费相同的计算量，且不可解释，无法说明为什么某个提示获得特定数量的样本。这造成了计算资源浪费和推理过程的不可解释性。

Method: 设计一个轻量级模糊控制器（fuzzy controller），将可解释信号（估计的提示复杂度、模型置信度）映射到每查询的采样预算。简单或高置信度的提示分配较少样本，困难或低置信度的提示分配更多样本。在公平对齐协议下（匹配解码设置、控制答案选择）与best-of-N、计算感知扩展、基于自置信度的基线进行比较。

Result: 在问答和数学推理任务上，跨多个模型和数据集，自适应模糊控制优于多个标准基线，并在减少平均采样数的同时保持了与选择器匹配的全预算控制的接近性能。

Conclusion: 可解释的自适应采样是大语言模型实现更高效测试时推理的实用方向，能够在降低计算成本的同时维持较高的推理性能。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [217] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE（Human Input-Variation Engine），一套覆盖语音转录扰动和QWERTY键盘扰动的评估套件，系统研究了人类输入扰动对LLM性能的影响，发现语音扰动比键盘扰动代价更高，且损伤根源在于问题token被破坏而非新增token，并考察了测试集污染、轻量适配和思维预算等缓解因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过打字或语音输入与语言模型交互，两种渠道分别携带不同的噪声特征（键盘的拼写噪声、语音的转录不流畅与重构），这些扰动的性质不同，对LLM性能的影响也未知。此前缺乏系统评估工具来量化这些真实人类输入扰动对模型鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 构建HIVE套件，包含语音转录扰动（disfluency、reconstruction等）与QWERTY键盘扰动（拼写错误等），在所有指令微调模型上进行系统性鲁棒性评估，并进行消融分析（区分token被破坏与新增）、渠道对比（构造/推断任务与选择题）、污染控制实验、轻量适配实验以及思维预算（thinking budget）测试。

Result: 提出七个发现：(i)语音转录扰动在所有指令微调模型上均降低准确率，且损伤来自转录的结构而非填充词；(ii)键盘扰动代价较小，模型可吸收大量扰动；(iii)两类损伤统一归因于问题token的破坏，新增token代价小；(iv)渠道差距仅在需要构造或推断答案的任务中出现，选择题无差异；(v)损伤并非仅由测试集污染造成；(vi)轻量适配无法消除损伤；(vii)思维预算几乎完全恢复键盘渠道性能，但对口语寄存器无效，压缩语音甚至恶化。

Conclusion: 人类输入渠道的扰动对LLM构成不对称的鲁棒性挑战：语音渠道的结构性转录扰动对模型损伤远大于键盘拼写噪声，且难以通过轻量适配或扩展推理恢复。研究为理解LLM对真实人类输入变异的鲁棒性提供了系统基准和归因分析。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [218] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectRL框架，利用强化学习训练中被丢弃的"黄金负轨迹"（专家模型失败的推理轨迹）作为反思素材，通过反思推理和策略迁移提升大语言模型的推理能力，在9个基准、4个骨干模型和4种on-policy训练方法上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于专家轨迹引导的on-policy训练方法在处理高难度问题时，由于专家也往往失败，这些失败轨迹通常被当作负样本丢弃，导致模型在高难度问题上失去主要的监督信号。作者认为这些失败轨迹中蕴含着有价值的推理信号，不应被简单地抛弃。

Method: ReflectRL包含两个核心步骤：(1) Reflective Reasoning——利用黄金负轨迹作为反思对象，让模型对失败轨迹进行反思而非模仿；(2) Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition——将反思阶段习得的推理行为迁移回直接推理模式，确保模型在推理时无需依赖外部轨迹。该框架是轻量级即插即用的，可嵌入多种on-policy训练方法中。

Result: 在9个基准测试、4种LLM骨干模型和4种on-policy训练方法上进行了实验，ReflectRL一致地提升了推理性能，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹并非无用，将其作为反思对象而非模仿对象，可以挖掘其中蕴含的推理信号，有效提升模型在困难问题上的推理能力，且该方法具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDRSR方法，通过严格的数学推导拓展了符号回归中表达式分解与化简的适用范围，避免了AI Feynman方法中依赖暴力搜索子表达式的局限，在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习符号回归方法缺乏对内在数学和物理原理的深刻理解。AI Feynman方法虽然利用了数据背后的数学性质，但其表达式化简机制适用范围窄、容易在复杂方程上失效，且依赖暴力搜索子表达式，严重限制了实际用途。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导与证明，提出Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR)方法。该方法从根本上拓宽了表达式分解与化简的适用性，无需暴力搜索子结构，兼顾了更广泛的普适性与严格的理论正确性。

Result: 实验评估表明，所提出的理论原则在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均带来了显著的优势。

Conclusion: 论文最后讨论了该范式的适用场景与固有局限性，并指出了未来有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [220] [Multimodal Auto-regressive Transformer Surrogate for Modeling Variable Operations and Quantifying Uncertainty in Geological Carbon Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02629)
*Yifu Han,Louis J. Durlofsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于在地质不确定性条件下模拟可变射孔和注入策略的CO2地质封存作业，基于修改的SEAM CO2地质模型（含断层系统和三个堆叠含水层），通过多模态编码器融合3D地质模型、相对渗透率标量参数和控制变量，经Transformer编码器自注意力融合并在时间解码器中自回归预测。代理模型使用4000次GEOS流动模拟训练，在测试集上中位饱和度MAE为0.028，其他量的中位相对误差在0.2-5%之间，能捕捉速率-井底压力控制切换，并成功集成到分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛数据同化流程中实现关键元参数（特别是断层渗透率）的不确定性显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高地质碳封存（GCS）作业效率需要优化可变井射孔和注入策略，但传统数值模拟（如GEOS全物理模拟）计算成本高昂，难以在不确定性条件下进行快速预测和数据同化。因此需要一个高效且准确的代理模型来替代昂贵的流动模拟，以便在考虑地质不确定性的情况下快速评估不同操作策略的效果。

Method: 提出一种多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，核心设计包括：(1) 三个独立编码器分别处理3D地质模型、相对渗透率函数标量参数和控制变量三种输入模态；(2) 通过Transformer编码器中的自注意力机制融合多模态特征；(3) 时间解码器通过编码器-解码器交叉注意力自回归生成预测（包括监测点饱和度与压力、总注入和可移动CO2质量、饱和度足迹）。训练采用4000个GEOS流动模拟数据，并集成到分层MCMC数据同化框架中。

Result: 在随机采样的地质模型和控制变量的测试集上，代理模型实现中位饱和度MAE为0.028，其他关注量的中位相对误差为0.2-5%。模型能够捕捉从速率控制到井底压力控制的切换行为。在分层MCMC数据同化中，针对三种操作策略，关键元参数（尤其断层渗透率）的不确定性显著降低，饱和足迹和总量预测与真实模型结果总体一致。

Conclusion: 多模态自回归Transformer代理模型有效解决了GCS作业中高效预测与数据同化的挑战，既能在复杂断层-多层含水层系统中精确预测多类物理量，又能整合到MCMC框架中实现地质参数的不确定性量化，为规模化地质碳封存作业优化提供了可行的高效工具。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [221] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的电路追踪流水线，通过让语言模型直接对特征描述进行分组来生成超节点（supernodes），取代了传统电路追踪中耗时的人工分组步骤。实验表明该流水线生成的超节点与人工标注同样可解释，在二跳首府任务中97/100的提示词正确恢复了中间跳对应的超节点，并展示了在开放探索场景下的初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪是揭示语言模型内部计算的重要技术，但现有的电路追踪流程需要人工将单个特征或MLP神经元手动分组为超节点，这一步骤耗时且难以规模化，限制了电路追踪技术的广泛应用。

Method: 提出一个简单的自动化流水线：将特征描述直接呈现给语言模型，由模型自动将它们分组为超节点。使用自动化可解释性指标验证生成的超节点质量，并在二跳首府任务中评估流水线恢复中间跳超节点的能力，同时在1000个维基百科提示补全的归因图上进行开放探索验证。

Result: 自动化流水线生成的超节点与人工标注的同样可解释；在二跳首府任务中，97/100的提示词成功恢复了对应中间跳的超节点；自动化标注了1000个归因图，并用LLM裁判标记出值得人工审查的图。

Conclusion: 简单的自动化方法也能产生有意义的归因图注释，展示了自动化电路追踪的可行性，为推动该方向的进一步研究提供了动机和基础。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [222] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoID-PINN，一种用于SIRD动力学的物理信息神经网络，通过行随机源组成矩阵表示空间依赖关系，并利用空间先验进行正则化。在四区域模拟和路易斯安那州64县COVID-19数据中验证，结果显示准确轨迹并不能保证恢复区域依赖结构，预测精度优于传统基线，但分布拟合略逊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据中本地传播、报告偏差、种子感染和外部感染压力难以区分，且由于聚合信息的可辨识性不足，准确拟合传染动力学模型并不保证能恢复真实的区域间依赖结构。现有方法缺乏对先验设定敏感性及依赖结构可辨识性的系统检验。

Method: GeoID-PINN将SIRD方程嵌入物理信息神经网络，用行随机源组成矩阵（每行非负且和为1）建模空间依赖，并以距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构造的空间先验对该矩阵进行正则化。通过模拟已知真实值的四区域实验和64县COVID-19回顾性分析评估模型，并与自回归负二项基线对比。

Result: 四区域模拟中：兼容距离先验下源组成误差0.099，无正则化误差0.159，强错误先验下达0.577；轨迹拟合和传播尺度估计在各设定下相似。COVID-19分析中：Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN的MSE从基线32,957降至11,468，MAE从70.60降至57.73；基线NLL更低（5.158 vs 5.346）。15县对比中邻接先验使MSE降低6.85%、MAE降低3.1%。

Conclusion: 准确的轨迹拟合不保证恢复区域依赖结构的正确性；结构化正则化优于无正则化，强烈错误先验会严重损害依赖结构估计但轨迹拟合仍看似良好；不同合理先验下预测性能相近，支持结构化正则化的使用但不支持唯一边恢复的推断，需进行先验敏感性和观测模型检验后再做解释。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [223] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证器引导（VG）的神经-符号工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，通过动力学和物理可接受性标准从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选方程，实现从合成ODE轨迹预训练的Transformer向高维物理数据的可靠迁移。在Van der Pol振荡器和涡脱落（Vortex Shedding）现象上验证了该方法，它能发现可解释的降阶方程且泛化到未见参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理系统仿真代价高昂，机器学习替代模型往往不透明且隐含系统动力学假设，限制泛化能力。虽然预训练Transformer可将合成ODE轨迹映射为符号方程，但将其可靠迁移到高维物理数据仍是未解决的挑战。

Method: 围绕ODEFormer作为符号骨干构建验证器引导（VG）工作流：利用动力学和物理可接受性标准（如物理合法性、动力学一致性）从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选最佳符号方程。通过坐标约简处理高维涡脱落数据，在固定和变化Reynolds数下进行符号发现，不依赖尾流特定候选库或预设Navier-Stokes结构。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器的留出初始条件下，VG方法优于原始ODEFormer工作流。在涡脱落任务中，VG发现固定参数降阶方程可恢复基本脱落振荡器及其高次谐波；跨参数模型可泛化到保留的Reynolds数区间。此外发现重建保真度本身不能决定符号可发现性，潜在动力学与骨干预训练分布的兼容性更为关键。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了验证器引导的神经-符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释、可物理审计的预测提供了新途径，证明了通过合适的验证标准和坐标约简，预训练符号Transformer可以可靠迁移到高维物理系统。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [224] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-HEG（连续时间异质EHR图）模式，将ICU住院编码为带类型和时间戳的图（含visit、vital、lab_event三种节点类型与2D边属性），并实例化为CHIRP-Net（四层异质GATv2Conv网络）。在MIMIC-IV v3.1（31,142次ICU住院）上，CHIRP-Net达到AUROC 0.8449±0.0071，集成模型达AUROC 0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745）。消融实验显示双向连接是模型有效工作的必要条件（移除反向边使AUROC下降0.1968），时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247 AUROC，而压缩异质边类型为单一关系（参数减少7倍）反而在所有seeds上表现更优。校准后ECE为0.0307。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重症监护室（ICU）死亡预测需要建模跨异构实体类型的不规则临床观测。现有序列模型能处理不规则采样但忽略类型化的关系结构，而现有图模型假设固定间隔输入。因此需要一种能够同时捕获不规则时间事件与异质实体关系结构的建模框架。

Method: 1) 提出CT-HEG模式：每个ICU住院编码为带类型和时间戳的图，含三种节点类型（visit、vital、lab_event）和2D边属性（t_hours/48编码时间、value_norm编码数值），无需插补。2) 将其实例化为CHIRP-Net，即四层异质GATv2Conv网络。3) 在MIMIC-IV v3.1上进行评估（31,142次ICU住院，LOS≥48h，死亡率13.4%），使用五种子和bootstrap置信区间，对比基线（逻辑回归、mTAND、Transformer、GRU-D），并开展消融研究。

Result: CHIRP-Net五种子平均AUROC为0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209）；集成模型AUROC为0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745）。消融实验表明：移除反向边切断观测节点到visit读出节点的连接，AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073；时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093 AUROC；将异质边类型合并为单一关系（参数减少7倍）在所有seeds上反而优于完整模型。校准后ECE为0.0307。

Conclusion: 双向连接性对模型利用输入至关重要，CT-HEG在校准（验证集拟合温度缩放）后表现良好。结果支持CT-HEG用于不规则EHR数据，但作者明确指出，在声称鲁棒性之前仍需进行外部验证、预指定的时间评估和人口统计公平性审计。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [225] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的框架，将欧几里得残差连接与Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm)理论统一在黎曼流形的视角下。作者发现，在超球面上所有retraction映射族可以归结为单一标量设计选择，并据此提出了p-SpheretNorm参数化一族角retraction。其中Proj-SpheretNorm (p=1)和Cay-SpheretNorm (p=2)是精确保范且仅需代数运算的方法，而GeoNorm和恒等映射分别对应极端情况(p→∞和p→0)。实验表明在nanoGPT上，有限的p值取得最佳验证损失，说明指数映射并非球形残差流的最佳选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是深度神经网络稳定训练的关键机制，但现有方法（如GeoNorm）依赖黎曼指数映射，计算开销较大且不一定是最优选择。作者希望探索更广泛的retraction映射族，将欧几里得残差连接与GeoNorm统一到同一理论框架中，并寻找计算更简便、性能更优的替代方案。

Method: 作者在超球面上证明所有retraction映射可以归结为单个标量设计选择——即如何将更新的幅度转换为隐藏状态与更新所确定的平面内的旋转角度。基于此观点，提出参数化一族角retraction：p-SpheretNorm。该族的旋转角随更新幅度饱和增长而非无界增长。通过选择metric projection retraction和Cayley retraction，分别得到Proj-SpheretNorm (p=1)和Cay-SpheretNorm (p=2)，两者精确保范且只需代数运算，无需指数映射。恒等映射和GeoNorm分别作为p→0和p→∞的极限情形出现。

Result: 在nanoGPT基准上，p-SpheretNorm族的三种方法（有限p值）均优于现有的轻量级深层连接方案。最优验证损失出现在有限的p值处，而非p→∞（即GeoNorm）或p→0（即恒等映射），表明指数映射并非球形残差流的最佳retraction选择。

Conclusion: 球形残差流中的retraction选择本质上只是旋转角度饱和策略的设计问题。GeoNorm所用的指数映射仅是这一谱系的一端，而非最优选择。p-SpheretNorm提供了一个计算更高效且性能更优的替代方案，同时也为残差连接的设计提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [226] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhenMol框架，一种结构保持的、表型感知的分子表示学习方法。通过将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享和私有的组成部分，在保留化学结构信息的同时实现表型引导的跨模态对齐，在约3.04×10^4对分子-细胞形态数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态表示学习方法在优化跨模态对齐时，没有考虑化学空间的固有组织结构，导致分子表示发生扭曲和结构信息丢失。这对于表型药物发现而言是一个关键问题，因为化学结构的完整性直接关系到药物发现的可靠性。

Method: PhenMol框架：(1) 将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享组件和私有组件；(2) 通过专用的分子分支实现表型引导的对齐；(3) 在整合细胞表型信息的同时保持分子邻域组织，避免结构信息丢失。

Result: 在约3.04×10^4个分子-细胞形态对上的实验显示：PhenMol在270个生物活性任务上提升了分子性质预测，同时在分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测方面表现优异。ECFP4结构分析表明该方法更好地保留了分子邻域并减少了嵌入扭曲。

Conclusion: 结果表明结构感知约束在多模态分子表示学习中至关重要，PhenMol为将细胞表型与化学知识整合用于药物发现提供了一种有效方法。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [227] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLOBE框架，利用跨多个训练检查点的梯度轨迹表示样本，通过多阶分布匹配、Group LASSO与Elastic Net正则化及非负预算约束，将样本选择建模为全局稀疏加权优化问题，在六个基准和五种架构上超越现有coreset选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的coreset选择方法依赖单一模型快照上的梯度，采用贪心或逐次逼近的选择策略，无法捕捉深度学习优化过程中的动态变化，也难以处理强相关的样本，导致在低保留率下性能受限。

Method: GLOBE框架：(1) 用跨多个训练检查点的梯度轨迹表示每个样本，捕捉其在优化不同阶段的影响；(2) 引入多阶匹配目标，联合对齐一阶均值与投影非中心二阶矩；(3) 结合Group LASSO和Elastic Net正则化以及非负预算约束，诱导组级与样本级稀疏性并稳定相关轨迹的权重；(4) 采用类平衡Top-K选择确保类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准数据集和五种评估架构上，GLOBE在下游测试准确率上始终优于现有coreset选择方法，尤其是在低保留率场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配与结构化稀疏性的结合，为数据高效学习提供了有效的coreset选择方案，验证了轨迹对齐策略的可行性与优势。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [228] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OptR是一种面向输出的旋转方法，通过最小化经W_O投影后的注意力输出误差来优化KV缓存的INT2量化，相比现有方法在推理和编码基准上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的INT2量化方法（如QuaRot、OSCAR）在完全注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，但模型最终受经注意力传播和W_O输出投影后的误差影响，存在优化目标与实际影响不匹配的问题。

Method: OptR将W_O之后注意力输出误差分解为键诱发项和值诱发项，并通过完整的INT2量化与注意力路径学习逐头正交校正；同时采用注意力等价的键重参数化来减少大规模通道偏移而不改变softmax分布。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理与编码基准上，OptR一致地改进了QuaRot和OSCAR的性能，增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保留了分页KV缓存格式且推理开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: OptR通过输出感知的优化策略有效解决了INT2量化中优化目标与实际推理误差的错位问题，是超低比特KV缓存量化的有效改进方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [229] [Navigating LLM Valley: From AdamW to Memory-Efficient and Matrix-Based Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09176)
*Aditya Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型（LLM）的优化器设计进行了系统性综述，从系统和优化双重视角审视了从经典一阶方法到Adam、内存高效变体、二阶方法、符号型优化器、低秩投影方法及矩阵型优化器（如Muon）等各类算法，并提出优化器研究正从单一算法加速宣称转向严格的、尺度感知的联合比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam仍是LLM预训练和微调的主流优化器，但近期研究几乎重新审视了优化栈的每个组件。随着模型规模不断增大，仅追求统计有效性已不够，还需要在极端规模下具备计算和内存效率。该综述旨在系统梳理这一快速发展的领域，填补缺乏从系统和优化综合视角进行整合梳理的空白。

Method: 本文采用结构化分类方法，将优化器文献组织为八类：经典一阶优化器、自适应优化器、内存高效变体、二阶及曲率感知方法、符号型与自动发现优化器、低秩与投影方法、矩阵型优化器（如Muon）。同时建立了包含超参数公平性、规模依赖、墙钟效率、token效率、内存开销和下游评估在内的基准测试方法论框架。

Result: 文章识别出LLM优化器研究的范式转变：从单一算法的收敛速度宣称，转向在收敛性、稳定性、内存和实现复杂度等多个维度上进行的严格的、规模感知的比较评估。论文系统性地梳理了各类优化方法的技术路线及其在LLM训练场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: LLM优化器研究正进入新阶段，需要建立统一的、综合考虑收敛性能、稳定性、内存开销和实现复杂度的评测体系，以支持在极端规模下进行公平且有意义的优化器对比。

Abstract: Training large language models requires optimization algorithms that are not only statistically effective, but also computationally and memory efficient at extreme scale. Although Adam remains the dominant optimizer for large-scale language-model pretraining and fine-tuning, recent work has revisited nearly every component of the optimization stack: adaptive moment estimation, decoupled weight decay, memory footprint, curvature approximation, sign-based updates, large-batch stability, low-rank gradient structure, and matrix-wise orthogonalized updates. This survey reviews optimizer design for large language models through a systems-and-optimization lens. We organize the literature into classical first-order optimizers, adaptive optimizers, memory-efficient variants, second-order and curvature-aware methods, sign-based and discovered optimizers, low-rank and projection-based methods, and matrix-based optimizers such as Muon. We also discuss benchmarking methodology, including hyperparameter fairness, scale dependence, wall-clock efficiency, token efficiency, memory overhead, and downstream evaluation. We argue that optimizer research for LLMs is entering a new phase: moving from single-algorithm speedup claims toward rigorous, scale-aware comparisons that jointly evaluate convergence, stability, memory, and implementation complexity.

</details>


### [230] [PatTree: a novel approach for automated creation of multimodal, graph-based patient representations for medical classification tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02692)
*Julia Gehrmann,Lars Quakulinski,Hamza Naseem,Oya Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PatTree，一种基于图的、可自动从真实世界临床数据中构建的多模态患者整体表示方法。它无需预先标准化输入即可实现早期数据集成，并在ADNI-1子集（n=763）的三分类任务（阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍、认知正常）中达到98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数，展示了免假设、自动化多模态医学数据结构化可作为临床AI管道的可扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据能提升AI在医学分类任务中的表现，但真实临床数据存在异构性（缺失值、多时间点、多模态、格式与语义不一致），传统的数据协调（harmonization）过程资源密集且易出错，限制了大规模、可复现的AI决策支持系统的发展。

Method: 提出PatTree图结构，从真实世界临床数据中通过自动化结构化多模态临床数据来构建患者的整体表示。该方法在统一知识图谱中表示异构临床数据的同时保留跨模态和数据源的数据元素之间的语义关系，实现互操作性和机器可解释的数据访问，无需依赖预标准化的输入。

Result: 在ADNI-1队列子集（n=763）上，直接在PatTree上进行患者三分类（阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍、认知正常）获得98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数，达到最先进的分类性能。

Conclusion: 免假设的自动化多模态医学数据结构化可以作为临床AI管道的可扩展基础，绕过繁琐的数据准备和标准化步骤，为大规模、可复现的医学AI决策支持提供新路径。

Abstract: Access to holistic, multimodal data improves the performance of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in medical classification tasks compared to utilizing single modalities or data sources. However, the inherent heterogeneity and complexity of clinical real-world data pose significant challenges to structured data analysis and AI application. This heterogeneity includes missing values, multiple time points, diverse modalities, and inconsistent formats and semantics. Data harmonization prior to data integration tackles this challenge but remains resource-intensive and error-prone, limiting the scalability and reproducibility of holistic, AI-driven decision support on clinical real-world data. We therefore propose PatTree, a graph-based, holistic representation of patients that can be derived from real-world clinical data through the automated structuring of multimodal clinical data. PatTree enables early-stage data integration without relying on pre-standardized inputs. While representing heterogeneous clinical data within a unified knowledge graph, PatTree preserves the semantic relationships between data elements across modalities and data sources, facilitating interoperability and machine-interpretable data access. Using a subset of the ADNI-1 cohort (n = 763), we demonstrate that classification of patients is directly feasible on PatTree reaching state-of-the-art classification performance. In the three-class classification task distinguishing Alzheimer's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and cognitively normal individuals, we achieve a balanced accuracy of 98.5% and an F$_1$ score of 0.987 on the held-out test set. Our results show that assumption-free, automated structuring of multimodal medical data can serve as a scalable foundation for clinical AI pipelines bypassing tedious data preparation and standardization.

</details>


### [231] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的轨迹推断算法Acceleration Matching (AM)，通过将插值问题提升到相空间并回归到显式的条件加速度场来生成平滑轨迹，训练过程仅需位置数据、无需轨迹模拟或昂贵的预处理，在多个基准问题上表现优于或媲美现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域的基本问题，但现有算法存在计算挑战：要么依赖预处理子例程来保证平滑性，要么依赖基于模拟的训练目标，两者都可能代价高昂。

Method: 提出Acceleration Matching (AM)算法：将原始插值问题提升到相空间（phase space），然后回归到显式的条件加速度场，该加速度场能诱导随机、平滑且与给定边际分布一致的轨迹。训练仅需位置数据，避免轨迹模拟，且无需昂贵的预处理。

Result: 提供了充分的数值证据，表明AM算法在现有文献中的多个基准问题上与现有算法具有竞争力或优于现有算法。

Conclusion: AM算法通过规避预处理和模拟训练的开销，提供了一种更高效且有效的轨迹推断方案，在多个基准问题上展示出优越或相当的性能。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [232] [Measuring Explainer Stability via Attribution Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02697)
*Eddie Conti,Álvaro Parafita,Axel Brando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于分布的框架来量化和评估归因方法（AMs）的稳定性，通过分析归因分数排名的可分离性和可靠性指数来捕捉其随机性，并扩展用于跨数据集比较不同解释器的排名稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法由于定义中的随机成分可能产生可变的归因分数，但缺乏统一的框架来衡量这种不稳定性，因此需要一种方法来系统评估归因排名的可靠性。

Method: 构建基于分布的框架，量化排名归因向量的可分离程度，并计算特征排名保持可靠的最大索引；进一步扩展该框架以比较不同归因方法在数据集上的排名稳健性。

Result: 通过实验展示了该方法如何用于评估解释器的稳定性，并提供了评估归因方法稳定性的互补准则。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布框架为评价归因方法的稳定性提供了一种补充性标准，有助于理解归因排名的可靠范围。

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

</details>


### [233] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NANQ是一个面向模拟存算一体(CIM)的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，通过对eFlash CIM阵列的实测噪声建模，将噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度和逐层位宽分配，在不增加量化资源的前提下显著提升低比特量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIM导向的量化方法主要最小化理想量化误差，忽略了硬件噪声底(noise floor)，导致精度分配不高效。实际模拟CIM中器件变异和读取噪声会严重降低低比特量化模型的性能，需要一种感知硬件噪声的量化方案。

Method: NANQ由两部分组成：(1) 从eFlash CIM阵列的实测响应中建模与幅值相关的权重噪声，将噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度——在低噪声区域分配更精细的量化分辨率，在噪声主导区域避免无效精度；(2) 通过统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，据此分配逐层位宽，实现混合精度配置。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上进行片上实验，在2-bit权重幅值量化条件下，NANQ在视觉模型上比PowerQuant提升8.05个百分点的准确率，在语言模型上平均降低54.7%的困惑度(PPL)。混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可捕获额外量化资源带来的绝大部分增益。

Conclusion: NANQ通过将硬件噪声信息融入量化的非均匀分布设计和位宽分配，有效避免了在噪声主导区域浪费精度资源，实现了对模拟CIM硬件噪声底的高效利用，以极低的等效比特数达到接近更大量化资源的效果。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [234] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否利用随机训练模型时产生的训练日志作为协变量，来提高模型性能比较的精度（即降低不确定性）。研究发现，基于早期训练日志的简单臂内协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment）通常能减少模型比较的不确定性，但协变量选择是一个关键限制——广泛搜索与结果最相关的统计量会引入过大的选择噪声，反而得不偿失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较随机训练的模型时，需要估计性能差异及其不确定性。传统方法仅从重复运行的结果中估计，但忽略了训练过程中产生的丰富日志信息。作者希望探究这些训练日志中的协变量能否用于提高模型比较的统计精度。

Method: 采用臂内协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment）策略：每个模型仅使用自身运行的日志统计量进行调整，原始均值差异仍作为报告的效应量。在包含三种架构、三个数据集的视觉任务研究中，比较了基于早期训练日志的简单调整方法，并考察了从日志池中广泛搜索最相关统计量的效果。

Result: 基于早期训练日志的简单调整通常能降低模型比较的不确定性；但广泛搜索日志池中最相关统计量的做法往往引入比消除更多的噪声，即使存在事后看来有用的统计量。协变量选择是该方法的主要局限。

Conclusion: 训练日志确实有助于更精确的模型比较，但前提是调整过程必须避免过大的选择噪声。协变量选择策略是决定该方法有效性的关键因素。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [235] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可寻址且保持基数（cardinality-preserving）的虚拟内存机制，用于消息传递神经网络（MPNN），以缓解节点到虚拟节点再到节点（node--VN--node）流水线中全局状态容量瓶颈的问题，无需自注意力，代价为O(nMd)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的虚拟节点（VN）机制将图压缩成单一同质状态并广播给所有节点，存在有限容量瓶颈，无法保留多重性（multiplicity）信息，限制了MPNN的表达能力。

Method: 基于Two-Radius分析框架，提出两个要求：(1) 将全局记忆分解为可独立写入和读取的寻址交叉注意力槽（addressable cross-attention slots）；(2) 由于softmax注意力对均匀复制具有不变性会导致多重性丢失，每个槽查询需作为私有的键/值锚点（key/value anchor）插入，恢复被丢弃的归一化质量，从而在有界颜色域上得到单射多重集表示，可实现1-WL细化。

Result: 在多重性感知的Two-Radius、子图计数（motif counting）和约束链路集预测实验中，该可寻址且保持基数的虚拟内存机制取得了支持性结果，计算代价为O(nMd)。

Conclusion: 通过将虚拟内存因子化为可寻址槽并恢复归一化质量，可以构建无需自注意力的、保持多重性的全局通信机制，在表达能力与计算效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [236] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部收敛输入神经网络（NNLCI）的新应用，用于提升多资产期权定价数值方法的效率。NNLCI通过神经网络对粗网格和细网格数值解进行局部校正，仅需少量高精度训练数据即可将RMSE降低约4-12倍，并在多个维度（1D/2D/3D Black-Scholes和Heston模型）上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多资产期权定价在高维空间下计算成本高昂，传统数值方法（如有限差分、蒙特卡洛）在实时交易和风险管理场景中难以满足效率需求。作者旨在通过神经网络对现有数值解进行局部修正，以最小化训练数据需求并显著提升求解精度与效率。

Method: NNLCI利用神经网络对粗网格与细网格数值解之间的偏差进行局部学习与校正。具体而言，该方法只需在少量参数组合上生成高保真训练数据，神经网络通过学习局部收敛模式，将粗网格解校正至接近细网格解的精度。文中引入了最简洁的输入格式以提升便利性。实验覆盖Black-Scholes方程下1/2/3维现金或无现金期权，以及Heston随机波动率模型下的单资产下降-触出障碍看涨期权。

Result: 在各类测试案例中，NNLCI将细网格数值解的均方根误差（RMSE）降低了约4-12倍，且仅需在少量参数组合子集上进行训练即可实现。结果表明NNLCI在高维期权定价问题中显著降低了计算需求，同时具备低训练成本和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: NNLCI为高维期权定价提供了一种高效且低成本的增强方案，特别适用于实时期权交易和风险管理场景，具有很强的实际应用前景。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>
